Adding Sliced Isotropic Gaussian Regularization (SIGReg) substantially improves performance and narrows this gap. The effect of regularization is task-dependent: moderate SIGReg helps fine-grained ranking, while stronger regularization can weaken local alignment. We further show that different encoding branches support different retrieval regimes. These results position within-document predictive learning as a promising citation-free complement for scientific document representation, provided that embedding geometry is carefully controlled.

</details>


### [72] [Do large language models scrutinise what they review? A multimodal audit of scoring calibration, error detection, and author-identity effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28626)
*Emad Alharbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-Large-124B在ICLR 2026投稿中的同行评审能力。结果显示LLM评分普遍偏高（7.0-8.1 vs 人类3.4-6.8），错误检出率低（自然提示下仅12.1%，验证提示下22.2%），且添加图片反而降低错误检出率并提高评分。作者身份不影响结果，LLM的录用决定与简单分数平均一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地用于生成同行评审意见，亟需考察其进行批判性评估的实际能力，特别是在处理多模态内容、识别错误以及避免偏见方面的表现。

Method: 选取ICLR 2026的165篇投稿，使用两个多模态LLM（Qwen2.5-VL-72B和Pixtral-Large-124B）作为评审员。实验设计包括：对作者身份进行盲审、替换为高/低声望机构；提供纯文本或文本加图片格式；在55篇稿件中插入145个可检测的错误，分别使用自然提示和验证导向提示来评估错误识别能力。

Result: 1. LLM给出的评分显著高于人类（LLM: 7.0-8.1，人类: 3.4-6.8）。2. 错误检出率较低，自然提示下为12.1%，加入一句验证指令后提升至22.2%，仍有78%的错误未被发现。3. 提供图片降低了错误检出率但提高了评审评分。4. 没有任何视觉错误能可靠地与对应图片交叉验证，半数纯文本评审描述了不存在的图片。5. 作者身份信息（无论声望高低）未影响评分或错误检出。6. LLM的最终编辑决策与简单的分数平均结果完全一致。

Conclusion: 当前多模态LLM在同行评审中表现出评分通胀、错误识别能力有限以及对图像信息处理不可靠等问题。尽管它们能生成结构化的评审意见，但在批判性评估和事实核查方面仍远未达到人类专家水平，且其决策机制较为简单化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate peer reviews, prompting examination of their capacity for critical evaluation. This study evaluates two multimodal LLMs, Qwen2.5-VL-72B and Pixtral-Large-124B, as reviewers across 165 submissions to the 2026 International Conference on Learning Representations, a venue that postdates both models' training cutoffs. Manuscripts were presented to both models with author identities blinded, replaced with high-prestige affiliations, or replaced with low-prestige affiliations, and in either text-only or text-with-figure format. Additionally, 145 verifiably detectable errors were inserted into 55 manuscripts to assess error identification under natural and verification-oriented prompts. Across all manuscript groups, including rejected submissions, LLM scores ranged from 7.0 to 8.1, whereas human mean scores ranged from 3.4 to 6.8. The models detected 12.1\% of the verified errors under natural prompting, and a one-sentence verification instruction increased detection to 22.2\%; however, 78\% of the errors remained undetected. Providing figures reduced error detection while increasing review scores. No visual error was reliably verified against its corresponding figure, and half of the text-only reviews described figures that were not provided. Author identity did not influence either review scores or error detection. LLM editorial decisions exactly matched those produced by simple score averaging.

</details>


### [73] [Intelligent Identification and Repair of Design Defects in BIM via Domain-Specific Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28629)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Yun-Hong Cai,Xiang-Rui Ni,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于领域特定大语言模型（LLM）的BIM缺陷识别与修复集成框架，通过BIM转文本、提示学习及幻觉控制策略，实现从原始数据输入到修复建议生成的端到端流程，显著提升了缺陷识别准确率并消除了大量幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏一种通用且高效的方法来识别和解决BIM中设计缺陷的多样性问题。

Method: 1. 引入带有组件平衡分块的BIM-to-Text方法，以桥接BIM数据与LLM；2. 采用结合规则注入、少样本提示（Few-shot prompting）和检索增强生成（RAG）的提示学习方法来识别缺陷并生成修复建议；3. 引入结合关键标识符验证和令牌长度阈值的幻觉控制策略以确保可靠性。

Result: 能力扩展使识别准确率达到85%（传统规则检查为70%），合理修复建议率达94%；幻觉控制策略将准确率从64%提升至85%，并在单次干预轮次中消除了92.5%的幻觉。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个从原始BIM数据输入、缺陷识别到修复建议生成的端到端原型系统，证明了基于领域特定LLM的方法在解决BIM设计缺陷多样性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Existing methods lack a generalized approach to efficiently identify and resolve the diversity of design defects in BIM. Therefore, this study proposes an integrated framework to identify and repair various defects in BIM via domain-specific LLMs. Firstly, a BIM-to-Text method with component-balanced chunking is introduced to bridge BIM data with LLMs. Then, prompt learning with rule injection, few-shot prompting and RAG is proposed to identify defects and generate repair suggestions. Meanwhile, a hallucination control strategy combining key identifier validation and token-length thresholds is introduced to ensure reliability. Experiments show capability expansion yields 85% identification accuracy versus 70% for traditional rule checking, achieving a 94% rate of reasonable repair suggestions. Moreover, the proposed hallucination control further increased accuracy from 64% to 85%, eliminating 92.5% of hallucinations in a single intervention round. This study establishes an end-to-end prototype from raw BIM data input, through defect identification, to repair suggestion generation.

</details>


### [74] [Enabling Proactive Spoken Turns via a Generalized Style-Aware Full-Duplex Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28630)
*Tianrui Pan,Qinglin Zhang,Chong Deng,Luyao Cheng,Qian Chen,Wen Wang,Jie Tang,Gangshan Wu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通用的、风格感知的全双工对话框架，包含轻量级主动语音轮次控制器（LPS-TC）、大规模真实世界数据集（WildTurn）及双层评估方案，显著提升全双工对话的轮次时机控制与响应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有半双工系统响应滞后以及真实场景中全双工系统难以在保持响应质量的同时实现及时中断和反馈（backchannels）的问题，旨在提升自然全双工对话中的轮次时机准确性。

Method: 构建包含三个核心组件的通用风格感知全双工框架：1) LPS-TC：一种即插即用的轻量级主动语音轮次控制器，提供细粒度动作空间以支持反应性和主动性行为；2) WildTurn：包含约2,981小时过滤后的多轮立体声对话的大规模真实英语数据集，标注了5种轮次转换风格和5种反馈风格；3) 双层评估方案：在流式约束下同时评估块级时间精度和轮次级交互质量。

Result: LPS-TC结合半双工模型（如Qwen2.5-Omni）和全双工模型（如Freeze-Omni）进行实验，展示了其在时机适宜性和响应质量方面的优越性能；该框架具备丰富的口语动态特性，表现出细粒度的风格可控性和强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合LPS-TC和WildTurn数据集，实现了更自然、拟人化的 spoken interactions，有效解决了全双工对话中时机控制与响应质量的平衡难题，并验证了其在真实流式场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Compared with half-duplex dialogue systems where the system waits for user turn completion before it responds, natural full-duplex dialogue systems require agents to act proactively in real time, including timely interruptions and backchannels. This creates a key challenge: improving turn timing without sacrificing response quality. To address limitations in realistic proactive turn-taking, we build a generalized style-aware full-duplex framework with three key components. Firstly, we propose LPS-TC, a Lightweight Proactive Speech Turn Controller for plug-and-play integration. It features a fine-grained action space covering both reactive and proactive turn behaviors, enabling half-duplex models with full-duplex capabilities and enhancing existing full-duplex models with superior timing control. Secondly, we construct WildTurn, a large-scale, real-world English dataset containing approximately 2,981 hours of filtered multi-turn stereo conversations from face-to-face and telephone conversations, annotated with five turn-taking and five backchanneling styles. Trained on WildTurn, LPS-TC exhibits rich spoken dynamics that are not captured by existing static full-duplex benchmarks. Thirdly, we introduce a two-tier evaluation scheme that assesses both chunk-level timing precision and turn-level interaction quality under realistic streaming constraints. Our experiments, integrating LPS-TC with half-duplex models like Qwen2.5-Omni and full-duplex models like Freeze-Omni, showcase its superior performance in timing appropriateness and response quality. Our framework also demonstrates fine-grained style controllability and strong generalizability, enabling more natural and human-like spoken interactions.

</details>


### [75] [Evaluating LLMs on Conversational Text-to-SQL under Chain Ambiguity and Intent Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29543)
*Yujia Liu,Jiayan Lin,Zijin Hong,Zheng Yuan,Shengyuan Chen,Hao Chen,Qinggang Zhang,Xiao Huang,Feiran Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TIDE-Bench，一个针对对话式文本到SQL任务的基准测试，专注于评估链式歧义和意图漂移。现有基准主要关注执行准确率，忽略了多轮对话中用户意图的变化。TIDE-Bench基于BIRD数据集的514个锚点SQL构建了1,542个样本，并引入了专门的指标来评估链识别和漂移识别-解决能力。对12个先进LLM的评估揭示了模型在链识别方面存在瓶颈，且在处理意图漂移时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对话式文本到SQL基准测试主要评估执行准确率，未能充分覆盖多轮澄清和修订过程中用户意图的展开与变化（如链式歧义和意图漂移）。为了更全面地评估LLM在这一复杂场景下的能力，需要一个新的基准来专门针对这些模式进行评测。

Method: 1. 构建TIDE-Bench基准：基于BIRD数据集中的514个锚点SQL，生成1,542个样本。
2. 定义两种关键模式：链式歧义（未明确的问题触发带有条件依赖的分层澄清）和意图漂移（用户撤回并替换先前承诺的请求元素）。
3. 开发专用指标：除了执行准确率外，引入用于链识别和漂移识别-解决的独立评估指标。
4. 评估实验：对12个先进的LLM进行评估，分析其在上述模式下的表现。

Result: 1. TIDE-Bench成功构建了包含特定意图变化模式的测试集。
2. 评估结果显示，所有测试的LLM都存在持续的“链识别瓶颈”，且该瓶颈不受澄清频率的影响。
3. 模型在“漂移识别-解决”方面存在巨大差距。
4. 当链式歧义和意图漂移同时发生时，失败模式之间存在重叠。

Conclusion: TIDE-Bench填补了对话式文本到SQL领域在多轮意图变化评估方面的空白。研究表明，当前LLM在处理复杂的链式歧义和意图漂移时仍存在显著不足，特别是在链识别和意图变更的理解上。该基准及其代码的发布为后续研究提供了重要资源，旨在推动模型在这些复杂交互场景下能力的提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have established conversational text-to-SQL as a practical interface between users and databases, often involving multiple turns of clarification and revision. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate execution accuracy, leaving the unfolding and shifting of user intent across turns largely uncovered. To address this, we introduce TIDE-Bench, a benchmark for conversational text-to-SQL under chain ambiguity and intent drift evaluation, targeting two recurring patterns: chain ambiguity, where an underspecified question triggers layered clarification with conditional dependencies, and intent drift, where the user retracts and replaces a previously committed request element. Built on 514 anchor SQLs from BIRD, TIDE-Bench comprises 1,542 samples and introduces dedicated metrics for chain identification and drift recognition-resolution beyond execution accuracy. Evaluating 12 advanced LLMs reveals a persistent chain identification bottleneck unaffected by clarification frequency, a wide drift recognition-resolution gap, and overlap between failure modes when jointly activated. The corresponding code of TIDE-Bench is released for further research.

</details>


### [76] [PAUSE: Editable Strategy Artifacts for Long-Form Cultural Story Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28633)
*Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Mohammad Saifullah,Abdur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PAUSE（暂停与更新策略编辑）干预方法，将AI文化适应决策转化为可编辑的结构化策略，通过实验证明人类对策略的修改能有效传播至长文本生成的最终章节，提高了AI文化适应过程的透明度和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统日益参与文化适应过程，但其文化决策往往隐藏在提示词、模型内部计划或最终文本中，缺乏透明度，导致用户难以理解和控制这些关键的文化适配决策。

Method: 开发PAUSE框架，将文化适应策略作为中间结构化工件暴露给用户进行审查和编辑；在中国源序列化小说的两个案例中，测试人类对策略的编辑是否能有效传播到章节级别的 prose 生成中，并通过人工评估和标记审计对比编辑组与控制组的输出效果。

Result: 在9次编辑与控制的章节对比中，法官在所有9例中均选择编辑策略的输出；标记审计显示，8/9的编辑输出包含目标标记且无禁止标记，而控制组0/9包含目标标记且全部包含禁止标记，证明策略编辑具有高度的遵循性和有效性。

Conclusion: PAUSE提供了一种实用方法，使AI介导的文化适应在长期生成前更具可检查性和可争议性，尽管研究定位为小规模编辑遵循性验证，而非宣称输出具有文化权威性或文学质量提升，但显著增强了用户对AI文化决策的控制力。

Abstract: Generative AI systems increasingly mediate cultural adaptation, but their cultural decisions are often hidden inside prompts, transient model plans, or final prose. We study PAUSE (Pause-And-Update Strategy Editing), an intervention that exposes an editable adaptation strategy as a human control surface for cultural decisions in long-form story adaptation. The strategy is a structured artifact that can be inspected, edited, and then projected through downstream character, entity, and chapter-localization stages. In two Chinese-source serialized novels, we test whether human edits to this strategy propagate into chapter-level prose. Across 9 edited-vs-control chapter comparisons, judges select the edited-strategy output in all 9; a marker audit shows target markers in 8/9 edited outputs and 0/9 controls, with forbidden markers absent from edited outputs and present in all controls. We frame these results as a smoke-scale edit-adherence study, not a claim that the outputs are culturally authoritative or literary-quality improvements. PAUSE offers one practical way to make AI-mediated cultural adaptation more inspectable and contestable before decisions propagate through long-form generation.

</details>


### [77] [Do MLLMs Really Understand Low-Resource Khmer Documents? A Pilot Study on Khmer Document VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28635)
*Nimol Thuon,Panhapin Theang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对开源多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在低资源高棉语文档理解任务中的可靠性进行了诊断性评估。研究发现，虽然引入外部OCR辅助能显著提升准确率至61.9%左右，但原生高棉语及混合脚本内容的理解仍是当前模型的显著瓶颈，可靠的原生高棉语文档智能处理尚待突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在低资源、非拉丁语系场景下的可靠性存疑，特别是针对包含复杂排版、高棉-英语混合字段及双币种数值的高棉语表单，且缺乏相关的高质量评估资源。

Method: 基于KH-FUNSD数据集构建子集，涵盖发票、收据等业务表单；采用直接图像提示与结合解析器/外部OCR（Tesseract, PaddleOCR）的配置，对Qwen-VL系列模型（重点评估Qwen3-VL-8B）进行能力与失效模式分析。

Result: Qwen3-VL-8B直接推理准确率为51.9%；引入外部OCR后性能最佳，Tesseract达61.9%，PaddleOCR达61.6%。高棉语和混合脚本答案的处理难度显著高于英语和数字字段。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs能有效处理视觉清晰的英语和结构化数字内容，但在原生高棉语文档理解方面仍存在重大挑战，尚未达到可靠水平。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have advanced document understanding, visual question answering, and text extraction. However, their reliability in low-resource, non-Latin settings remains uncertain. Khmer form documents present particular challenges because they contain complex script forms, mixed Khmer-English fields, and monetary values in both Cambodian Riel and US Dollars. Available resources for Khmer Document VQA are also limited. This paper presents a pilot diagnostic evaluation of open MLLMs on Khmer document images. We construct an evaluation subset from the previously introduced KH-FUNSD collection, covering invoices, receipts, quotations, and other business forms. The subset includes questions in English and Khmer, with answers retained in their original English, Khmer, mixed-script, or numeric forms. Rather than introducing a full public benchmark, this study examines the capabilities and failure modes of existing models. We evaluate representative open Qwen-VL models using direct image-based prompting and compare parser-assisted and external OCR-assisted configurations with Qwen3-VL-8B. Direct Qwen3-VL-8B outperforms smaller models, achieving 51.9% overall accuracy, although performance remains limited for Khmer-script and mixed-script answers. External OCR produces the strongest results, reaching 61.9% with Tesseract and 61.6% with PaddleOCR. Nevertheless, Khmer-script answers remain substantially more difficult than English and numeric fields. The results indicate that current MLLMs can process visually clear English and structured numeric content, but reliable native Khmer document understanding remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [78] [PromptKWS: A Novel Prompt-Guided Open-Vocabulary Keyword Spotting Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28640)
*Gaopeng Xu,Chengfei Li,Xianliang Wang,Lin Zhu,Juan Wei,Wenpeng Li,Jianwei Niu,Jie Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PromptKWS框架，通过引入提示短语预测网络(PPN)和跨注意力机制，显著提升开放词汇关键词识别(KWS)在噪声和发音变化环境下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有开放词汇KWS系统在复杂真实世界环境（如噪声、发音变化）中表现不佳的问题，提升系统鲁棒性和准确率。

Method: 1. 构建提示短语预测网络(PPN)，采用编码器-解码器架构提取关键词提示嵌入；2. 利用提示-声学多头交叉注意力(MHCA)将提示嵌入注入到KWS编码器中。

Result: 相比基线系统，唤醒率提升超过10%；相比纯声学模型，在测试集上的平均准确率提升超过15%，尤其在噪声和发音变化场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: PromptKWS通过结合语义提示与声学特征，有效增强了KWS系统在复杂环境下的适应能力和性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we present PromptKWS, a novel Prompt-guided keyword spotting (KWS) framework to improve the accuracy of open vocabulary KWS systems. In specific terms, we introduce the Prompt Phrases Prediction Network (PPN), an encoder-decoder architecture designed to effectively extract keyword prompts embeddings. we employ the PPN encoder to encode the keyword prompts and infuse the prompt embedding into the Prompt-guided KWS encoder by utilizing a Prompt-acoustic Multi-head Cross-attention (MHCA). Experiments show that PromptKWS improves the wakeup rate by over 10% compared to baseline system. Notably, another strength of PromptKWS is its ability to effectively leverage keyword prompts for adapting to complex real-world environments involving noise and pronunciation variations. In comparison to purely acoustic models, which often struggle in such situations, PromptKWS demonstrates remarkable performance, with an average accuracy improvement of over 15% in test sets.

</details>


### [79] [Terminal-Bench-LILT: Multilingual Agentic Coding Benchmark Grounded in Language, Region, and Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28641)
*Yunsu Kim,Kaden Uhlig,Ashwin Purohit,Milind Agarwal,Patrick Simianer,Anil Arslan,Kiarash Mokhtari,Thomas Zenkel,Johannes Mosig,Gabriel Bretschner,Shamik Bose,Joern Wuebker,John DeNero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Terminal-Bench-LILT，包含300个涵盖10种非英语语言的真实编程任务，旨在解决现有评估仅关注英语的局限性。实验显示最强模型通过率仅为63.1%，且多语言编程能力与通用基准排名无关，揭示了该领域的独特性与不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码代理评估主要局限于英语环境，无法反映现实世界中多语言部署的需求，缺乏对非英语软件特有问题的考量。

Method: 构建包含阿拉伯语、中文等10种语言的300个真实编程任务集，任务聚焦国际化、编码、文本规范化及文化惯例等非英语特有痛点；由母语程序员编写并经多阶段质量控制验证；对六个前沿模型进行评估。

Result: 最强模型的通过率仅为63.1%，许多任务无人能解；各语言表现差异显著，且多语言编程能力与通用代码基准排名无相关性。

Conclusion: 多语言编程能力是一个独立且未被充分探索的能力维度，当前模型在处理非英语特定软件工程问题上仍存在显著差距。

Abstract: Most evaluations for coding agents are conducted exclusively in English, which does not reflect real-world multilingual deployment. We present Terminal-Bench-LILT, a suite of 300 authentic coding tasks in ten languages: Arabic, Czech, German, Spanish, Hindi, Japanese, Korean, Serbian, Turkish, and Chinese. Each task targets issues specific to non-English software development that have no direct English equivalent, e.g., internationalization, encoding, text normalization, and cultural conventions. All tasks are authored by native-speaker programmers and validated through a multi-stage quality control pipeline. Evaluation of six frontier models reveals that even the strongest model reaches only 63.1\% pass rate, with many tasks unsolved by any model. Performance varies substantially by language and does not track general coding benchmark rankings, highlighting that multilingual coding competence is a distinct and underexplored capability axis. Sample tasks are available at https://github.com/lilt/terminal-bench-lilt

</details>


### [80] [Redesigning and Auditing Deep Research Writing for Faithful Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28643)
*Hiroaki Hayashi,Pranav Narayanan Venkit,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLAIMPROBE评估指标和CLAIMWRITER生成框架，解决深度研究系统中事实错误和幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于量表的评估方法往往掩盖了深度研究（DR）系统在生成报告中存在的细粒度事实失败（如幻觉、错误归因等）。

Method: 1. 提出CLAIMPROBE：一种声明级审计方法，将报告分解为声明，并针对检索证据测量幻觉、错误归因、引用规范性和必要事实召回率。
2. 提出CLAIMWRITER：一种分层声明驱动的作者模型，通过提取源事实、映射到查询大纲，并从与源链接的声明表示中起草每个部分来生成报告。

Result: 在三个现有DR框架中替换为CLAIMWRITER后：幻觉减少2.6至4.5倍，必要事实召回率提高1.2至1.7倍，同时保持整体报告质量；且在源数据变更时，能以最高速率传播更改并更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 声明级审计能揭示被传统评估掩盖的问题，而基于声明的生成方法（CLAIMWRITER）能显著降低幻觉并提高事实准确性及更新效率。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluations of deep-research (DR) systems often obscure fine-grained factual failures in generated reports. We introduce CLAIMPROBE, a claim-level audit that decomposes DR reports into claims and measures hallucination, misattribution, citation hygiene, and necessary-fact recall against retrieved evidence. Using CLAIMPROBE, we find that strong DR pipelines can omit key evidence and misattribute claims even when their rubric scores remain stable. We then propose CLAIMWRITER, a hierarchical claim-based writer that extracts source facts, maps them to a query-derived outline, and drafts each section from a source-linked claim representation. Across three prior DR frameworks, replacing only the report writer with CLAIMWRITER reduces hallucination by 2.6 to 4.5 times and improves necessary-fact recall by 1.2 to 1.7 times, while largely preserving overall report quality. CLAIMWRITER also enables localized revision: when sources change, it propagates changed source facts into revised reports at the highest rate among update methods, while also being more cost-effective.

</details>


### [81] [Cross Lingual Transfer in Tulu Legal Comprehension: Script-Dependent Improvement and RAG-Induced Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28645)
*Sindhu Shetty,Spurthi Setty,Natan Vidra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究以法律领域为背景，评估了Llama3、Hex-1和Sarvam三个模型在低资源德拉威语（Tulu）上的跨语言理解能力。研究发现，虽然通过脚本转写（特别是转为Kannada）能带来初步理解，但检索增强生成（RAG）效果参差不齐。模型失败的主要原因并非语料库内容，而是模型自身的推理缺陷，如事实替换和幻觉。研究提出了一种结合推理轨迹分析和统计诚实性框架的新方法，用于更严谨地评估低资源多语言RAG系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言缺乏充足的训练语料，通常依赖高资源语言作为脚手架进行理解。然而，目前缺乏严格的评估方法来识别模型在跨语言低资源环境下的失效机制，特别是在需要精确理解的领域（如法律）。

Method: 1. 选取三种大语言模型（Llama3, Hex-1, Sarvam）。
2. 测试对象为用低资源德拉威语（Tulu）撰写的法律投诉分类任务。
3. 引入脚本转写策略（将Tulu转换为其他德拉威语脚本，如Kannada）以辅助理解。
4. 构建基于Kannada法律论文语料库的RAG框架进行检索增强。
5. 采用推理轨迹分析（reasoning-trace analysis）和统计诚实性框架（statistical-honesty framework）对模型表现进行深度评估。

Result: 1. 脚本转写能帮助模型获得初步理解，且效果高度依赖具体脚本（Kannada转写效果最好）。
2. RAG框架下的结果混合：部分条件下有微弱正向趋势，但失败常见于两个维度：事实替换（过度依赖特定段落片段导致推理偏差）和幻觉（无依据的虚构）。
3. 根本原因分析表明，失败源于模型的信息解析和后续推理能力不足，而非语料库本身的问题。
4. 脚本依赖性与RAG鲁棒性之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 在低资源领域中，模型的理解失败主要归因于其内部推理机制（解析与推理），而非外部数据源。脚本依赖性和RAG鲁棒性密切相关。所提出的推理轨迹分析和统计诚实性框架为低资源多语言RAG系统的评估提供了更具普适性的严格方法论。

Abstract: Low-resource languages without an adequate training corpus often use a related, higher-resource language as a scaffold for comprehension. Still, there is a need to develop rigorous evaluation methods to identify when models fail in cross lingual low-resource environments. Using the legal domain as a backdrop, three models (Llama3, Hex-1, Sarvam) were tested on the ability to classify legal complaints written in a low resource Dravidian language (Tulu). Transliterating queries across Dravidian scripts allowed models to gain a preliminary understanding of speakers' complaints without the use of wide scale training, though the level of comprehension was heavily script dependent (with Kannada - another relatively low-resource language - producing the strongest positive trend). Retrieving from a corpus of Kannada legal papers across a RAG framework caused mixed results. Some models had a weak positive trend in comprehension under certain conditions, but when models failed, it was often across two axes: fact substitution (fixating on specific passage excerpts that skewed reasoning) and confabulation (hallucination that had no basis in either query or corpus). Within low resource domains, results identify the model's parsing of information and subsequent reasoning as the source of reasoning failure, rather than corpus contents. Script-dependent comprehension and RAG robustness also seem to travel together. This is further supported by the reasoning-trace analysis and a statistical-honesty framework deployed - techniques that are more broadly applicable to low-resource multilingual RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [82] [Can Large Language Models Identify Meaningful Touchpoints in Conversion Attribution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28649)
*Jinqi Wu,Sishuo Chen,Zhangming Chan,Yong Bai,Chao Yi,Han Zhu,Shuodian Yu,Lei Zhang,Sheng Chen,Chenghuan Hou,Jian Xu,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对电商转化归因中触点选择问题，揭示现有协同过滤启发式方法存在语义偏差，并通过评估大语言模型（LLMs）发现其能有效识别隐性相关触点。研究分析了提示策略和模型选择的影响，并利用LLM生成的标签提升工业级CVR模型性能，为从规则匹配转向语义推理提供了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的转化归因触点选择方法严重依赖基于协同过滤的启发式规则，无法对齐用户感知的语义意图，导致许多隐含相关且具有语义意义的触点被遗漏。

Method: 通过人工标注揭示语义差距，系统评估大语言模型（LLMs）在识别隐性关联触点方面的能力；分析不同提示策略和基础模型选择对识别性能的影响；利用LLM归因的转化标签增强工业级点击率（CVR）模型的训练。

Result: LLMs能有效 uncover 大量隐性相关触点，但在选择性能上仍有改进空间；揭示了LLMs的推理模式与有效性；在离线实验中，使用LLM归因标签显著提升了工业CVR模型的性能。

Conclusion: 研究证明了LLMs在转化归因中的潜力，指出从机械规则匹配向人类对齐的语义推理转型的新方向，并为工业界应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Touchpoint selection in conversion attribution, namely identifying meaningful touchpoints contributing to conversions, is essential for e-commerce recommendation and online advertising. Current selection methods rely heavily on collaborative-filtering-based heuristics, which fail to align with user-perceived semantic intent. Through human annotation, we reveal a significant semantic gap: many implicitly-related, semantically relevant touchpoints remain undetected by existing rules. Therefore, we systematically evaluate the capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) in identifying these hidden associations. Our evaluation shows that while LLMs effectively uncover a substantial portion of implicitly-related touchpoints, significant room for improvement remains in their selection performance. Furthermore, we analyze the impact of different prompting strategies and foundation model choices on identification performance, providing valuable insights into their reasoning patterns and effectiveness. These insights offer a new roadmap for transitioning conversion attribution from mechanical rule-matching to human-aligned semantic reasoning. Moreover, we leverage the LLM-attributed conversion labels for enhancing industrial CVR model training and achieve significant offline performance gains, showing the potential of LLMs in conversion attribution.

</details>


### [83] [Test-Time Scaling for Scientific Equation Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28660)
*Haowei Lin,Hubert Lim,Xiangyu Wang,Letian Huang,Di He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了测试时扩展（TTS）在开放式自动方程发现任务中的应用，提出统一的计算分配视角，并证实搜索宽度是决定性能的关键参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时扩展（TTS）研究多集中于数学和编程等封闭式任务，缺乏对开放式任务（如自动方程发现）中模型如何根据数据反馈搜索候选方程的深入探讨。

Method: 将LLM驱动的方程发现建模为迭代搜索过程，统一了Best-of-N、序列细化、树搜索和进化类方法；通过固定预算下的最小并行控制器对比，隔离计算分配效应与提示工程的影响，并在LLM-SRBench基准上进行评估。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench任务中发现，搜索宽度是主要的分配参数：最佳宽度随计算预算增加而增大；种群分支比例和控制器选择影响较小；适当的宽度选择能通过提高并行度提升时钟效率。

Conclusion: 在有信息量的验证器支持下，控制探索与利用的平衡是扩展基于LLM的方程发现能力的核心关键。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves language model reasoning by allocating additional test-time compute, but prior work mainly studies closed-ended tasks such as math and coding. We study TTS for automated equation discovery, an open-ended setting where models search over candidate equations and rely on observed datapoints for feedback. We formulate LLM-driven equation discovery as an iterative search process that unifies Best-of-N, sequential refinement, tree search, and evolution-style methods under a common compute-allocation view. To isolate allocation effects from prompt engineering and other heuristics, we compare minimal parallel controllers under fixed budgets. On LLM-SRBench equation-discovery tasks, we find that search width is the dominant allocation parameter: the best width in our sweep generally increases with the compute budget, while the population--branching split and controller choice matter less. Appropriate width selection also improves wall-clock efficiency by increasing parallelism. These results suggest that, given an informative verifier, controlling exploration and exploitation is central to scaling LLM-based equation discovery.

</details>


### [84] [GreenBench: Benchmarking Energy Efficiency and Carbon Footprint of Open-Source LLM Inference on Apple Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28667)
*Rajeswari Kannan,Raj Firke,Shreya Bengle,Srushti Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究构建了GreenBench基准框架，评估了5个开源LLM在Apple M4 Pro芯片上的能效表现。结果显示，M4 Pro在单用户部署中比数据中心GPU节能30-40倍，且小模型（3-3.8B）在大模型上具有显著的吞吐量和能效优势。研究为不同需求场景提供了具体的模型选择建议及碳排放估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLM）的快速普及引发了对其推理阶段环境影响的关注，但现有绿色AI研究主要集中于数据中心GPU和嵌入式平台。Apple Silicon凭借其统一内存架构，其在LLM推理中的能源特征尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出GreenBench基准测试框架，在配备48GB统一内存的Apple M4 Pro上，对五个参数量在3-9B之间的开源LLM进行三项NLP任务的评估。使用macOS powermetrics直接测量功率，并结合Ollama的纳秒级精度计时，分析能效、吞吐量及碳足迹。

Result: 1. M4 Pro在持续推理期间CPU+GPU封装功耗仅为0.47W，系统总功耗为8-12W，相比数据中心GPU在单用户部署中实现每token能效提升30-40倍。
2. 较小模型（3-3.8B）相比大模型（7-9B）提供2.6-4.2倍的更高吞吐量，并减少高达62%的每token能耗。
3. Pareto分析显示Qwen 2.5 (7B)在准确率（57% MMLU）与效率（59 tokens/s）之间达到最佳平衡；Llama 3.2 (3B)适合延迟关键型应用（175 tokens/s）。
4. 提供了包级别和系统级别的每token能耗数据，以及针对印度和美国电网的CO2估算。

Conclusion: Apple Silicon在LLM推理方面展现出卓越的能效潜力，特别是在单用户本地部署场景下。研究明确了不同规模模型在性能与能效间的权衡，为开发者根据具体应用场景（如高精度需求或低延迟需求）选择合适的模型提供了实证依据，并强调了边缘设备在降低LLM环境足迹方面的重要性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Large Language Models (LLMs) has raised concerns about their environmental impact during inference. While Green AI research has focused on datacenter GPUs and embedded platforms, the energy profile of LLM inference on Apple Silicon, with its unified memory architecture, remains unstudied. This paper presents GreenBench, a benchmarking framework that evaluates the energy efficiency, throughput, and carbon footprint of five open-source LLMs (3-9B parameters) across three NLP tasks on an Apple M4 Pro with 48 GB unified memory. Using macOS powermetrics for direct power measurement and Ollama's nanosecond-precision timing, we find that the M4 Pro draws only 0.47 W of CPU+GPU package power during sustained inference, with total system power of 8-12 W, achieving 30-40x better energy efficiency per token than datacenter GPUs in single-user deployment. Smaller models (3-3.8B) deliver 2.6-4.2x higher throughput and up to 62% less energy per token than larger models (7-9B). Pareto analysis identifies Qwen 2.5 (7B) as the optimal accuracy-efficiency trade-off at 57% MMLU and 59 tokens/s, while Llama 3.2 (3B) suits latency-critical applications at 175 tokens/s. We provide per-token energy at package and system levels with CO2 estimates for India and US grids.

</details>


### [85] [ReVA: A Region-Aware Visual Assistant for Visually Grounded Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28707)
*Anoop Senthil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReVA模型，通过双桥接机制整合全局与区域级视觉特征，解决MLLMs在VQA中的空间推理和幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在需要精确空间推理和细粒度视觉理解的问答任务中表现不佳，常出现对象、属性和空间幻觉，原因是缺乏足够的区域级和细粒度视觉定位能力。

Method: 构建ReVA模型，冻结CLIP ViT-L/14作为视觉编码器，连接Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct LLM。使用双桥接器：图像桥将最终Transformer块特征映射为图像令牌；区域桥从ViT各层提取丰富中间特征，映射为每个边界框的K个区域令牌。利用RAM++、spaCy和Grounding DINO提供零样本边界框检测。将图像令牌和区域令牌拼接为LLM提示前缀，联合编码场景上下文和细粒度区域证据。

Result: 在VQAv2、MMBench、POPE和SEED-Bench上评估，ReVA在POPE数据集上达到82.85%的平均F1分数，优于仅使用图像令牌的基线模型（81.14%）。

Conclusion: 显式的区域感知视觉表示能有效减少对象幻觉，提升MLLMs的事实 grounding 能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in Visual Question Answering (VQA), yet they continue to struggle with questions requiring precise spatial reasoning and fine-grained visual understanding. These limitations often manifest as object, attribute, and spatial hallucinations, where models generate confident but visually unsupported responses due to insufficient region-level and fine-grained visual grounding. To address this challenge, we propose ReVA, a region-aware VQA model that employs a frozen CLIP ViT-L/14 Vision Transformer (ViT) and a Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct large language model (LLM) connected through a dual bridge that aligns both whole-image and region-level representations with the LLM's embedding space. The image bridge maps final transformer block features into image tokens. The region bridge maps cropped features from enriched intermediate features across ViT blocks so early texture and later object cues are more evident, into K region tokens for every bounding box. ReVA uses a detector stack that supplies automatic zero-shot bounding boxes that are both question-agnostic and question-dependent, using RAM++ (Recognize Anything Model), spaCy, and Grounding DINO. The image tokens and region tokens are concatenated as an LLM prompt prefix to jointly encode scene-level context and fine-grained regional evidence when answering questions. Evaluated on VQAv2, MMBench, POPE, and SEED-Bench, ReVA achieves 82.85% mean F1 on POPE, compared with 81.14% for an image-token baseline without region tokens. These results demonstrate that explicit region-aware visual representations reduce object hallucination and improve the factual grounding of MLLMs.

</details>


### [86] [Evaluating Multilingual Sentence Embeddings for Translation Error Detection:An English--Greek Contrastive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28776)
*Eleftherios Kalogeros,Athanasios Ntalakas,Manolis Gergatsoulis,Paschalis Nikolaou,Sotiria-Lito Alexaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种多语言句子嵌入模型与COMETKiwi在区分英-希正确翻译与细微错误翻译上的表现。BGE-M3在嵌入模型中表现最佳（89.30%准确率），但COMETKiwi整体精度更高（94.49%）。结果显示嵌入模型对显式事实/词汇错误敏感，而COMETKiwi在时态、代词等难点上表现更好。两者具有互补性，建议将嵌入模型作为更广泛翻译评估框架的组件而非独立指标使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多语言句子嵌入广泛用于跨语言语义相似度估计，但其对细粒度翻译错误的敏感性尚未得到充分理解。本研究旨在探究通用多语言嵌入模型能否有效区分正确的英-希翻译与其经过最小修改的错误替代版本。

Method: 构建了一个包含1,850个示例的对比数据集，涵盖10个核心和5个探索性错误类别（如事实、词汇语义、语法、关系、指称及语篇层面），由两位翻译专家审核。评估了五种多语言句子嵌入模型（BGE-M3, Multilingual E5, Multilingual MPNet, LaBSE, Jina Embeddings v3）通过余弦相似度计算源句与译文间的相似度，并以参考无关的COMETKiwi模型作为机器翻译质量估计基线。评估指标包括对比准确率和类别特定的分数边际。

Result: BGE-M3在嵌入模型中取得最高准确率89.30%，COMETKiwi达到94.49%。嵌入模型能更可靠地检测显式的事实和词汇变化，但在时态/体貌和代词指称错误上表现较弱。COMETKiwi在时态、代词指称和语义角色等困难类别上性能提升，但对日期和时间错误敏感度较低，且在数字处理上不如嵌入模型。

Conclusion: 多语言句子嵌入模型与COMETKiwi展现出互补的错误敏感性特征。虽然嵌入模型能提供有用的语义充足性信号，但它们更适合作为更广泛的翻译评估框架的组成部分，而不宜作为独立的评估指标。

Abstract: Multilingual sentence embeddings are increasingly used to estimate semantic similarity across languages, yet their sensitivity to fine-grained translation errors remains insufficiently understood. This study investigates whether general-purpose multilingual embedding models can distinguish correct English-Greek translations from minimally modified erroneous alternatives. A contrastive dataset was developed from FLORES+ sentence-aligned reference translations and reviewed by two translation experts. It contains 1,850 examples across ten core and five exploratory error categories, covering factual, lexical-semantic, grammatical, relational, referential, and discourse-level phenomena.
  Five multilingual sentence-embedding models (BGE-M3, Multilingual E5, Multilingual MPNet, LaBSE, and Jina Embeddings v3) were evaluated using cosine similarity between each English source sentence and its correct and erroneous Greek translations. A reference-free COMETKiwi model was also evaluated as an MT quality-estimation baseline. Performance was assessed through contrastive accuracy and score margins for category-specific sensitivity. BGE-M3 achieved the highest accuracy among embedding models at 89.30 percent, while COMETKiwi achieved 94.49 percent. Embedding models detected explicit factual and lexical changes more reliably than tense-and-aspect and pronoun-coreference errors. COMETKiwi improved performance on several difficult categories, including tense and aspect, pronoun and coreference, and semantic-role errors, but showed lower sensitivity to date-and-time errors and underperformed the embedding models on numbers. The results show complementary error-sensitivity profiles: multilingual sentence embeddings provide useful semantic adequacy signals but are better suited as components of broader translation-evaluation frameworks than as standalone metrics.

</details>


### [87] [A rigor-matched audit of periodic-step layer skipping for efficient llm inference: conflayers versus swift, with a supplemental analysis of trained routing alternatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28846)
*Prateek Kumar Sikdar,Arpan Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过严格的三种子审计对比了两种在线搜索方法（ConfLayers和SWIFT）及两种训练路由方法（LayerRoute和LayerDrop）。结果显示SWIFT在准确性和推理速度上均优于ConfLayers；而训练路由方法虽加速有限且准确率大幅下降，但本研究提供了严谨的效率评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型（LLM）推理中的层跳过方法，缺乏在严格匹配条件下对多种决策粒度方法的系统性、公平比较，旨在提供 rigorous-matched 的评估以厘清各方法的真实性能。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-0.5B/1.5B模型在GSM8K和CNN/DailyMail任务上进行三种子审计。对比两类方法：1）在线搜索法：ConfLayers（置信门控早退）与SWIFT（自投机解码）；2）训练路由法：LayerRoute与LayerDrop。分离在线搜索开销与纯推理成本进行精细化分析。

Result: 1）SWIFT在3/4情况下准确率最高，且去除搜索开销后其纯推理速度比ConfLayers快5-21%；2）ConfLayers搜索开销稳定（1-2%），SWIFT较大且波动（至28.7%）；3）训练路由方法仅获1.08-1.33x加速，但准确率显著低于在线搜索法，LayerRoute在GSM8K上近乎失效（准确率0.003）。

Conclusion: SWIFT是综合性能最强的方法，揭示了纳秒级墙钟时间可能误导性能判断，必须分离搜索开销。同时指出训练路由方法在细粒度输入门控下存在严重精度损失。研究发布了完整的审计协议模板，以促进未来效率比较的严谨性。

Abstract: Layer-skipping methods for efficient LLM inference decide, at some granularity, which transformer layers to execute for a given input. We present a rigor-matched, three-seed audit of two periodic-step, search-based methods that make this decision online at inference time and re-evaluate it every few generation steps: a confidence-gated early-exit baseline (ConfLayers) and genuine self-speculative decoding (SWIFT, Xia et al. 2024), together with vanilla autoregressive decoding, across two model scales (Qwen2.5-0.5B and Qwen2.5-1.5B) and two tasks (GSM8K reasoning and CNN/DailyMail summarization). SWIFT is the strongest method on accuracy in three of four cells; ConfLayers is dominated everywhere, with particularly large deficits on GSM8K at 1.5B. Once online-search overhead is separated from pure inference cost, SWIFT's true inference speed is faster than ConfLayers's in all four cells (5-21%), reversing the naive wall-clock ranking in three of them. ConfLayers's search overhead is small and stable (1-2% of cost), while SWIFT's is larger and more variable (up to 28.7%). We additionally examine two trained-routing methods, LayerRoute (Sikdar, 2026) and LayerDrop (Fan et al. 2020), as a supplemental analysis because they operate at coarser decision granularities. Under a verified protocol with genuine per-input gating, a genuine full-model baseline, and genuine inference-time compute skipping, both show modest speedups (1.08-1.33x) but accuracy well below the periodic-step methods, including a near-total collapse for LayerRoute on GSM8K at 1.5B (0.003 mean exact-match across three seeds). We release the full audit protocol as a template for rigor-matched efficiency comparisons.

</details>


### [88] [Latent-Space Intervention for Cross-Lingual Factual Consistency: Consistency Improvements without Accuracy Drops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28860)
*Faeze Ghorbanpour,Constanza Fierro,Alexander Fraser,Anders Sogaard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过训练层特定自编码器对平行多语言表示进行干预，在推理时对事实性问答提示应用校正，从而显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）跨语言回答的一致性，且未损害事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（LLMs）在面对相同事实性问题时，在不同语言间产生不一致答案的问题。

Method: 在平行多语言表示上训练层特定自编码器，并在推理阶段对事实性问答提示应用潜在空间干预（包括自编码器重建、PCA投影和均值偏移）。

Result: 潜在干预改善了语言间的几何对齐，从而提高了与英语的跨语言一致性。在开放式问答中，英语-阿拉伯语和英语-俄语对的Spearman等级相关系数分别提升0.16和0.20；在多项选择题问答中，答案与英语的一致性在KLAR和mParaRel数据集上均稳定提升。消融实验显示，自编码器重建在无准确率损失下带来一致增益，PCA贡献有限，而均值偏移虽大幅提升一致性但牺牲了部分准确率。

Conclusion: 跨语言潜在空间干预能有效减少LLM的多语言事实回答不一致性，是一种在不降低事实准确性的前提下提升跨语言一致性的有效方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often answer the same factual question differently across languages. We study whether cross-lingual latent-space intervention can reduce this inconsistency. We train layer-specific autoencoders on parallel multilingual representations and apply inference-time corrections to factual QA prompts. We find that latent intervention improves geometric alignment between languages, and that this improvement translates into consistent gains in cross-lingual consistency with English across both open-ended and multiple-choice QA formats, without degrading factual accuracy. In open-ended QA, Spearman's rank correlation between English and non-English languages improves substantially, with gains of 0.16 for English-Arabic and 0.20 for English-Russian pairs. In multiple-choice QA, answer agreement with English improves consistently across both KLAR and mParaRel. Ablations show that AE reconstruction yields consistent gains at no accuracy cost, while PCA projection contributes marginally, and mean-shift produces substantially larger consistency gains in open-ended QA at the cost of some accuracy.

</details>


### [89] [No Detectable Change in Side-Level WER from Prompt-Level Context: A Preregistered Ablation on a Production Oral-History Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28875)
*Theodore O. Cochran,Stephanie Dodson,Keith Nore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在真实生产环境中测试向大型多模态模型提供上下文以提升语音转录准确率，发现对整体词错误率（WER）无显著影响，且存在较大的运行波动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前在小模型上的研究显示，在推理时提供上下文能显著提升领域特定的语音转录性能；本研究旨在验证该机制在实际生产的口述历史转录工具中的有效性。

Method: 采用预注册的配对消融实验设计，冻结分析代码哈希值以确保确认性批次评分的客观性。使用19张卡带侧边（约10.6小时70-80年代受损音频），通过gpt-4o-transcribe和gemini-2.5-flash两种配置，对比全上下文、无上下文及混合提示条件下的转录结果，并与人工校正参考文本进行比对。

Result: 对于gpt-4o-transcribe，全上下文与无上下文的配对差异中位数为+0.6 WER点，置信区间为[-1.1, +1.0]，无统计显著性；Gemini结果不稳定无法推断。后续分析显示管道内部运行变异大于干预效果，且仅对特定短语有微小改善，不足以改变整体WER。

Conclusion: 简单的提示级上下文机制在实际生产中未能带来可检测的整体性能提升。评估上下文机制需结合序列对齐的术语级、插入及说话人标签指标，而非仅依赖聚合准确率。

Abstract: Supplying context at inference time to a large multimodal model is an inexpensive lever for adapting speech transcription to a domain, and earlier results on smaller models reported large gains. This work tested that mechanism where it ships, in the prompt-conditioning layer of a production oral-history transcription tool, on a sample from its own production corpus. Full prompt-level context did not detectably change side-level word error rate (WER), and none of the four preregistered hypotheses was supported. The design was a within-item paired ablation, preregistered with the analysis code frozen by hash before the confirmatory batch was scored; two disclosed gpt-4o pilot sides had been scored earlier, during scorer development. Nineteen cassette sides, about 10.6 hours of degraded 1970s-80s interview audio, were reprocessed through the production code path under three prompt arms, crossed with two deployed commercial configurations, gpt-4o-transcribe and gemini-2.5-flash, and scored against operator-corrected verbatim references. For gpt-4o-transcribe the median paired difference between the full-context and no-context arms was +0.6 WER points, with a side-resampled interval of [-1.1, +1.0]; the Gemini estimates were too unstable to support a comparable negative inference. A post-hoc rerun found run-to-run pipeline variability larger than the confirmatory differences, so effects of that size cannot be resolved from one transcription per cell. An implementation audit verified the manipulation was live, and sequence-alignment analysis found a small improvement on complete context-listed phrases, too small to materially change side-level WER, and for Gemini coexisting with worsened unlisted-token error. Evaluating context mechanisms therefore requires sequence-aligned term-level, insertion, and speaker-label measures alongside aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [90] [Moving the Mean Toward the Known Good, Not Beyond It: What Inference-Time Interventions and Weight Consolidation Buy in Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28886)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在在线装箱问题上，通过对模型生成的经过验证的成功案例进行LoRA整合训练，能显著提升模型在未见变体上的表现（减少1.7-3.1点的超额成本），但最佳候选解最终收敛于经典启发式算法水平，无法实现超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索生成循环中“生成、验证、选择”环节对模型学习的价值；基于推理时账本发现，模型编写的方案回顾有助于文档集成，而验证器被模仿导致大量伪造判决行，需明确有效学习机制。

Method: 采用“生成-验证-选择-LoRA整合”循环流程，针对在线装箱问题进行实验；设置价值过滤候选者对比随机整合控制组及仅监督微调（SFT）对照组；通过三次独立种子复现及一次性读取的保留测试集进行验证电池测试。

Result: 价值过滤候选者的整合使模型在保留变体上性能提升（p=0.008）；最佳观察到的候选解在所有三条谱系中精确收敛至经典启发式算法水平（0.021028），未再进一步；SFT对照表明是监督锚点而非对坏候选者的排斥导致了集中效应；整合降低了优于经典算法的候选者比例（10%降至3.9%），但因产量增加，绝对数量仍有所上升（5 vs 1）。

Conclusion: 平均质量的候选者可被购买并复制，但观察到的最佳解已触及经典启发式算法的上限，目前无法超越该基准；这表明单纯依靠生成和验证成功样本进行整合，其收益存在天花板。

Abstract: What does a generation loop gain from learning on its own verified successes? In cycles of generate, verify, select and LoRA-consolidate on online bin packing, training on value-filtered candidates shifts what the model writes on held-out variants toward value (-1.7 points of excess, p=0.008; -3.1 against a random-consolidation control, p=0.004) while the best observed candidate converges to the classic heuristic's level and no further. A confirmation battery replicates the whole procedure three times, with fresh seeds and a never-consulted held-out set read exactly once: the mean was nearly identical in all three lineages (-2.0, -1.8, -1.9), and after aggregating within held-out variant all seven evaluable variants favored consolidation (p=0.008). The best observed candidate moved to the classic heuristic's level, exactly (0.021028 in all three lineages, for attract and for the random control alike), and never beyond it. A matched SFT-only control shows the supervised anchor, not repulsion from bad candidates, does the concentrating (96% of candidates land exactly at the classic heuristic's level). The tails cut both ways: consolidation lowers the per-candidate rate of better-than-classic candidates (10% to 3.9%) while its larger production yields more such candidates absolutely (5 against 1, on few events). As motivation we report the inference-time ledger that led here: a model-written schematic recap buys judged document integration and nothing buys development; a verifier written into the stream is imitated, 16.4 fabricated verdict lines per notebook. Mean quality among valid candidates can be bought and replicated; the observed best goes to the classic and, so far, never beyond it.

</details>


### [91] [Cloud and On-Premises Deployment of Uzbek Legal RAG via Targeted Retriever Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29284)
*Tatul Danielyan,Mariam Avetisyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对乌兹别克语法律问答的低资源场景，构建了检索增强（RAG）助手及两个新基准测试。研究发现通过微调可缩小开源与专有模型差距，但鉴于硬件需求和法律条文频繁更新，微调并不实用。最终发布了基准、代码及优化的文本嵌入模型UTE-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用评测榜单无法捕捉低资源语言在法律问答中的特殊挑战，特别是在严格的运营成本限制和私有数据不出域（本地部署）的硬约束下，缺乏针对该特定场景的有效评估方法。

Method: 1. 构建并运营面向乌兹别克语的RAG法律助手，支持云端托管（追求质量）和本地部署（隐私保护）两种模式。2. 建立两个领域基准：包含178个专家标注查询的检索基准，以及包含504个问答对的端到端基准（使用LLM裁判并经人类法官验证）。3. 在不同模式下应用基准进行评估，并进行QLoRA微调实验以验证可行性。

Result: 1. 开源与专有模型之间的性能差距很小，且可通过微调低成本弥补。2. 训练出在开源模型中表现最佳的乌兹别克语文本嵌入器UTE-1。3. QLoRA实验显示，尽管微调能提升性能，但由于长上下文法律问答对硬件要求极高，且法律条文变动频繁，微调既不可行也无必要。

Conclusion: 在实际生产环境中，面对低资源语言和严格约束，无需依赖高成本的长期微调。研究提供了实用的部署指导，并通过开源基准、评估代码和UTE-1模型支持未来的低资源法律NLP研究。

Abstract: Deploying large language models for legal question answering raises challenges that general-purpose leaderboards do not capture, particularly for low-resource languages and under hard operational constraints. We report on building and operating a retrieval-augmented (RAG) legal assistant for Uzbek that must run in two regimes: a managed cloud service that maximizes answer quality within a per-token cost ceiling, and an on-premises deployment for clients whose legal data may not leave their infrastructure, restricting us to open-weight models on limited local hardware under latency constraints. Because no evaluation existed for this setting, we build two domain benchmarks: a retrieval benchmark of 178 expert-annotated legal queries with gold provision spans, and an end-to-end benchmark of 504 expert-curated question--answer pairs scored by an LLM judge whose ratings we validate against human judgments and against an independent-family judge. Applying these benchmarks under each regime, we find the open-versus-proprietary gap is small and cheaply closed by fine-tuning. Therefore, we train UTE-1, which is a state-of-the-art text embedder among open models for Uzbek. We also demonstrate that closing the performance gap via fine-tuning is both impractical due to the intensive hardware demands of long-context legal Q\&A and unnecessary, given that legal acts change frequently. We support this by reporting a negative result from a QLoRA experiment. We distill practical guidance for similar deployments, drawn from a system serving real users in production. We release our benchmarks, evaluation code and the fine-tuned embedder (UTE-1) \href{https://metric-ai-lab.github.io/Uzbek-Legal-RAG/}{at this https URL} to support future work on low-resource legal NLP.

</details>


### [92] [VoiceCodeBench: Evaluating Exact Structured-Token Recovery in Automatic Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28916)
*Tyler Baumgartner,Brandon Tai,Lisa Kaelin-Martin,Candice Fan,Luc Debaupte,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VoiceCodeBench基准测试，指出传统WER指标无法充分评估ASR在精确结构化数据（如标识符、数值）恢复上的能力，揭示了现有系统在关键实体识别上的显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音识别评估主要依赖词错误率（WER），但许多工作流依赖于对标识符、路径和测量值的精确解析。即使转录文本流畅且WER较低，也可能损坏下游系统必须解析或执行的关键数值，因此需要更严格的评估标准。

Method: 构建VoiceCodeBench基准，包含300段人工录制的职场音频，涵盖8个工作流领域和1,482个目标实体。采用纯音频输入协议，除了WER外，引入规范令牌/实体匹配（CTEM）、任务成功率（TSR）及按类型精确恢复率进行评估。

Result: 在12个基线系统中，虽然较低的WER通常对应更好的结构化令牌恢复，但相关性仅为-0.73，表明WER不能完全决定性能。最佳基线系统的TSR仅为68.7%，意味着近三分之一的录音中至少有一个关键工作流值未被正确恢复。

Conclusion: 传统的WER指标不足以全面评估ASR输出在生产环境中的可用性。为了准确判断ASR是否能保留下游系统所需的精确值，必须引入对实体敏感的评估指标。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems are commonly evaluated with word error rate (WER), yet many voice workflows depend on exact written values for identifiers, paths, and measured quantities. A transcript can appear fluent and achieve low WER while corrupting a value that a downstream system must parse, store, or execute.
  We introduce VoiceCodeBench, a benchmark for evaluating exact structured-token recovery in English ASR. It contains 300 human-recorded workplace segments spanning eight workflow domains and 1,482 audited target entities across 26 entity types, each with a canonical written form recoverable from the audio. Under a raw-audio-only protocol, systems receive audio bytes without additional context or metadata. Alongside WER, we evaluate Canonical Token/Entity Match (CTEM), Task Success Rate (TSR), and per-type exact recovery.
  Across 12 baseline ASR systems, lower WER generally corresponded to better structured-token recovery but did not fully determine it: Spearman correlations were -0.73 for both WER versus CTEM and WER versus TSR. The strongest baseline by TSR reached only 68.7%, leaving nearly one third of recordings with at least one unrecovered workflow-critical value. These results show that entity-sensitive metrics are needed to assess whether ASR output preserves exact values that production systems must parse, route, store, compare, or execute.

</details>


### [93] [Causal Interventions Reveal Typologically Organized Syntactic Mechanisms in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28924)
*Sasha Boguraev,Toshiki Nakai,Kyle Mahowald,Julius Steuer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用机制可解释性技术，在多元语言大模型中发现并验证了跨语言的句法处理机制具有可迁移性，且迁移程度与语言类型学相似度呈正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语言学理论长期认为跨语言句法共性由相似机制处理，但因人脑处理机制缺乏细粒度、可操控的访问手段，该假设难以实证检验。

Method: 利用机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）技术，在四个多元语言大模型中，针对三种经典句法结构（主谓一致、代词性别一致、填充物-间隙提取），先隔离语言内部机制，再测试其跨语言迁移能力。

Result: 发现一致的跨语言机制迁移现象；迁移效果呈现梯度特征，即语言间类型学相似度越高，机制迁移越显著。

Conclusion: 研究为跨语言句法结构和多元语言处理提供了新假设，并展示了通过研究语言模型来反哺语言学理论的潜力。

Abstract: Linguistic theory has long recognized cross-linguistic syntactic regularities, leading to claims that these similar structures are processed by similar mechanisms. However, this hypothesis has been difficult to test empirically due to our lack of fine-grained, manipulable access of human processing mechanisms. In this work, we take advantage of techniques from mechanistic interpretability to study such a question in multilingual LMs. We first isolate language-internal mechanisms before attempting to transfer them cross-lingually. Across four models and three well-studied constructions (subject--verb number agreement, anaphoric pronoun gender agreement, and filler--gap object extraction) we find consistent cross-lingual mechanism transfer. We further find transfer to be graded, with more transfer between more typologically similar languages. We believe our work provides novel hypotheses about cross-linguistic syntactic structures and multilingual processing, and more broadly shows how the study of language models can help inform linguistic theory.

</details>


### [94] [Leveraging Turn-taking Dynamics for Intent Recognition in Multi-party Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28926)
*Galo Castillo-López,Alexis Lombard,Gaël de Chalendar,Nasredine Semmar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种利用辅助任务建模轮次转换动态的多方对话意图识别方法，通过引入“轮次转换熵”这一自监督目标提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法往往忽略多方交互中的动态特征，导致意图识别性能受限。

Method: 采用多任务学习框架，设计“轮次转换熵”作为自监督辅助目标，该目标基于说话人转换序列计算，用于量化交互模式的可预测性。

Result: 在多个预训练模型上的实验表明，加入该辅助任务后，意图识别性能优于忽视多方交互动态的现有方法；且该连续目标可作为单任务目标被有效学习，证明其携带有用信息。

Conclusion: 建模多方对话中的轮次转换动态有助于提升意图识别效果，提出的轮次转换熵是一个有效的信号源。

Abstract: We propose a multi-task learning approach for multi-party dialogue intent recognition that leverages an auxiliary task that models turn-taking dynamics. Specifically, we introduce turn-transition entropy, a self-supervised target computed from the sequence of speaker transitions, which quantifies the predictability of interaction patterns. Experiments on multiple pre-trained models demonstrate that incorporating this auxiliary task improves intent recognition performance, outperforming existing approaches which ignore multi-party interaction dynamics. We find that our proposed continuous target can be learned as a single-task objective, suggesting that it is an actual signal carrying useful information.

</details>


### [95] [The Hallucination Signal Is a Mean Shift: Why Simple Probes Suffice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28930)
*Jungseob Lee,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型幻觉检测信号主要由单一均值偏移主导，而非复杂的非线性结构。简单的L2正则化逻辑回归即可达到优异性能，证明了现有复杂探针架构的冗余性。提出的LayerMix方法通过聚合连续层信号，实现了无需oracle访问的接近最优检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管隐藏状态探针能有效检测LLM幻觉，但其信号几何特征尚不明确，导致研究者不断构建日益复杂的探针架构。本研究旨在厘清信号的真实几何结构，评估复杂架构的必要性，并寻找更高效的检测方法。

Method: 在三个7B规模模型和三个数据集上，采用配对示例范式进行分析。使用收缩线性判别分析（Shrinkage LDA）探索信号维度；对比多种控制下的架构替代方案与简单L2正则化逻辑回归的性能；提出LayerMix方法，通过聚合具有连续层分布的信号来匹配Oracle层性能。

Result: 1. 信号几何：幻觉检测信号主要由单一的均值偏移分量主导，移除该方向会导致检测率降至随机水平。
2. 复杂度归因：高维协方差估计的难度而非可利用的非线性导致了表观的架构复杂性。Shrinkage LDA填补了1D与全维分类器间约73%的差距。
3. 性能对比：简单的L2正则化逻辑回归（AUROC 0.952）优于或匹敌十二种受控架构替代方案。
4. LayerMix优势：多层聚合策略在无Oracle访问情况下达到了Oracle层的检测性能，超越了CLAP跨层注意力探针。

Conclusion: 在受控的配对示例范式中，LLM幻觉检测信号的几何结构相对简单，主要体现为均值偏移。这表明现有的复杂探针架构很大程度上是应对高维估计困难而非捕捉深层非线性特征。简单的线性模型结合有效的层聚合策略（如LayerMix）即可实现高性能检测，为简化探针设计提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Hidden-state probes effectively detect LLM hallucinations, but the geometry of the signal remains poorly characterized, driving increasingly complex probe architectures. Across three 7B-scale models and three datasets in a paired-example paradigm, we find the signal overwhelmingly dominated by a single mean-shift component, and removing this direction collapses detection to chance. Shrinkage linear discriminant analysis closes about 73% of the gap between 1D and full-dimensional classifiers, so apparent architectural complexity largely reflects high-dimensional covariance estimation difficulty rather than exploitable non-linearity. A simple L2-regularized logistic regression (0.952 AUROC) bounds or outperforms twelve controlled architectural alternatives, and our multi-layer aggregation exceeds CLAP cross-layer attention probing under matched paradigm. Because the signal spans a contiguous layer band, LayerMix aggregates it to match oracle-layer performance without oracle access. Our claims characterize the geometry within the controlled paired-example paradigm. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/LayerMix.

</details>


### [96] [You Know What I Mean: A Benchmark for Agentic Conversational Reference Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29834)
*Karen Fuchs,Uri Katz,Yoav Goldberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出对话参考接地（CoRG）问题及RepoRef基准，评估智能体在多工具环境中解析间接引用的能力，结果显示最佳智能体成功率仅67.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决协作对话中非直接命名引用的目标识别难题，该过程需结合对话上下文与外部工作区证据。

Method: 构建RepoRef基准，包含92个仓库中的400段开发者聊天数据，关联GitHub Issue、PR和Commit，通过多步骤工具使用进行参考解析。

Result: 当前智能体在CoRG任务上表现仍有局限，最佳智能体成功率为67.0%，意味着仍有三分之一引用未能正确解析。

Conclusion: CoRG为研究智能体在真实多工具环境下的搜索、检查及验证信息能力提供了具体基准，揭示了现有模型在此类复杂场景下的不足。

Abstract: Collaborative conversations frequently contain references whose targets are indirect rather than named: resolving "this looks like the fix discussed yesterday" requires combining conversational context with evidence from the surrounding workspace which is accessible through APIs or user interfaces. We formalize this problem as Conversational Reference Grounding (CoRG): using a given set of tools to resolve a reference in conversation to the unique external item intended by the speaker. CoRG is challenging because it combines lexical, semantic, and temporal cues distributed across the conversation and the external workspace. Agents must translate these heterogeneous signals into effective tool use: formulating strategies, discovering plausible candidates, inspecting their metadata and content, and ruling out close alternatives. We study CoRG through RepoRef, a benchmark of 400 developer-chat segments grounded in GitHub issues, pull requests, and commits across 92 repositories. Unlike single-shot retrieval tasks, RepoRef often requires multi-step tool use. Our results show that CoRG remains challenging for current agents, even the best agent reaches only 67.0% success rate, leaving one third of references unresolved. These findings position CoRG as a concrete benchmark for studying how agents search, inspect, and verify information in realistic multi-tool environments.

</details>


### [97] [VocalAffectBench: Evaluating Vocal Emotion Recognition in AI Audio Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28932)
*Models Luc Debaupte,Tyler Baumgartner,Brandon Tai,Candice Fan,Bill Wang,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VocalAffectBench，一个用于评估AI音频模型从原始语音中识别情感能力的公开基准。结果显示，尽管模型能提取部分情感信号，但在离散情感识别上表现脆弱，尤其是非中性情感（如惊讶和恐惧）的识别率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音产品的发展，需要利用语音中存在的、但转录文本中缺失的情感线索。目前缺乏专门针对AI音频模型仅凭原始音频识别表达情感的公开评测基准。

Method: 构建包含273个英语WAV片段（51位说话人，1.95小时，7类情感标签：愤怒、厌恶、恐惧、快乐、中性、悲伤、惊讶）的测试集。在无转录和无上下文元数据的情况下，对6个基线模型进行评估，并进行细粒度（7分类）和粗粒度（效价桶分析：正/中/负）的性能分析。

Result: 平均准确率为35.5%，最强模型gemini_3_5_flash达到46.5%（随机基线为14.3%）。在效价桶分析中，聚合准确率为50.9%。性能分布不均：中性情感召回率最高（75.6%），而惊讶（10.7%）和恐惧（15.4%）最低。

Conclusion: 当前基线模型能从语音中提取一定的情感信号，但离散表达情感识别仍然脆弱，特别是对于语音代理工作流中最重要的非中性情感。这表明现有AI音频模型在情感鲁棒性方面仍有巨大提升空间。

Abstract: Voice products increasingly need affective cues that are present in speech but absent from transcripts. We introduce VocalAffectBench, a public, test-only benchmark for evaluating whether AI audio models can identify expressed vocal emotion from raw audio. The benchmark contains 273 human-recorded English WAV clips from 51 speaker accounts totaling 1.95 hours across seven labels: angry, disgusted, fearful, happy, neutral, sad, and surprised, with 39 clips per class. All baselines are evaluated from audio alone, without transcripts or contextual metadata. Across six released baselines, average accuracy is 35.5%. The strongest baseline, gemini_3_5_flash, reaches 46.5% on the seven-way task, above the 14.3% random baseline but far from robust emotion recognition. A secondary valence-bucket analysis maps labels into positive, neutral, and negative classes, excluding surprised because its valence is ambiguous. Aggregate accuracy under this coarser view is 50.9%. Performance is highly uneven across classes. By recall, neutral is identified most reliably at 75.6% averaged across baselines, while surprised and fearful reach only 10.7% and 15.4%, respectively. These results show that the evaluated baselines can extract some affective signal from speech, but discrete expressed-emotion recognition remains fragile, especially for non-neutral emotions that are often most important in voice agent workflows.

</details>


### [98] [REIGN: Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks for Efficient Context-Length Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29899)
*Devrim Çavuşoğlu,Emre Akbaş*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出REIGN模型，通过冻结的引导网络（GN）缓存上下文块嵌入而非原始token，大幅降低长文档密集检索的训练成本（约四个数量级），并在多项基准测试中达到与更大参数模型相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长文档密集检索计算昂贵的问题。现有Token级编码器随序列长度二次方扩展，而长上下文嵌入模型通常需通过架构技巧或扩展数十亿参数LLM才能达到32K token限制。

Method: 提出REIGN（Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks），这是一种对比训练的孪生编码器。它操作于来自冻结引导网络（GN）的上下文化块嵌入序列，而非原始token。将Token级处理与文档级推理解耦，并将GN嵌入缓存至磁盘，主要针对文档到文档的多块输入检索。

Result: 1. 训练成本：相比分块Transformer微调，每文档训练成本降低约四个数量级。
2. 性能表现：在Wikipedia基准、LoCo OOD套件和真实专利检索案例中，REIGN在小参数预算下匹配了密集的长上下文检索器。
3. 对比优势：在专利任务中与比其大1.6-4.3倍的模型相当；在LoCo上与比其大20倍的模型性能差距小于0.65 nDCG@10。

Conclusion: REIGN通过解耦处理和缓存机制有效解决了长文档检索的高成本问题，以较小的参数规模实现了与大型模型相当的检索性能，并发布了相关的合成长文档检索基准。

Abstract: Dense retrieval over long documents is expensive. Token-level encoders scale quadratically in sequence length, and most long-context embedding models reach 32K tokens only through architectural workarounds or by stretching billion-parameter LLMs. We propose REIGN (Refurbished Embeddings with Integrated Guidance Networks), a contrastively trained bi-encoder that operates on sequences of contextualised chunk embeddings from a frozen Guidance Network (GN) rather than on raw tokens. REIGN targets multi-chunk inputs, primarily for document-to-document retrieval; single-chunk inputs stay with the GN. Decoupling token-level processing from document-level reasoning, and caching the GN embeddings to disk, cuts per-document training cost by roughly four orders of magnitude relative to chunked Transformer fine-tuning. We also release a synthetic long-document retrieval benchmark for contrastive training and evaluation at long context lengths. Across an in-distribution Wikipedia benchmark, the LoCo out-of-distribution suite, and a real-world patent retrieval case study, REIGN matches dense long-context retrievers at smaller parameter budgets in each regime. A paired significance test puts it on par with models 1.6-4.3x larger on the patent task, and it stays within 0.65 nDCG@10 of a 20x-larger model on LoCo.

</details>


### [99] [CoVA-SFT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Chain of Visual Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28958)
*Tsung-Han Wu,Heekyung Lee,Anya Ji,Haoming Chen,Trevor Darrell,Joseph E. Gonzalez,David M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CoVA-SFT数据集和CoVA-Bench基准，通过多模态推理步骤训练模型在文本推理中构建视觉工作区，显著提升性能但仍不及纯文本CoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决纯文本思维链（CoT）在处理视觉问题时需将视觉信息序列化为 awkward prose 的局限性，填补缺乏大规模、多步、自纠正的多模态推理数据集以教导模型建立内部视觉工作区的空白。

Method: 构建包含51.9K样本、222K+多模态推理步骤的CoVA-SFT结构化语料库，涵盖5种布局家族和17项复杂任务；同时发布含1,700个测试样本的CoVA-Bench基准。通过显式推理公式、代理渲染和验证循环，指导多模态语言模型交错处理文本与视觉抽象。

Result: 在CoVA-Bench上，基于CoVA-SFT微调的模型表现优于所有交错式CoT基线模型2倍以上，但仍未达到强纯文本CoT基线的水平。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了多模态模型在视觉相关推理任务中的能力，揭示了当前多模态推理相比纯文本推理仍存在的差距，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has dramatically improved large language models (LLMs) by allowing them to decompose problems into intermediate steps. While CoT is widely effective for linguistic tasks, text-only CoT forces models to serialize visual problems into awkward prose. Although architectural solutions exist to process visual inputs, the community lacks a massive, multi-step, self-corrected dataset to teach models how to build and maintain internal visual workspaces when solving purely textual reasoning problems. To address this limitation, we introduce CoVA-SFT, a highly structured corpus of 51.9K samples containing over 222K multimodal reasoning steps across 5 distinct layout families and 17 complex tasks, and CoVA-Bench, a companion benchmark of 1,700 held-out test samples spanning the same tasks for reproducible evaluation. By providing explicit rationale formulations, agentic renderings, and verification loops, CoVA-SFT teaches multimodal language models to interleave text and visual abstractions. We validate the dataset by demonstrating that models fine-tuned on CoVA-SFT outperform all interleaved CoT baselines by more than 2x on average on CoVA-Bench, though they still fall short of strong text-only CoT baselines, highlighting open challenges for future work.

</details>


### [100] [The Illusion of Replacement: Rethinking Specialized Machine Learning Models in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28980)
*Kiyan Rezaee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 尽管语言模型在特定任务中表现优异，但现有证据并未支持其完全取代针对结构化数据的专用架构。当结构计算至关重要时，专门化设计往往以其他形式（如图模块、结构令牌）重新引入，而非消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨传统用于结构化数据的机器学习专用架构是否可被基于语言的模型完全替代，并评估预测精度与结构表示/计算之间的关系。

Method: 审查2016-2026年间涵盖九种模态的159篇论文，将现有方法归纳为从纯语言系统到完全专用架构的八个表征体制，重点分析结构保留与计算能力。

Result: 语言中介模型在极端少样本预测、离散符号任务等特定场景下具有竞争力；但在直接评估结构表示或计算时，未发现通用架构替代的证据。常见模式是：当纯语言不足时，缺失的结构通过非语言组件（如图模块）重新引入。

Conclusion: 专业化设计更多是“转移”而非“消失”。虽然扩展规模能提升语言模型性能，但其能否最终消除与结构感知架构之间的差距尚未经过验证。

Abstract: Can the specialized architectures that machine learning has traditionally built for structured data be replaced by language-based models? This question is examined through a review of 159 papers (2016--2026) across nine modalities, with predictive accuracy considered alongside structural representation and computation. A distinction is made between performing a task and preserving and computing the structure that makes the task tractable, and existing approaches are organized into eight representational regimes, ranging from language-only systems to fully specialized architectures. Language-mediated models are found to be highly competitive in specific settings, including extreme few-shot prediction, discretized symbolic tasks, textually annotated knowledge graphs, and large-scale single-modality pretraining. However, whenever structural representation or computation is directly evaluated rather than accuracy alone, no evidence of general architectural replacement is found. Instead, a recurring pattern is observed across independent research communities: when language alone is insufficient, the missing structure is reintroduced through a graph module, structural tokens, specialized attention, or another non-linguistic component. In this sense, specialization more often relocates than disappears. Moreover, although performance of language-based models is improved by scaling, whether the gap to a structure-aware architecture can eventually be eliminated remains untested.

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Polarization: The Generative Constraint of Chain-of-Thought in Pointwise Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30398)
*Xiaoyang Chen,Jie Liu,Haijin Liang,Haibo Shi,Jin Ma,Ben He,Yingfei Sun,Dezhi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在逐点文档重排序中，思维链（CoT）模型相比直接评分模型表现较差。尽管通过强化学习、细粒度监督和架构解耦等干预措施提升了分类准确性和绝对分数，但相对排名差距依然存在。这表明该瓶颈源于连续相关性语义通过离散文本路由时导致的排名信号分辨率受限，而非简单的训练偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为何在逐点文档重排序任务中，思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）模型通常表现不如直接评分模型，并验证这种性能差距是否可以通过针对性的训练策略来弥补。

Method: 1. 进行实证研究，确认性能差距在高达320亿参数的模型规模下保持稳定，排除模型和数据容量混淆因素。
2. 应用压力测试，结合强化学习、细粒度监督和架构解离等技术，试图修复分类、分数极化或校准崩溃等问题。

Result: 各项干预措施虽然提高了分类准确率和绝对分数，但CoT模型与直接评分模型之间的相对排名差距依然持续存在，且该现象具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 在逐点评分范式下，将连续的相关性语义通过离散文本进行路由会限制排名信号的分辨率。这是一个稳定且难以通过当前标准方法克服的根本性瓶颈，而非容易解决的训练偏差。

Abstract: In pointwise document reranking, Chain-of-Thought models typically underperform direct scoring models. While existing diagnostics attribute this to inferior classification, score polarization, or calibration breakdown, whether targeted training can bridge this gap remains unclear. Our empirical study first confirms that this gap is stable across scales up to 32B parameters, ruling out model and data capacity confounders. We then apply stress tests utilizing reinforcement learning, fine-grained supervision, and architectural decoupling to explicitly repair these deviations. Although these interventions improve classification accuracy and absolute scores, the relative ranking gap persists. These findings suggest that, within the pointwise scoring paradigm, routing continuous relevance semantics through discrete text constrains ranking signal resolution, revealing a bottleneck that is stable and difficult to overcome under current standard methods, rather than an easily resolvable training bias.

</details>


### [102] [Detecting and Guiding LLM-Generated Korean Poetry with Interpretable Form-level Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28986)
*Keunhyeung Park,Seunguk Yu,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过量化四个形式级语言维度（输出长度、行尾形式多样性、行长不规则性、正字法规范性）来检测韩国诗歌的人类与LLM作者差异，并指导LLM生成更贴近人类风格的作品。基于五个可解释特征的逻辑回归分类器在零样本跨分布检测中取得83.60的AUC-ROC，优于最强基线；专家评估显示特征引导生成的诗歌更受青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理现代韩国诗歌时往往表现不佳，产出类似“断句散文”的输出。旨在解决两个耦合任务：检测韩国诗歌是由人类还是LLM创作，以及引导LLM生成形式上更接近人类写作的诗歌。

Method: 1. 定义四个形式级语言维度：输出量（Volume）、结构变异性（Structure Variation）、节奏不规则性（Rhythmic Irregularity）和规范性遵从度（Normative Adherence）。2. 将上述维度操作化为五个可解释的特征。3. 使用基于这五个特征逻辑回归分类器进行诗歌作者身份检测。4. 利用这些特征引导LLM（如GPT-5.2, Gemini-3）生成诗歌，并通过专家评估和分布分析验证效果。

Result: 1. 检测方面：在七个未见过的LLM上进行零样本跨分布检测，该方法平均AUC-ROC为83.60，相比最强基线KatFishNet（75.84）提升了7.76个绝对点（相对提升10.23%）。2. 生成方面：专家评估偏好特征引导生成的诗歌；对GPT-5.2和Gemini-3的分析表明，针对长度、节奏和结尾统计数据的引导使生成内容向人类分布靠拢。

Conclusion: 可解释的、特定于语言的特征能够有效桥接LLM生成诗歌的诊断与引导过程，改善其形式质量并提高检测准确性。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with modern Korean poetry, producing outputs that resemble "line-broken prose." We address two coupled tasks: detecting whether a Korean poem is human- or LLM-authored, and guiding LLMs to generate poetry closer in form to human writing. We quantify the human-LLM gap along four form-level linguistic dimensions: output length (Volume), the diversity and connective use of line-final forms (Structure Variation), the irregularity of line lengths (Rhythmic Irregularity), and adherence to standard orthography (Normative Adherence). We operationalize these dimensions as five interpretable features. For detection, a logistic regression classifier over these five features attains an average AUC-ROC of 83.60 in zero-shot out-of-distribution detection across seven unseen LLMs, versus 75.84 for the strongest baseline in our comparison, KatFishNet, an absolute gain of 7.76 AUC points and a 10.23% relative improvement; one generator-specific punctuation pattern outside our taxonomy remains a boundary case. For generation, expert evaluation on GPT-5.2 prefers feature-guided poems over the unconstrained baseline, and analyses across GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3 show that targeted length, rhythm, and ending statistics move toward the human distribution. These results suggest that interpretable, language-specific features can bridge the diagnosis and guidance of LLM-generated poetry.

</details>


### [103] [A Unifying Perspective on Language Model Representations: From Filler-Role Structure to Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29034)
*Zhang Enyan,R. Thomas McCoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用张量积表示（TPRs）作为统一假设，旨在弥合现有语言模型可解释性方法之间的孤立状态。通过数学推导和实证研究，证明TPR能统一加法类比、线性探测、稀疏自编码器和激活补丁等主流方法，为理解神经网络内部结构提供统一视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型可解释性方法及其产生的见解处于相对孤立的状态，缺乏一个统一的底层结构来解释为何这些方法都能有效揭示模型内部运作机制。

Method: 提出使用张量积表示（TPRs）作为统一假设，将组合结构表示为向量空间中的填充-角色绑定（filler-role bindings）。通过数学推导证明多种可解释性方法可从TPR中衍生，并在从小型玩具模型到大型语言模型（LLMs）的广泛模型上进行实证验证，构建并测试了各方法的衍生变体。

Result: 数学上证明了加法类比、线性探测、稀疏自编码器和激活补丁等方法均可由TPR推导得出；实证上，基于TPR构建的这些方法变体在性能上与标准变体相当，验证了TPR作为统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作迈向了对神经网络本质的统一解释，不仅提供独立的观察结果，更解释了这些观察之间的联系，为可解释性领域提供了更具整合性的理论框架。

Abstract: A wide range of methods have been proposed for interpreting language models, delivering important insights into their inner workings. However, different methods and their resulting insights stand in relative isolation: what could the underlying structure of language models be, such that they give rise to all our interpretations? In this work, we propose using Tensor Product Representations (TPRs) as a unifying hypothesis. TPRs give a concrete proposal for how compositional structure could be represented in vector space --- as filler-role bindings. We show, both mathematically and empirically, that TPRs can unify several prior interpretability methods: additive analogies, linear probing, sparse autoencoders, and activation patching. Mathematically, we show that these methods can all be derived from TPRs. Empirically, we apply the derivations to a range of different models --- from small toy models to LLMs --- to construct instances of each of the above interpretability methods; these constructed variants perform comparably to their standard variants. We view this work as a step toward what interpretability will ideally provide: a unified account of the nature of neural networks, corroborated not just by individual observations but also by an explanation of the connections between them.

</details>


### [104] [Hi-Q: Hierarchical Evidence-guided Query Refinement for Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30468)
*Jueun Kim,Sungho Park,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hi-Q通过证据条件化的分层查询细化框架，解决多跳QA中问题粒度与证据粒度不匹配的问题，无需固定图结构或预定义模板，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答（QA）的核心瓶颈在于问题表达粒度与可检索语料证据粒度之间的不匹配。现有方法（如固定图结构、迭代重写查询或执行生成程序）未能显式判断何时查询单元已得到证据支持，何时需要细化。

Method: 提出Hi-Q框架，将问题建模为“可检索粒度发现”。在每个查询节点使用解析算子测试证据支持度：已解析节点终止，未解析节点通过依赖保持的二元算子扩展，并由语义覆盖验证器检查。生成的查询树拓扑由语料支持信号决定，而非固定分解模板。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准测试的全语料检索设置下，Hi-Q平均达到52.3 EM和64.0 F1，优于IRCoT（+15.1 EM / +18.2 F1）和PropRAG。在受限的支持/干扰项设置下，同样取得最佳准确率（57.9 EM / 69.3 F1），显著超越基线模型。

Conclusion: Hi-Q通过动态构建基于证据支持的查询树，有效解决了多跳QA中的粒度不匹配问题，在多种评估设置下均取得了最先进的性能表现。

Abstract: A central bottleneck in multi-hop Question Answering (QA) is that the granularity at which a question is expressed often differs from the granularity at which corpus evidence is retrievable. Existing methods address this mismatch by imposing fixed graph structures over the corpus, by iteratively reformulating the query, or by executing a generated program over it, but these strategies do not explicitly decide when a query unit is already supported by evidence and when it should be refined. We formulate this bottleneck as retrievable granularity discovery and introduce Hi-Q, an evidence-conditioned framework for hierarchical query refinement. At each query node, a resolution operator tests whether retrieved evidence supports the current query unit; resolved nodes terminate, while unresolved nodes are expanded by a dependency-preserving binary operator and checked by a semantic coverage verifier. Hi-Q therefore grows a query tree whose topology is determined by corpus support signals rather than by a fixed decomposition template or a pre-built graph. We evaluate Hi-Q on three multi-hop QA benchmarks, primarily under full-corpus retrieval, where dependent evidence must be located among open-domain distractors rather than within a small annotated pool. In this setting Hi-Q reaches 52.3 EM and 64.0 F1 averaged over the three benchmarks, ahead of the iterative retrieval baseline IRCoT by 15.1 EM / 18.2 F1 on that same average, and ahead of the graph-based RAG baseline PropRAG by 11.5 EM / 12.0 F1 on MuSiQue-full, without corpus-wide graph construction. In the restricted supporting/distractor setting used by prior work, Hi-Q likewise attains the best accuracy, with 57.9 EM and 69.3 F1 on average, ahead of PropRAG by 5.6 EM / 3.9 F1 and IRCoT by 13.7 EM / 15.8 F1. The project page is available at https://hi-q-project.github.io/.

</details>


### [105] [RouteSparse: Input-Conditional Pattern Routing for Budgeted Long-Context Prefilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29058)
*Chao Zhang,Yifan Ji,Ziyan Zhang,Kai Song,Fei Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RouteSparse，通过为每个注意力头和提示段在稀疏模式库中动态路由，解决MInference等固定稀疏模式方法在不同输入下适应性差的问题。该方法结合低成本探测、延迟感知路由和不确定性回退机制，在Llama 3.1-8B上实现6.5倍预填充加速，且精度损失显著低于固定路由方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态稀疏注意力方法（如MInference）虽高效，但假设单个注意力头的偏好模式和稀疏预算在所有输入中保持不变，忽略了输入条件的差异性，导致在复杂场景下性能受限。

Method: 引入RouteSparse框架：1) 构建少量GPU高效的稀疏模式库；2) 使用低成本探针评估模式效用与不确定性；3) 基于约束风险最小化 formulate 路由决策，推导遗漏概率质量的注意力输出误差证书；4) 对不确定情况采用更密集掩码作为回退策略。

Result: 在Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct的128K-token长上下文任务中，RouteSparse实现6.5倍预填充速度提升，RULER指标仅下降0.2点；相比之下，固定每头路由方案速度提升7.3倍但RULER下降1.6点。消融实验验证了输入条件路由、硬件配置分析和选择性密集回退对质量-延迟权衡的贡献。

Conclusion: 输入条件动态路由结合不确定性管理能有效平衡长上下文处理的推理速度与模型精度，优于静态稀疏模式分配策略。

Abstract: Dynamic sparse attention can reduce the quadratic cost of long-context prefilling without changing model weights. MInference assigns each attention head one pattern offline and estimates that pattern's sparse indices for every prompt. This design is efficient, but it assumes that a head's preferred pattern and sparsity budget remain suitable across inputs. We introduce RouteSparse, which routes each head and prompt segment among a small library of GPU-efficient sparse patterns. A low-cost probe estimates pattern utility and uncertainty; a latency-aware router then selects a pattern and budget, while uncertain cases fall back to a denser mask. We formulate routing as constrained risk minimization, derive an attention-output error certificate from omitted probability mass, and evaluate the method on long-context retrieval, question answering, summarization, and language modeling. On Llama 3.1-8B-Instruct with 128K-token prompts, RouteSparse achieves $6.5\times$ dense prefill speed with a 0.2-point RULER drop relative to dense attention, compared with $7.3\times$ speed and a 1.6-point drop for fixed per-head routing. Ablations confirm that input-conditional routing, hardware profiling, and selective dense fallback each contribute to the quality--latency tradeoff.

</details>


### [106] [ECGQuest: Benchmarking and Fine-Tuning Language Models for Electrocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30893)
*Mohammadsina Hassannia,Matthew A. Reyna,Reza Sameni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ECGQuest基准，用于评估大语言模型在心电图（ECG）上下文知识上的能力。通过微调小型开源模型，使其性能接近大型商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型基准主要评估广博医学知识或单一ECG信号/图像解读，缺乏对ECG解释所需的更广泛上下文知识的评估。

Method: 基于23份ECG参考文献及会议论文，利用GPT-4o生成10,904个真/假问题及其否定形式（共21,808对Q&A）。评估了3个商业和20个开源模型，并对5个7-14B参数的开源模型进行低秩适应（LoRA）微调，以BERT等作为基线。

Result: 零样本准确率在49.5%-74.4%之间，通用模型优于专科模型。微调使所有开源模型准确率提升6.5%-14.1%，其中DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B达76.3%，五模型投票集成达78.5%。微调主要帮助较弱或存在类别偏差的模型。

Conclusion: ECGQuest提供了可复现的ECG上下文知识基准。参数高效微调能使较小的语言模型在与大型商业模型竞争中具备竞争力。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation requires knowledge of cardiology, electrophysiology, clinical diagnosis, ECG waveforms, signal acquisition, and instrumentation. Existing language-model benchmarks, however, primarily assess broad medical knowledge or interpretation of individual ECG signals and images rather than the broader contextual knowledge required for ECG interpretation. We developed ECGQuest, a literature-grounded resource for evaluating and fine-tuning ECG-specific language models. A GPT-4o-based pipeline generated questions from 23 ECG references and Computing in Cardiology proceedings from 2003-2025. The final dataset contains 10,904 unique True/False questions paired with their negated forms (21,808 Q&A pairs). We evaluated three commercial and 20 open-source language models on a held-out test set in a zero-shot setting. Five open-source models with 7-14B parameters were fine-tuned using Low-Rank Adaptation, with BERT and BiomedBERT included as supervised encoder baselines. Generalization was assessed on ECG-related subsets of MedMCQA and MedQA converted to binary True/False questions using official answer keys. Zero-shot accuracy on ECGQuest ranged from 49.5% to 74.4%, with GPT-5 performing best. General-purpose models outperformed medically specialized models, several models showed strong True/False bias, and encoder baselines performed near chance. Fine-tuning improved all open-source models by 6.5-14.1%. Fine-tuned DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-14B reached 76.3% accuracy, while a five-model voting ensemble reached 78.5%. On MedMCQA and MedQA, fine-tuning mainly benefited weaker or class-biased models and did not consistently improve strong base models. ECGQuest provides a reproducible benchmark for contextual ECG knowledge and shows that parameter-efficient fine-tuning can make smaller language models competitive with substantially larger commercial models.

</details>


### [107] [Not All or None: Dynamic Construction of Target-aware Memory Graph for Conversational Stance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29066)
*Yifan Xiang,Bin Liang,Yuqi Huang,Ruifeng Xu,Kam-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TamGraph，一种通过熵引导回溯机制动态构建目标感知图的方法，用于从对话历史中精准提取相关信息以增强大语言模型在会话姿态检测中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决真实社交媒体场景下会话姿态检测的挑战，即如何有效利用跨会话的历史陈述来识别用户立场，同时避免引入无关噪声或处理所有历史对话带来的计算负担。

Method: 提出目标感知记忆图TamGraph，采用逐步的、由熵引导的回溯机制，从历史对话中有选择地激活与目标相关的记忆，并动态构建目标感知图以建模话语间的姿态关系。

Result: 在英语和中文基准测试上的实验结果表明，TamGraph显著提升了大语言模型（LLM）在会话姿态检测任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效利用对话历史中的目标相关信息进行姿态检测，同时防止噪声干扰，证明了其在多语言环境下的有效性。
