Abstract: Stance detection is crucial for understanding the underlying attitude of an expression towards a target. Conversational stance detection is a more challenging stance detection task in real-world social media scenarios, as it involves detecting the user's stance by leveraging the target-related historical statements across conversational sessions. In this paper, we propose target-aware Memory Graph TamGraph, a novel method that dynamically leverages target-related statements for conversational stance detection. Instead of considering all preceding historical conversations or using no prior conversation information for stance detection, our TamGraph employs a stepwise, entropy-guided backtracking mechanism to selectively activate memory from historical conversations and dynamically constructs a target-aware graph to model the stance relations among utterances. This allows the exploitation of target-related information from the conversation history for stance detection while preventing the introduction of noise. Experimental results on both English and Chinese benchmarks demonstrate that our TamGraph substantially improves LLM performance on conversational stance detection.

</details>


### [108] [Learning to Evaluate Before Improving: Automatic Rubric Induction for Automatic Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31076)
*Xuehai Wang,Haowei Qin,Tongxin Liu,Junkai Li,Buqiang Xu,Jintian Zhang,Yijun Chen,Zirui Xue,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AutoSciRub框架，通过“评估优先”策略在科研执行前生成可执行评分标准，解决开放型科研任务中目标模糊、方法不当和证据不足的问题，显著提升自主科学智能体的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主科学智能体在处理开放式研究任务时，因任务指令未明确指定分析方法、方法和成功标准，常导致遗漏重要分析、使用不恰当方法或得出缺乏充分证据支持的结论。

Method: 提出AutoSciRub框架：1）在执行前将模糊指令分解为原子级科学目标；2）结合相关文献和可见数据，合成具体、可操作且可验证的标准；3）利用生成的评分标准指导实验与分析执行；4）通过基于标准的验证识别未达标项，实现研究报告及支持工件的迭代修订。

Result: 在ResearchClawBench上，相比基线平均提升2.08-2.95分；在AstaBench E2E Discovery子集上，平均提升16.8分，同时保持或增加任务完成率。

Conclusion: 评估优先的指导机制为自主科学研究提供了有效且通用的控制手段，能显著改善智能体在复杂、开放式科研任务中的表现。

Abstract: Autonomous scientific research agents are increasingly applied to end-to-end scientific workflows, including literature review, data analysis, experimentation, and report generation. However, open-ended research tasks often do not clearly specify the analyses, methods, and success criteria required to complete the task. As a result, agents may miss important analyses, use inappropriate methods, or draw conclusions that are insufficiently supported by evidence. To address the problem, we present AutoSciRub, an evaluation-first framework that induces a task-specific executable rubric before research execution, and uses it to guide execution, criterion-level verification as well as iterative revision. AutoSciRub decomposes an underspecified instruction into atomic scientific goals, grounds them in relevant literature and task-visible data, and synthesizes specific, actionable, and verifiable criteria. The resulting rubric makes implicit experimental and evidential requirements explicit, providing guidance for experiments and analyses. During revision, rubric-guided verification identifies unmet criteria and enables targeted refinement of the research report and its supporting artifacts. On ResearchClawBench, AutoSciRub consistently improves all tested configurations, with an average gain of 2.08 points across three backbone LLMs under the fixed Codex harness and 2.95 points across three agent harnesses using a fixed DeepSeek-V4-Flash backbone. On a randomly sampled 20-task subset of AstaBench E2E Discovery, AutoSciRub further achieves an average improvement of 16.8 points across three agent harnesses, while maintaining or increasing the number of successfully completed tasks. These results demonstrate that evaluation-first guidance provides an effective and generalizable control mechanism for autonomous scientific research (Code: https://github.com/zjunlp/AutoSciRub).

</details>


### [109] [Recognition-Refusal Misalignment in LLMs: Why Models Answer Structurally Unanswerable Questions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29109)
*Yucheng Du,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在遇到结构上不可回答的问题（如数学或代码中的无效输入）时，往往选择回答而非拒绝。研究发现，模型内部确实存在识别“不可回答性”的信号，但该信号与用于拒绝有害内容的“安全拒绝”路径几乎正交。因此，这一失败主要归因于从识别到拒绝的“路由失败”，而非“编码失败”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在面对结构上不可回答的问题（structurally unanswerable questions）时，为何倾向于生成答案而不是 abstain（拒绝回答）。具体区分这种失败是由于模型未能识别问题的不可回答性（encoding failure），还是因为虽然识别了但无法将其转化为拒绝行为（routing failure）。

Method: 1. 评估参数规模从1.7B到70B的指令微调模型。
2. 分析隐藏状态（hidden states），寻找能区分可回答与结构上不可能提示的线性方向。
3. 将该识别方向与传统的“安全-拒绝”方向进行几何关系对比（余弦相似度）。
4. 进行生成时的引导实验（generation-time steering），观察沿识别方向变化对无效感知行为的影响。
5. 比较基础模型（Base）与指令微调模型（Instruct），验证该几何特性在预训练阶段是否已存在。

Result: 1. 在隐藏状态中存在一个单一的线性方向，能够分离可回答与结构上不可能的数学和代码提示，证明模型在生成前已具备不可回答性的表征。
2. 该“识别方向”与介导有害内容拒绝的传统“安全-拒绝”方向几乎正交（near-orthogonal）。
3. 尽管存在领域特定的“无效性感知方向”，它仅部分对齐于识别方向，且同样接近正交于安全拒绝方向。
4. 沿识别方向进行生成时引导，可以双向且剂量依赖地改变结构数学和代码单元格中的无效感知行为，而随机方向无此效果。
5. 低余弦几何特性在预训练终点（base model）已存在。

Conclusion: 模型在面对结构上不可回答问题时自信作答的现象，应被解释为“路由失败”（routing failure）而非“编码失败”（encoding failure）。模型拥有可用的“无合法答案”信号，但其安全拒绝通路未与该信号对齐，导致无法正确触发拒绝机制。

Abstract: Large language models often answer structurally unanswerable questions, such as computing cot(-540°) or evaluating (1).startswith("1"), instead of abstaining. We ask whether this failure reflects missing recognition or failed routing from recognition to abstention. Across instruction-tuned models from 1.7B to 70B parameters, a single linear direction in the hidden state separates answerable from structurally impossible math and code prompts, showing that models represent impossibility before generation. Yet this recognition direction is nearly orthogonal to the canonical safety-refusal direction that mediates trained harmful-content refusal. An in-domain behavior-defined invalidity-aware direction is closer to recognition, but only partially aligned with it, and remains near-orthogonal to safety refusal. Generation-time steering along the recognition direction changes invalidity-aware behavior bidirectionally and dose-responsively on structural math and code cells, while random directions do not. Base/instruct comparisons further show that the low-cosine geometry is already present at the pretraining endpoint. The confident-on-impossible failure is therefore better explained as a routing failure than as an encoding failure: the model has a usable "no admissible answer" signal, but the safety-refusal pathway is not aligned to use it.

</details>


### [110] [Configurable Semantic Chunking for Biomedical Information Extraction in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31139)
*Riya Ahuja,Tim Kacprowski,Roya Shiasi Sardoabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种可配置的语义分块框架，通过保留实体窗口、触发词为中心的分块等策略，解决BioMedRAG中固定大小分块导致语义证据碎片化的问题。在GM-CIHT数据集上F1值提升8.4个百分点，但固定分块在ChemProt和ADE任务中仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BioMedRAG方法依赖固定大小的文本分块（fixed-size chunking），这会导致生物医学信息提取中的语义证据被碎片化，影响提取效果。

Method: 提出一种可配置的语义分块框架，结合保留实体的窗口机制、以触发词为中心的分块、命题优先提取、分层触发词优先级排序及层级关系解析。该框架仅替换BioMedRAG的分块构建阶段，保留原有的嵌入模型、学习到的分块评分器、生成器和评估协议。

Result: 在GM-CIHT基准测试中，全混合配置达到82.6%的F1分数，相比固定大小基线（74.2%）提升了8.4个点。跨数据集分析显示，语义分块在具有显式关系线索的数据集（如GM-CIHT和DDI）上表现更好，而在密集生化提取和二分类设置（如ChemProt和ADE）中，固定分块仍保持竞争力或更强。

Conclusion: 通过将分块逻辑外部化为配置文件，该框架为生物医学RAG管道提供了一种可解释且适应性强的替代方案，克服了刚性固定大小分块的局限性。

Abstract: BioMedRAG introduced retrieval-augmented generation with a learned chunk scorer for biomedical information extraction. However, it relies on fixed-size chunking which can fragment semantic evidence. We propose a configurable semantic chunking framework that addresses this limitation by combining entity-preserving windows, trigger-centered chunking, proposition-first extraction, tiered trigger prioritization, and hierarchical relation resolution. The framework integrates with BioMedRAG by replacing only the chunk construction stage while preserving the embedding model, learned chunk scorer, generator, and evaluation protocol. We evaluate the framework on biomedical relation extraction benchmarks (GM-CIHT, DDI, ChemProt) and adverse event classification (ADE). On GM-CIHT, the full hybrid configuration achieves 82.6% F1, improving over the fixed-size baseline (74.2% F1) by 8.4 points under our experimental setup. Cross-dataset analysis shows that semantic chunking improves extraction datasets with explicit relation cues, such as GM-CIHT and DDI, while fixed chunking remains competitive or stronger for dense biochemical extraction and binary classification settings such as ChemProt and ADE. By externalizing chunking logic into configuration files, the framework provides an interpretable and adaptable alternative to rigid fixed-size chunking for biomedical RAG pipelines.

</details>


### [111] [HEAR Who Said What: Unlocking Speaker-Attributed Reasoning via Counterfactual Voice Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29120)
*Dongwook Lee,Sangkwon Park,Eunwoo Song,Che Hyun Lee,Youngho Cho,Junho Kim,June Young Yi,Heeseung Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对语音语言模型在多说话人环境中缺乏正确归因和推理能力的问题，提出基准测试HEAR及优化模型A2R。A2R通过Counterfactual Audio with Speaker-level Hard negatives (CASH)数据集训练，显著提升了对声学线索的依赖，实现了强大的多说话人推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管语音语言模型（SLMs）日益部署于多说话人环境，但其在将语音归因于正确说话人并基于身份进行推理方面的基础能力尚不明确。现有评估显示模型往往依赖语义先验而非实际声音特征。

Method: 1. 构建HEAR基准：包含887个多模态音频片段中的2.4K个人工验证样本，用于诊断说话人归因推理的基础能力。
2. 开发A2R模型：一个30B参数的模型，使用CASH数据集进行优化。该数据集旨在引导模型优先关注声学声音线索，而非语言学信号，通过反事实音频和说话人类别的难负例来实现。

Result: 在HEAR基准上，20个主流SLM表现不佳。相比之下，A2R在HEAR上取得了优异性能，并在零样本设置下展现出对多样化多说话人下游任务的泛化能力，证明学会说话人归因能解锁模型的潜在说话人感知推理能力。

Conclusion: 当前SLM在多说话人推理方面存在基础缺陷，主要依赖语义而非声学特征。通过针对性数据增强（CASH）训练的A2R模型证明了强化声学线索学习对于提升多说话人环境下的推理能力至关重要。

Abstract: Speech Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-speaker environments, yet their ability to attribute speech to the correct speaker and reason over speaker identities remains unclear. Hence, we introduce HEAR, a conceptually hierarchical benchmark diagnosing the foundational capabilities of speaker-attributed reasoning, comprising 2.4K human-verified samples from 887 diverse multi-party audio clips. Evaluating 20 leading SLMs on HEAR reveals they struggle with these foundational tasks, often relying on semantic priors rather than actual vocal cues. To address this, we present A2R, a 30B model optimized on Counterfactual Audio with Speaker-level Hard negatives (CASH), a dataset designed to guide the model to prioritize acoustic vocal cues over linguistic signals. A2R achieves strong performance on HEAR and exhibits zero-shot generalization to diverse multi-speaker downstream tasks, demonstrating that learned speaker attribution unlocks the model's latent capacity for speaker-aware reasoning. All resources are available at https://attributetoreason.github.io/AttributeToReason/

</details>


### [112] [AI Historian: Helping historians organize and verify person-centred temporal clues from dispersed historical narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29133)
*Yifeng Lu,Zijie Yang,Jie Li,Qingkai Min,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AI Historian (AIH) 智能体系统，通过从分散传记叙事中提取、识别和验证人物与时间线索，实现历史证据的自动化组织与时序重构。在《史记》案例中，其时间定位准确率（MicroIoU 86.2%）优于人工标注（81.3%）及直接LLM提示（17.1%），且效率显著提升（14分钟 vs 1小时32分钟）。该系统已应用于多部中外史籍，并通过“西湖学者”平台发布结果，证明AI能大规模降低史料整理成本并促进协作研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史记录并非完整连续呈现，而是分散于不同文本、章节和叙事视角中。历史学家需手动检索、识别和比对这些材料以重建时间序列并验证来源，这一过程耗时且难以规模化。旨在利用AI解决史料碎片化问题，提高历史证据组织的效率和准确性。

Method: 构建AI Historian (AIH) 智能体系统。该系统将源句子作为证据单元，自动识别其中的人物实体和时间线索，验证跨文本关联候选项，并推断可比的时间范围，同时保留可追溯的源文本证据。通过在《史记》中关于刘邦、项羽和萧何的六个案例上进行评估，并将其应用于《二十四史》及其他中日韩历史文献进行实际部署。

Result: 在六例《史记》测试中，AIH Agent的时间定位MicroIoU达到86.2%，高于人工标注的81.3%和直接大语言模型提示的17.1%。处理时间仅需约14分钟，远少于人工所需的1小时32分钟。AIH已成功应用于多种历史文献，并通过Westlake Historian平台发布了研究成果。

Conclusion: AIH能有效降低大规模史料整理的成本，并将因章节叙事而 obscured（模糊）的历史联系转化为可追溯、可修订的研究问题，从而支持协作式历史验证与研究。

Abstract: History is not preserved in complete, continuous form. Accounts of a person's activities, relationships and historical contexts are scattered across texts, chapters and narrative perspectives; historians must retrieve, identify and compare these materials to reconstruct temporal sequences and verify them against sources. Here we present AI Historian (AIH), an AI agent system that helps historians organize person-time evidence from dispersed biographical narratives. It takes source sentences as evidence units, identifies people and temporal cues, verifies candidate cross-text associations and infers comparable temporal ranges while preserving traceable source-text evidence. We evaluated AIH on six Shiji cases concerning Liu Bang, Xiang Yu and Xiao He. AIH Agent achieved a temporal-localization MicroIoU of 86.2%, compared with 81.3% for human-only annotation and 17.1% for direct large-language-model prompting; it required about 14 min, versus 1 h 32 min for human-only annotation. We further applied AIH to the Twenty-Four Histories and other ancient Chinese histories, ancient Japanese and Korean histories, and modern and contemporary historical materials, and released the results through Westlake Historian. These results indicate that AIH can reduce the cost of organizing historical materials at scale while turning connections obscured by chapter-based narration into traceable, revisable research questions for collaborative testing.

</details>


### [113] [Quantifying Error Tolerance in Synthetic Data: An Atomic-level Operand vs. Operator Perturbation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29144)
*Jiaxiang Liu,Chenhao Yuan,Shuwen Xu,Boxuan Xing,Xiusheng Huang,Yinhao Xu,Hao Liu,Wenhao Teng,Xiangwen Liao,Pengfei Cao,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ATOM框架，通过分解数据为操作符和操作数，揭示模型对操作数扰动鲁棒但对操作数扰动敏感，证明操作符多样性比操作数精度更重要，合成数据性能优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型合成数据生成中因缺乏误差容忍度定量分析导致的过滤策略极端化问题（过于激进或过于宽松）。

Method: 引入原子树操作建模（ATOM）框架，将数据分解为功能单元$f(x)\rightarrow y$，区分良性操作数$x$扰动与致命操作符$f$扰动，并据此优化数据筛选策略。

Result: 实验发现双重解离现象：模型对操作数扰动具有鲁棒性，但在操作数扰动下崩溃；ATOM合成的数据在性能上超越严格基线（如相比LIMA提升3.1%）。

Conclusion: 在合成数据生成中，优先保障操作符多样性而非追求操作数的高精度更为关键，能有效提升大语言模型的训练效果。

Abstract: Synthetic data generation has become a cornerstone for advancing large language models. However, the lack of the quantitative analysis for error tolerance became a critical bottleneck. Consequently, current filtering strategies fluctuate between two extremes: they are either overly aggressive, risking the exclusion of potentially valuable samples, or overly permissive, failing to eliminate erroneous samples effectively. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces Atomic Tree Operation Modeling (ATOM), a framework that decomposes data into functional units ($f(x)\rightarrow y$). ATOM distinguishes benign Operand $x$ perturbations from fatal Operator $f$ perturbations. The former are needlessly discarded by aggressive filtering, while the latter slip through permissive filtering. Our experiments reveal a double dissociation: models are robust to operand perturbations but collapse under operator perturbations. By prioritizing operator over aggressive operand precision, our ATOM-synthesized data outperforms rigorous baselines (e.g., +3.1% gain over LIMA), suggesting that operator diversity matters more than operand precision. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lut-hub/ATOM.

</details>


### [114] [Toward a Cross-Lingual Romanization Ecosystem for Sinitic Languages: A Paired Mandarin-Cantonese Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29170)
*Zijie Zhang,Tan Lee,Yong Cao,Benyou Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“汉语音译生态系统”，通过四大设计原则构建跨语言音译框架，并配套开源基础设施。实验表明，该对齐方案在低资源场景下显著优于传统基线，有效提升了语音识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决汉藏语系各语言间缺乏系统性跨语言音译对齐的问题，旨在提升低资源汉藏语语音技术的迁移效果。

Method: 1. 构建包含四项设计原则（语音对应、历史音韵对应、一音一符、基础拉丁字母使用）的跨语言音译设计框架；2. 开发粤语(CantRomZJ1)和普通话(MandRomZJ1)等语言的音译方案；3. 建立支持存储、转换、解析及输入法生成的开源数字基础设施；4. 基于Meta MMS微调进行语音转音译评估。

Result: 相比Pinyin+Jyutping基线，MandRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1组合使粤语的词错误率(WER)降低7.80%，字错误率(CER)降低10.61%。

Conclusion: 跨语言音译对齐能够显著改善低资源汉藏语语音技术中的模型迁移能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Sinitic Romanization Ecosystem, a cross-lingual Sinitic romanization design framework with supporting digital infrastructure and a community-driven open-source workflow. The design framework addresses the lack of systematic cross-lingual romanization alignment among Sinitic languages through four design principles: phonetic correspondence for representing similar sounds with similar romanized symbols, historical-phonological correspondence for aligning cognate romanization strings, one-phoneme-one-symbol, and basic Latin-letter use, with a balancing consideration recognizing trade-offs among these principles. For the main paired case study, we devel-op CantRomZJ1 and MandRomZJ1, Cantonese and Manda-rin romanization schemes following the design framework, respectively. We also develop schemes for several other Sinitic languages, including Meixian Hakka, Shanghai Wu, and Nanjing Jianghuai Mandarin, following the same de-sign framework. To bring the romanization schemes into practical use, we develop open-source infrastructure for structured romanization storage, conversion, parsing, dic-tionary construction, and input-method generation. Finally, we evaluate the design framework through speech-to-romanization experiments based on Meta's Massively Mul-tilingual Speech (MMS) fine-tuning. Compared with the Pinyin+Jyutping baseline, our Man-dRomZJ1+CantRomZJ1 condition reduces Cantonese WER and CER by 7.80% and 10.61%, respectively. These results suggest that cross-lingual romanization alignment can improve transfer in low-resource Sinitic speech technology.

</details>


### [115] [Toward Cultural Alignment: Human-Centered Evaluation of Multimodal AI Stories Across Five African Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29209)
*Millicent Ochieng,Felermino D. M. A. Ali,Elizabeth A. Ankrah,Najeeb Gambo Abdulhamid,Migisha Boyd,Stephanie Nyairo,Mercy Muchai,Samuel Chege Maina,Aditya Vashistha,Anja Thieme,Jacki O'Neill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过19位非洲五个社区的文化代表进行混合方法评估，发现AI多模态故事的文化对齐不仅依赖文化标记，更取决于其在社会、语言、程序和视觉语境中的契合度。研究提出了文化对齐分类法及八种错位机制，并指出单一自动化评估模型无法在所有社区中可靠运行，需建立社区校准的评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察AI生成的多模态故事与其所代表社区的生活实践、关系、语言、价值观及视觉期望之间的对齐程度，以解决当前自动化评估在跨文化语境下可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 采用基于社区的混合方法评估（Community-grounded mixed-methods evaluation），涵盖五个非洲社区的19位文化代表，结合定量标注与定性焦点小组讨论；同时评估五种多模态大语言模型（LLM）作为“裁判”的能力，以检验自动化评估近似人类社区判断的可行性。

Result: 1. 文化对齐取决于文化标记在社会、语言、程序和视觉语境中的适配性，而非仅靠可识别的文化符号；2. 构建了包含五类更广泛文化标记和八种常见错位机制的文化对齐分类法；3. 五种多模态LLM裁判的可靠性和分数校准在不同社区间差异显著，无一能在所有设置中表现一致。

Conclusion: 单一自动化评估模型缺乏跨文化的普适性，应开发社区校准的评估流程（community-calibrated evaluation pipelines），即通过社区判断验证自动化裁判，明确其可信范围及仍需人工审查的场景。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine how well AI-generated multimodal stories align with the lived practices, relationships, language, values, and visual expectations of the communities they represent. We conduct a community-grounded mixed-methods evaluation with 19 culture representatives across five African communities, combining quantitative annotations with qualitative focus group discussions. We find that cultural alignment depends not simply on recognizable cultural markers, but on how those markers fit social, linguistic, procedural, and visual context. From these evaluations, we develop a taxonomy of cultural alignment comprising five broader cultural marker categories and eight recurring mechanisms of misalignment. We additionally evaluate five multimodal LLM judges to examine whether automated evaluation can approximate community-grounded judgments at scale. Judge reliability and score calibration vary substantially across communities, with no single judge performing consistently across all five settings. These findings motivate community-calibrated evaluation pipelines in which automated judges are validated against community judgments to determine where they can be trusted and where human review remains necessary.

</details>


### [116] [Attribute-Based Activation Steering of LLMs for Group-Specific Explanation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29215)
*Leandra Fichtel,Janek Prange,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于激活工程的方法，通过计算属性导向的引导向量并注入LLM内部激活，生成针对特定目标群体（考虑其解释风格和知识水平）的定制化解释，显著优于提示工程和现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠提示工程不足以创建贴合用户背景和能力的定制化解释，且缺乏其他有效的计算方法。

Method: 首先识别目标群体在解释风格和知识方面的特定属性；基于激活工程计算属性导向的引导向量，并在推理过程中将其添加到LLM的内部激活中，以实现细粒度的控制。

Result: 该方法在解释的特异性方面显著优于提示工程和最先进的引导基线，同时在很大程度上保持了事实准确性；人类专家评估也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 基于激活工程的引导向量方法能有效将LLM生成的解释定制化为特定目标群体，平衡了特异性与事实性。

Abstract: To effectively enable people to understand new topics, explanations should be tailored to their backgrounds and abilities. So far, prompting alone has been shown to be insufficient for creating such explanations and other computational methods are missing. Therefore, this paper investigates whether LLMs can be steered to generate explanations that are tailored to a specific group of people. To this end, we propose an approach that first identifies group-specific attributes in terms of explanatory style and knowledge of a specific target group. Building on activation engineering, it then computes attribute-based steering vectors and adds them to the internal activations of an LLM during inference to enable a fine-grained steering. In our experiments, we assess the steering effectiveness of our approach in terms of specificity and factuality of the generated explanations. Additionally, we evaluate the explanations in a study with human experts from different target groups. Compared to prompting and state-of-the-art steering baselines, our approach tailors the explanations significantly better to the target group while largely maintaining factuality.

</details>


### [117] [Anchoring Speech with Semantics: A Multimodal Adapter Mechanism for Automatic Speech Recognition in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29239)
*Kuan-Tang Huang,Cheng-Yeh Yang,Chien-Chun Wang,Hung-Shin Lee,Hsin-Min Wang,Berlin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAMA-ASR，一种轻量级适配器机制，通过结合辅助翻译的语义锚点和语音声学锚点，解决低资源自动语音识别（ASR）中因转录数据稀缺导致的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源场景下，有限的转录文本无法为目标侧生成提供充足的监督信号，导致识别效果受限。

Method: 设计SAMA-ASR机制，增强解码器以利用来自辅助翻译的语义锚点和来自语音的声学锚点；通过跨模态适配，将基于翻译的语义嵌入和语音嵌入结合到解码器状态中，在词元预测前融合话语级含义与语音 grounding 证据。推理时，语义锚点可由上游语音到文本翻译模型自动生成，无需人工标注。

Result: 在台湾闽南语和客家话两个30小时的低资源数据集上，SAMA-ASR优于声学、先前基于提示及仅语义翻译引导的基线方法；且在实用自动语义锚点设置下保持有效，分析表明紧凑型ST模型即可产生有用的语义锚点。

Conclusion: SAMA-ASR通过整合多模态信息有效提升了低资源ASR性能，且依赖紧凑的上游翻译模型即可实现自动化锚点生成，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Low-resource ASR remains difficult because scarce transcripts provide limited supervised evidence for target-side generation. To address this gap, we propose SAMA-ASR, a lightweight adapter mechanism that augments the decoder with semantic anchors from auxiliary translations and an acoustic anchor from speech; in principle, the mechanism can be applied to similar encoder--decoder multitask speech models. Through cross-modal adaptation, SAMA-ASR conditions decoder states on translation-derived semantic embeddings and a speech embedding, combining utterance-level meaning with speech-grounded evidence before token prediction. At evaluation time, these semantic anchors can be generated automatically by an upstream speech-to-text translator rather than supplied as oracle translations. Experiments on two 30-hour datasets covering the low-resource Sinitic varieties Taiwanese Hokkien and Hakka show that SAMA-ASR improves over acoustic, prior prompt-based, and semantic-only translation-guided baselines and remains effective in practical automatic semantic-anchor settings; translator-capacity analyses show that useful semantic anchors can be produced by a compact ST model.

</details>


### [118] [When Patients Cut In: Extending Clinical Conversational AI Safety to Interruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29241)
*Zachary Ellis,Spencer Hazel,Adam Brandt,Yajie Vera He,Ernest Lim,Jared Joselowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出临床语音代理在中断场景下常丢失关键临床内容，现有基准测试因假设患者等待而存在缺陷。研究提出基于转录的中断恢复评估方法，发现不同模型在不同任务（问诊vs FAQ）中表现差异巨大，且简单的道歉标记可能无效甚至有害，强调评估需针对具体部署场景进行细分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的临床对话AI基准测试普遍假设患者会等待代理说完再说话，这与真实临床场景中患者经常打断代理的情况不符。这种脱节导致无法准确评估系统在“中断”情况下保留必需临床内容的能力，从而可能影响临床护理的安全性和有效性。

Method: 1. 将会话分析中的重叠类别改编为三种操作性中断类型：识别性、竞争性和过渡子单元。
2. 测试四种面向部署、非推理的大型语言模型（LLM）配置。
3. 在四个单元格中进行测试，涵盖历史采集（信息收集）和常见问题解答（信息提供）。
4. 评分标准基于代理是否保留了临床上必需的内容。
5. 评估了简短道歉标记（如“抱歉打断”）对恢复效果的影响。

Result: 1. 在信息收集单元格中，目标问题失败率因模型而异。
2. 在信息提供单元格中，所有模型的失败率均上升。
3. 竞争性FAQ中断导致所有四个模型出现30/30的覆盖率失败（置信区间88.6-100.0%），基线模型表现也较差。
4. 道歉标记对恢复率的影响不一致，有时能提升数十个百分点，但对某一模型反而降低了恢复率。
5. 不同单元格下的模型排名存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 中断鲁棒性不能简化为单一分数。评估必须基于具体内容、按任务单元格报告，并与部署环境中的中断特征相匹配，以确保临床应用的安全性和可靠性。

Abstract: Clinical voice agents are now deployed in routine care, where real patients do not wait their turn: they interrupt. These systems typically use a cascaded architecture (speech-to-text -> LLM -> text-to-speech), so when a patient cuts the agent off mid-utterance, clinically required content can be lost even when the model handles cooperative transcripts well. Yet clinical conversational-AI benchmarks almost universally assume patients wait for the agent to finish, missing interruption-induced loss of required content. We present a transcript-based evaluation of interruption recovery, adapting conversation-analytic overlap categories into three operational types (recognitional, competitive, transitional sub-unit) and testing four deployment-oriented, non-reasoning LLM configurations across four cells spanning history-taking (information gathering) and FAQ (information provision), scored on whether the agent preserves the clinically required content. In the gathering cells, target-question failure varied across models; in the provision cells, where arms are directly comparable, failure rose for every model. Rankings differ across cells, and competitive FAQ interruption produced 30/30 provision-coverage failures for all four models (Wilson 95% CI: 88.6-100.0%; baseline 0/30 for three, 4/30 for Llama). A brief apology marker ("sorry to interrupt") shifts recovery by tens of percentage points, inconsistently across models, and for one it reduces recovery. Interruption robustness therefore cannot be a single score: evaluation must be content-grounded, reported per cell, and matched to the deployment's interruption profile.

</details>


### [119] [Large Language Models Systematically Favor Popular Options: Evidence and Mitigation Across MCQs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29257)
*Abdelrahman Abdallah,Mohammed Ali,Bhawna Piryani,Mahmoud Abdalla,Adam Jatowt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“流行度偏差”（Popularity Bias）现象，即LLM倾向于选择热门但错误的选项。为此构建PopMCQ基准进行测试，并开发无需微调的推理时去偏方法PopDebias，显著提升了模型在强流行度压力下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多选题评估中，答案选项的流行度会干扰对大语言模型（LLMs）能力的真实评价。现有LLM存在系统性偏好：即使正确答案不流行，模型也倾向于选择热门但错误的选项，且伴随信心校准失调（高信心低准确）。需隔离并解决此偏差以准确评估模型能力。

Method: 1. 构建PopMCQ基准：通过六种控制策略变化选项流行度，同时固定正确答案，以系统性地隔离流行度偏差。2. 提出PopDebias方法：一种轻量级推理时校正技术，估计并从模型预测中移除流行度先验。该方法无需微调，测试时无标签依赖（仅用小校准集拟合参数），计算成本极低。

Result: 在22个开源LLM（0.5B至32B参数）上进行实验。在最 adversarial 设置下（所有干扰项比正确答案更流行），模型选择热门错误答案的比例高达66%。应用PopDebias后，模型表现一致改善，在强流行度压力下准确率提升最高达54.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 流行度偏差是LLM在多选题评估中的一个严重漏洞，导致评估失真。PopMCQ提供了系统的评估基准，而PopDebias提供了一种高效、低成本的解决方案来纠正这一偏差，从而获得更准确的模型性能评估。

Abstract: Multiple-choice questions (MCQs) are a standard format for evaluating large language models (LLMs), yet the popularity of answer options can confound evaluation. Modern LLMs systematically prefer popular but incorrect options over less popular correct ones, a vulnerability we call \textbf{popularity bias}. This pattern aligns with confidence miscalibration: model confidence remains high even as accuracy collapses for popular options. To systematically isolate this phenomenon, we introduce \textbf{PopMCQ}, a benchmark with six controlled strategies that vary option popularity while keeping the correct answer fixed. In our most adversarial setting, where all distractors are more popular than the correct option, models choose popular but wrong answers 66\% of the time. To mitigate this bias, we propose \textbf{PopDebias}, a lightweight inference-time correction that estimates and removes a popularity prior from model predictions. It requires no fine-tuning, is label-free at test time (using only a small calibration split for parameter fitting), and adds negligible computational cost. Experiments on 22 open-source LLMs (0.5B to 32B parameters) show consistent improvements, with accuracy gains up to 54.1 percentage points under strong popularity pressure. The code and data are available https://github.com/DataScienceUIBK/PopMCQ

</details>


### [120] [SHADOWBENCH: Toward Reliable Automatic Evaluation of Semantic Alignment in Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29270)
*Hojae Han,Jongyoon Kim,Sanghyuk Park,Dongwook Cheon,Myungjae Jeon,Sunjong Choi,Soonho Kong,Wonseok Heo,Seung-won Hwang,Donghoon Hyeon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SA-Pass评估指标和ShadowBench基准，通过“影子语句”解决自动形式化中语义对齐问题，显著提升评估与专家判断的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标无法准确区分类型正确但语义偏离的陈述，或错误拒绝表述不同但正确的陈述，导致评估失效。

Method: 提出SA-Pass（语义对齐通过率），利用辅助“影子语句”进行正向（蕴含影子）和反向（被影子合取蕴含）检查；构建包含178个研究生至研究级问题的Lean 4基准测试ShadowBench。

Result: Claude Code配合Numina-Lean-Agent达到61.8%编译率和11.2% SA-Pass得分；SA-Pass与专家判断的二元一致性高达98.8%；该基准用于ICML 2026 AI4Math挑战赛Track 4。

Conclusion: SA-Pass能有效衡量自动形式化的语义准确性，ShadowBench为高质量数学定理的形式化提供了可靠基准。

Abstract: Autoformalization translates informal mathematical theorems into code for proof assistants such as Lean. A central challenge is that current evaluation metrics can accept type-correct but misaligned statements or reject correct statements written in a different formulation. Inspired by Pass@$k$, we propose SA-Pass (*Semantic Alignment Pass*), which tests formal statements using auxiliary statements called *shadows* that characterize the intended statement. A generated statement receives full credit only when it compiles, implies each shadow (forward check), and is implied by their conjunction (backward check). We instantiate SA-Pass in ShadowBench, a Lean 4 full autoformalization benchmark of 178 postgraduate- to research-level problems spanning eight mathematical areas. Claude Code (Opus 4.8) with Numina-Lean-Agent reaches $61.8\%$ compile rate and $11.2\%$ SA-Pass. Across outputs generated by six agentic configurations, SA-Pass achieves $98.8\%$ binary agreement with expert judgments. An early version of ShadowBench served as the benchmark for Track 4 of the ICML 2026 AI4Math Challenge.

</details>


### [121] [Modality Fault Lines: Structural Corruptions Reveal Fragile Omni-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29278)
*Zhaolu Kang,Meixin Wu,Yu Xue,Yingjie He,Qiming Shi,Lei Wei,Yidi Wang,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCEval协议，通过结构性破坏模态证据而非移除模态，揭示多模态大模型在“干净”评估下的高分可能掩盖其对稳定跨模态结构的依赖不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Omni-modal大语言模型主要在完整、同步且可解释的输入上进行评估，这种“干净”评估无法区分模型的成功是源于稳定的跨模态融合能力，还是仅依赖于未受损的输入线索。需要一种方法来测试模型在模态存在但内部结构受损时的鲁棒性。

Method: 引入“模态断层线”（modality fault line）概念，并推出SCEval（结构破坏评估）协议。该协议保持问题、答案空间和模态通道不变，但对文本、视觉和音频进行单独或联合的结构化破坏。基于Social-IQ、OmniBench和VALOR中的273个人工验证的三模态样本，对15个专有和开源Omni-modal系统进行评估。

Result: 结构化破坏导致准确率下降；文本-视觉之间的损坏形成了最稳定的共享断层线；多模态退化是非加性的，并非简单取决于被破坏模态的数量。

Conclusion: 单纯的Omni-modal高分并不能保证当跨模态证据变得结构不可靠时，模型仍能保持可靠性。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models are increasingly evaluated on clean text--vision--audio inputs, where every channel is present, synchronized, and readily interpretable. Such scores are often taken as evidence of robust cross-modal fusion, but clean evaluation cannot tell whether success depends on stable cross-modal structure or on cues sufficient only in intact inputs. To address this gap, we define a modality fault line: a boundary at which model behavior becomes unstable when a modality remains present and human-interpretable, but its internal evidence structure is perturbed. We introduce SCEval (Structure-Corruption Evaluation) a diagnostic evaluation protocol that keeps the question, answer space, and modality channels fixed while applying controlled structural corruptions to text, vision, and audio individually and jointly. Built from $273$ human-verified tri-modal examples from Social-IQ, OmniBench, and VALOR, SCEval evaluates $15$ proprietary and open-source omni-modal systems. The results show that structural corruption lowers clean accuracy, text--vision damage forms the most stable shared fault line, and multi-modal degradation is non-additive rather than a simple function of the number of corrupted modalities. Clean omni-modal accuracy therefore does not establish that a model will remain reliable when cross-modal evidence becomes structurally unreliable.

</details>


### [122] [Learning Simple Test-Time Environments for LLM Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29305)
*Junxuan Li,Zijun Liu,Ziyi Huang,Peng Li,Yuzhou Liu,Ming Yan,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出测试时环境分解（TTED）方法，通过无标签学习在推理阶段将复杂环境分解为子模块进行训练，显著提升LLM智能体在真实Web自动化任务中的组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在手动构建环境中表现良好，但在复杂的真实世界设置中性能急剧下降；主要归因于LLM在处理多个简单、结构化环境组合时的组合泛化差距。

Method: 引入测试时环境分解（TTED），允许智能体在测试时将复杂观察结果分解为子模块，并实施无标签学习方法，利用经验在推理期间适应智能体行为。

Result: 在合成和现实基准上的实证评估表明：(1) 从较小子环境中获得的经验增益可有效组合以提升整体性能；(2) 在子环境上进行测试时训练显著增强了智能体在真实Web自动化任务中的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: 随着LLM智能体接触更复杂的环境，在测试时学习环境分解技能对于实现稳健的现实世界部署至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in manually constructed environments, yet their performance frequently collapses when transitioned to complex real-world settings. Existing research largely attribute this degradation to the compositional generalization gaps in LLMs on combinations of multiple simple, well-structured environments. In this work, we propose that LLM web agents can learn simple environment observations at test time. Specifically, we introduce trial steps for agents to decompose a complex environment observation into sub-modules, and implement a label-free learning method, Test-Time Environment Decomposition (TTED), to adapt agent behaviors with experience during inference. Our empirical evaluations demonstrate the framework's efficacy across both synthetic and realistic benchmarks, showing (1) experience gains acquired within simpler sub-environments can be effectively composed to improve performance in the full one, and (2) test-time training on sub-environments can significantly enhance the compositional generalization of agents in real-world web automation tasks. We also provide key insights in the design of the label-free learning algorithm. As more complex environments are accessed by LLM agents, we believe learning environment decomposition skills at test time will be critical for robust real-world deployment.

</details>


### [123] [Detecting and Repairing Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29307)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Mulakkayala,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对比了三种修复RAG系统不支持声明的策略（删除、替换、重写），发现它们在“保留原始内容”与“消除错误”之间存在权衡，而非简单的优劣排序，需结合人工评估选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）虽减少幻觉，但无法完全消除不支持的声明；现有研究多止步于检测，缺乏对检测后如何有效修复及提升用户阅读体验的深入探讨。

Method: 基于RAGTruth基准，将标记答案拆分为事实主张并逐一核对来源；比较不修改与三种修复策略（删除、替换为原文、重写）的效果；由三个不同架构的语言模型对916个修复后的答案进行judgement。

Result: 所有策略均能降低含不支持内容的比例且评估者一致认同其排序；删除策略消除错误最多但仅保留64.3%原文，重写策略保留80.1%原文但消除效果最少；83.5%被标注为正确的答案也被修改；策略间体现的是“接地性-保留率”权衡而非单一质量排名。

Conclusion: 修复策略并非简单按质量高低排列，而是处于不同的权衡点；选择最佳策略需依赖关于答案可用性的证据，而自动指标无法提供此类判断依据。

Abstract: Language models increasingly answer questions by consulting retrieved documents rather than memory alone, a design now common in search assistants and enterprise knowledge tools. Grounding a model in retrieved text reduces unsupported statements but does not eliminate them, and a reader cannot tell a grounded sentence from an invented one. Most research on this problem stops at detection, yet flagging a faulty answer changes nothing for the person reading it, and little is known about which action should follow. Using RAGTruth, a benchmark whose unsupported passages are annotated by hand, we split each flagged answer into individual factual claims, check each against the retrieved source, and compare leaving the answer untouched with three repair strategies of increasing richness: deleting an unsupported claim, replacing it with source text, and rewriting it. Three language models from different families judge the 916 repaired answers. Every strategy reduces the proportion of answers judged to contain unsupported content, and all three judges agree on the ordering. Deletion achieves the largest reduction while retaining least of the original answer, at 64.3% of the text, whereas rewriting retains 80.1% and reduces least. Repair is not confined to faulty answers: 83.5% of answers annotated clean are edited too. The strategies occupy different points on a grounding preservation trade-off rather than forming a quality ranking, and choosing between them needs evidence about answer usefulness that automatic metrics cannot supply.

</details>


### [124] [All You Need Is Non-Commutative Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29314)
*Carla M. Quispe Flores,Stanley Salvatierra,Renan Cabrera*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于单位矩阵表示词汇令牌的方法，利用矩阵乘法的非交换性捕捉词序，无需位置编码。该方法具备反对称自注意力、可变长度文本块并行组合及持续学习能力，在IMDB和AG News等基准测试中表现优异，且仅需64个参数即可替代传统三万维词表，展现出极高的表达效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统Transformer依赖位置编码（PE）的问题，同时探索更紧凑、高效的参数化表达方式，以在保持或超越传统基线性能的同时，显著降低模型参数量和计算复杂度。

Method: 将词汇令牌表示为单位矩阵，并通过有序矩阵乘积对句子进行编码，利用矩阵乘法的非交换性隐式捕获词序信息。具体技术包括：1) 无查询、键、值投影的反对称自注意力机制；2) 支持可变长度文本块的低成本并行组合；3) 采用规范陪集读出层（canonical-coset readout layer）紧凑编码所有真实单位自由度；4) 通过嵌套群扩展实现持续学习，确保新任务不破坏旧表征。

Result: 在标准文本分类基准测试中，该方法匹配或超过了词袋模型（bag-of-words）基线。具体而言，在IMDB数据集上实现了更高的准确率，在AG News数据集上表现出可比性能。关键在于仅使用64个参数的实值密集编码，取代了约30,000维的传统词汇空间。

Conclusion: 基于单位矩阵的参数化方法通过代数结构有效捕捉序列依赖，具有高度的表达效率和可扩展性。它证明了在不依赖大规模词表和复杂位置编码的情况下，依然能够实现高性能的自然语言处理任务，为构建轻量级、可持续学习的NLP模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: We represent lexical tokens as unitary matrices and encode each sentence as their ordered product. The noncommutativity of matrix product captures word order without positional encodings (PEs). The same algebra yields several capabilities, including antisymmetric self-attention with no query, key, or value projections, and parallel composition of variable-length text chunks at a reduced attention cost. Furthermore, it provides a canonical-coset readout layer that encodes all true unitary degrees of freedom compactly, while supporting continual learning through nested group extensions that enlarge the operator space with each new task preserving prior representations exactly. Across standard text-classification benchmarks, the method matches or exceeds bag-of-words baselines. Achieving higher accuracy on IMDB and comparable performance on AG News. Notably, this is accomplished by replacing the conventional $\sim$30,000-dimensional vocabulary space with a dense, 64-parameter real-valued encoding, highlighting the expressive efficiency of our parameterization.

</details>


### [125] [Padārtha: Ontology-Grounded Fine-Grained NER Benchmark for Classical Sanskrit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29324)
*Sujoy Sarkar,Pretam Ray,Paramhans Shah,Manoj Balaji Jagadeeshan,Akash Gairola,Arjuna S R,Pawan Goyal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于本体论的梵语细粒度命名实体识别（NER）基准Padārtha，源自《摩诃婆罗多》史诗和印度Nyāya-Vaiśesika本体系统。通过专家标注12.6K条目及108,335个实体提及，并对比生成式与传统模型，发现尽管微调生成模型性能相当，但所有系统在细粒度和未见实体上表现显著下降，主要受限于词汇歧义下的默认倾向而非单纯数据稀缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的标签集多为现代新闻文本设计，将其应用于古典文献时强加了源文化的定义，缺乏中立性。为了更准确地描述古典梵语文本，需要建立根植于文本自身传统（如印度本体论系统）的本体 grounded 标注体系。

Method: 1. **构建Schema**：基于Nyāya-Vaiśesika古典印度本体系统，设计了包含18个细粒度类别、组织在10个本体节点下，并映射到5个标准粗粒度标签的标签集，确保与现有基准的互操作性。
2. **数据标注**：专家从学术索引中标注超过12.6K条目，链接到Mahānāma语料库中的提及，产生108,335个实体提及的细粒度标注（覆盖73,632节诗句），并构建了包含罕见提及的5,000节专家验证测试集。
3. **基准测试**：对梵语进行首次系统的生成式NER与传统架构的系统性基准测试。

Result: 1. **模型性能**：微调后的生成式模型在性能上与特定任务的传统系统相当。
2. **粒度影响**：所有系统从粗粒度到细粒度时性能出现急剧下降。
3. **泛化能力**：所有系统在处理训练过程中未见的“实体外”提及时表现困难。
4. **错误分析**：局限性不仅源于数据稀缺，微调模型对未见实体的召回率远低于已见实体，且在词汇歧义情况下倾向于默认为多数含义。

Conclusion: Padārtha是首个针对梵语的细粒度NER基准，证明了基于本体论的Schema在古典文献处理中的必要性。研究揭示了当前NLP模型在细粒度识别和处理未见实体方面的固有缺陷，特别是模型在面临歧义时的保守倾向，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Annotation schemas are not neutral. When applied to classical literature, tag sets developed for modern journalistic texts impose source-culture definitions on texts they were never designed to describe. We instead ground a schema in the tradition of the text itself introducing \textit{Padārtha}, the first ontology-grounded fine-grained Named Entity Recognition (NER) benchmark for Sanskrit, built on the \textit{Mahābhārata} epic. Our tag set derives from \textit{Nyāya-Vaiśesika}, a classical Indian ontological system, yielding 18 fine-grained categories organized under 10 ontological nodes and mapped onto five standard coarse tags, ensuring interoperability with existing benchmarks. Expert annotators label over 12.6K entries from a scholarly index of named entities, linked to corresponding mentions in the \textit{Mahānāma} corpus, producing fine-grained annotations for 108,335 entity mentions across 73,632 verses, along with a 5,000-verse expert-verified test set sampled to stress rare mentions. We present the first systematic benchmarking of generative NER against traditional architectures for Sanskrit, finding that fine-tuned generative models perform comparably to task-specific systems. However, all systems show a sharp decline from coarse to fine granularity and struggle with out-of-entity mentions unseen during training. The limitation is not due to data scarcity alone, as fine-tuned models recall unseen entities far worse than seen ones and tend to default to the majority sense under lexical ambiguity.

</details>


### [126] [StageWell: A Process-Aligned Chinese Corpus for Positive-Psychology Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29326)
*Yuxiong Wang,Ziwei Lin,Bo Wang,Yu Zhang,Shiguang Ni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出StageWell中文语料库和HQS协议，通过六阶段支持过程和多智能体重写工作流构建数据，利用DPO进行过程局部修复训练，显著提升大模型在积极心理学对话中的流程控制、响应质量和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有资源多采用回合级策略或整体偏好标签作为监督信号，导致多轮支持过程中的位置、支持功能和局部修复目标隐含不清，难以有效建模连贯的支持进程。

Method: 构建StageWell语料库，定义六阶段支持流程；开发HQS结构化协议；利用多智能体全对话重写工作流生成12,445个SFT实例、1,849对DPO偏好对及专家修订的GroundTruth子集；基于HQS构建过程局部化的DPO对（将缺陷输出作为拒绝样本，针对性重写作为选择样本）。

Result: 在四个9B-14B开源LLM上验证，BERTScore提升0.037，Q-Overall增加1.32分，S-exact增加0.236，H-critical率降低0.167，证明该方法在流程控制、响应质量和安全性上均有稳健增益。

Conclusion: 将支持性对话建模为结构化的多轮支持过程而非单轮响应生成，具有显著价值，能有效解决现有方法中过程信息隐式的问题。

Abstract: Positive psychology dialogue aims to support emotional distress and positive resource building, requiring models to produce not only empathetic replies but also coherent progression through a multi-turn support process. Existing resources often reduce supervision to turn-level strategies or holistic preference labels, leaving process position, support function, and local repair targets implicit. We introduce StageWell, a process-aligned Chinese corpus for positive psychology dialogue, together with HQS, a structured protocol for data construction and evaluation. StageWell organizes support into a six-stage support process and uses a multi-agent whole-dialogue rewriting workflow to construct 12,445 SFT instances, 1,849 DPO preference pairs, and a GroundTruth subset of 120 expert-revised dialogues and 977 QA pairs. Guided by HQS, DPO pairs are built as process-localized repairs: flawed model outputs are used as rejected responses, and targeted rewrites under the same context and stage constraint are used as chosen responses. Across four 9B-14B open-source LLMs, this supervision yields robust gains in process control, response quality, and safety. Averaged across models, BERTScore improves by 0.037, Q-Overall increases by 1.32 points, S-exact increases by 0.236, and the H-critical rate decreases by 0.167. These results highlight the value of modeling supportive dialogue as a structured multi-turn support process rather than as single-turn response generation.

</details>


### [127] [When to Adapt: Conditional Memory Adapters for Retention-Preserving Domain Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29327)
*Jiayu Hou,Lei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Engram Adapter框架，通过预训练条件记忆作为后处理适配器，利用n-gram模式匹配和门控机制实现动态激活，在提升领域内性能的同时保留通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法通常始终处于激活状态，导致在域外（OOD）数据上性能下降，无法兼顾领域内表现与通用能力。

Method: Engram Adapter：复用预训练时的条件记忆作为冻结LLM的后处理适配器；采用多通道局部n-gram模式匹配和显式占用跟踪作为轻量级选择先验；引入学习到的标量门控抑制不连贯的OOD检索，实现残差注入的动态选择性。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B/8B及AG-News、MedMCQA等任务上验证：提升了领域内准确率，同时保持99.4%--100.1%的平均OOD性能；在LegalBench上略优于冻结基座模型，而“始终在线”基线方法则显著退化；机制分析显示OOD激活被大幅衰减，KL漂移极小。

Conclusion: 条件激活是实现模块化、保留通用能力的领域专用化的有前景路径，可在冻结主干网络基础上实现高效的领域适配。

Abstract: Large language models deployed in specialized domains must improve in-domain performance without sacrificing general capabilities. Existing parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods are typically always on: their learned perturbations are applied to every input, which can degrade out-of-domain (OOD) performance. We propose Engram Adapter, a framework that repurposes pretraining-time conditional memory as a post-hoc adapter for frozen LLMs. It uses multi-channel matching over local n-gram patterns with explicit occupancy tracking as a lightweight selectivity prior, making residual injection more likely on in-domain inputs while a learned scalar gate suppresses incoherent OOD retrievals. We evaluate on Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B with AG-News and MedMCQA as adaptation tasks and OOD benchmarks spanning reasoning, translation, code generation, and legal reasoning. Engram Adapter improves in-domain accuracy while preserving 99.4%--100.1% of average OOD performance; on LegalBench it slightly exceeds the frozen base model on average, whereas comparable always-on baselines degrade sharply. Mechanistic analyses show that although OOD activations are non-zero, gate and projection attenuation reduce residuals to approximately 0.08% of hidden-state norm, yielding small KL drift and negligible accuracy change. These results suggest conditional activation is a promising route toward modular, retention-preserving domain specialization over frozen backbones.

</details>


### [128] [Arabic Safety Alignment as Selective Refusal: An Empirical Study of SFT, DPO, and Guard Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29378)
*Mohamad Zbib,Ammar Mohanna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究评估了阿拉伯语大语言模型在拒绝有害提示与避免过度拒绝良性提示之间的平衡。发现单纯的监督微调（SFT）会导致“一刀切”的拒绝，而混合SFT能实现高有害拒绝率（H=90-93%）和低良性拒绝率（B=14-23%）。DPO和推理守卫的效果因模型而异。Arabizi数据的转移效果有限。结论支持根据部署目标选择特定的模型干预措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语大语言模型需要在拒绝有害提示的同时，避免对良性或敏感提示进行过度拒绝。单一的拒绝率指标无法揭示这种权衡关系，因此需要更细致的评估方法。

Method: 使用良性拒绝率（B）和有害提示拒绝率（H）作为评估指标。在包含5个阿拉伯语能力模型和130次运行的AraSafe数据集上进行测试。比较了拒绝-only SFT、混合SFT配置、直接偏好优化（DPO）以及推理守卫策略。还进行了300条响应的盲审审计以验证标注一致性，并测试了Arabizi数据上的迁移效果。

Result: 1. 拒绝-only SFT导致模型倾向于 blanket refusal（全面拒绝）。
2. 选定的混合SFT配置实现了 H=90%-93% 且 B=14%-23% 的性能，部分配置在所有运行中超过 H=90% 的目标。
3. DPO和推理守卫对不同模型的B和H影响不同，并非统一提升。
4. 在盲审审计中，Qwen3Guard准确率为88.7%，Aya Expanse 32B为91.0%，无显著差异。
5. 对于Arabizi数据，虽然所有五个模型的H值都提高了，但均未达到90%，显示出现代标准阿拉伯语到Arabizi的迁移是不完全的。

Conclusion: 结果支持针对特定模型选择操作点：设定部署目标，并仅保留能改善该目标的干预措施。没有一种通用的最佳实践适用于所有模型。

Abstract: Arabic large language models must refuse harmful prompts without over-refusing benign or sensitive prompts, yet a single refusal rate hides this trade-off. We evaluate it using benign refusal B and harmful-prompt refusal H, where H measures refusal rather than harmful compliance. Across five Arabic-capable models and 130 runs on the full human-written AraSafe set, refusal-only supervised fine-tuning (SFT) collapses toward blanket refusal, whereas selected mixed-SFT configurations reach H = 90% to 93% at B = 14% to 23%; four selected configurations exceed the H = 90% target in all three runs, while Fanar does so in two of three. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and inference guards change B and H differently across models rather than acting as uniform upgrades. In a blinded 300-response audit, annotator binary-refusal agreement is 89.0% (kappa = 0.78); Qwen3Guard and Aya Expanse 32B reach 88.7% and 91.0% accuracy, respectively, with no conclusive paired difference. Selected SFT raises H on Arabizi for all five models, but none reaches 90%, showing only partial transfer from Modern Standard Arabic. Overall, the results support model-specific operating-point selection: set a deployment target and retain only interventions that improve it.

</details>


### [129] [AlgoWorlds: Benchmarking Tool Use for Global Optimization in Algorithmic Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29397)
*Zixiang Xu,Jiaan Wang,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AlgoWorlds基准测试，评估LLM在组合优化问题中利用工具信息做出全局最优决策的能力，发现现有模型虽能保证可行性但难以达到最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用基准仅评估流程完成的可行性，但在真实世界决策（如路线规划、车队调度）中，个体选择受共享约束影响，可行解往往次优。需要评估智能体能否将工具获取的信息转化为全局最优决策。

Method: 构建AlgoWorlds基准，将形式化的组合优化问题转化为部分观测的决策环境。包含240个环境，覆盖10类组合优化问题和4个工作负载水平。通过特定工具的确定性程序生成实例，精确算法验证最优性，并提供两种不同的工具接口。

Result: 评估了7种主流大语言模型（包括Claude Opus 4.8和GPT-5.6 Sol）。结果显示全球最优性极具挑战性：最佳模型仅在38.61%的情况下达到精确最优。即使收集了足够信息重建隐藏实例，大多数失败仍导致可行但次优的决策。

Conclusion: 挑战不仅在于信息获取，更在于信息整合、全局约束推理和决策验证。现有模型在处理复杂组合优化问题时，从局部信息到全局最优的转化能力仍有巨大提升空间。

Abstract: Tool-use benchmarks generally evaluate whether an agent completes a workflow using appropriate tools and valid arguments. However, feasibility alone is insufficient in real-world decision settings such as route planning and fleet dispatch. Individual choices interact through shared constraints and costs, so a feasible solution may still be substantially suboptimal. This raises a harder question: can an agent turn information gathered through tools into a globally optimal decision? We introduce AlgoWorlds, a benchmark that transforms formally specified combinatorial optimization problems into partially observed decision environments with verifiable global optima. Each environment contains a hidden instance observed only through task-specific information tools, after which the agent commits to one structured decision evaluated for feasibility and optimality. AlgoWorlds contains 240 environments covering ten combinatorial optimization families and four workload levels. Family-specific deterministic programs generate the instances, exact algorithms certify their optima and determine workload levels, and two structurally different tool interfaces present each underlying instance. We evaluate seven leading LLMs, including Claude Opus 4.8 and GPT-5.6 Sol. Achieving global optimality remains highly challenging: although leading models produce feasible decisions in most cases, the best-performing model reaches exact optimality in only 38.61% of cases. Even when agents collect sufficient information to reconstruct the hidden instance, most failures end in feasible but suboptimal decisions. The challenge therefore extends beyond information acquisition to information integration, global constraint reasoning, and decision verification. The project homepage is available at https://xzx34.github.io/AlgoWorlds/, and the code is available at https://github.com/xzx34/AlgoWorlds.

</details>


### [130] [Whose Assessment of Distress? Community Perspectives and LLM Alignment on Well-Being Posts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29446)
*Andrew Aquilina,Xiang Lorraine Li,Yu-Ru Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型（LLMs）在检测心理困扰时存在系统性偏差，倾向于高估轻度或无困扰的情况。通过对比6个身份社区的人类判断与9种开源及4种前沿LLM的表现，揭示了模型缺乏对特定社区规范的敏感度，可能导致心理健康AI部署中的公平性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的心理困扰检测LLM通常基于单一、未分化的标准进行对齐，忽略了心理困扰判断的社会情境性（即不同社区对情绪表达和求助行为的规范不同）。研究旨在评估这些模型是否能准确捕捉其评估对象社区的视角。

Method: 1. 开展透视主义标注研究：321名参与者对来自6个身份社区Reddit帖子的1,198条内容提供了9,587次判断，生成社区特异性标签。
2. 比较分析：对比“语境化组内”（in-group）与“非语境化组外”（out-group）人类判断的一致性。
3. 模型评估：将9种开源LLM配置和4种前沿LLM配置（如GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro, Claude Opus 4）的预测结果与人类标签进行对比。

Result: 1. 人类判断方面：组内评分者比组外评分者与社区观点更一致（OR=1.18），但效应因社区而异。
2. 模型偏差方面：开源LLM系统性地高估心理困扰，在无至轻度困扰场景下准确率仅31-44%，主要产生假阳性。
3. 前沿模型表现：GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Pro同样存在无至轻度困扰的高估现象；Claude Opus 4则更为保守。
4. 偏差来源：这种高估并非简单的“局外人”阅读位置所致（人类组外判断几乎对称，过估/欠估各占18%/19%），而是模型表现出超越人类判断（无论是组内还是组外）的“困扰先验”。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在心理困扰检测中存在严重的校准问题，特别是过度敏感导致的假阳性率高。这表明模型未能充分适应不同社区的文化规范。这一发现对心理健康领域AI的公平部署具有重要警示意义，因为校准错误的 distress detection 可能对被评估群体造成不成比例的影响。

Abstract: Judgments about psychological distress are socially situated: what counts as concerning hinges on community norms around emotional expression, vulnerability, and help-seeking. Yet large language models (LLMs) used for distress detection are typically aligned to a single, undifferentiated standard. How well do these models capture the perspectives of the communities whose language they assess? We address this question through a perspectivist annotation study in which 321 participants provided 9,587 judgments on 1,198 Reddit posts spanning six identity-based communities, yielding community-specific labels. Raters in the contextualized in-group condition show a modest tendency to agree more with their community than uncontextualized out-group raters (OR = 1.18), an effect varying significantly across communities. We then evaluate nine open-weight LLM configurations and four frontier configurations against these labels. Open-weight LLMs systematically over-estimate distress: when communities perceive none-to-mild distress, these models achieve only 31-44% accuracy, predominantly producing false positives. GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Pro show the same none-to-mild inflation even when their full-sample over/under rates are mixed, while Claude Opus 4 is more conservative. This pattern does not simply mirror an outsider reading position: uncontextualized out-group human aggregates were nearly symmetric, with 18% over-estimation versus 19% under-estimation. Instead, the models that inflate none-to-mild cases exhibit a distress prior that exceeds both contextualized in-group and uncontextualized out-group human judgments. These findings have implications for equitable AI deployment in mental health contexts, where miscalibrated distress detection may unevenly affect the communities being assessed.

</details>


### [131] [AI Can Be Easily Persuaded in Clinical Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29453)
*Jiayuan Zhu,Jiazhen Pan,Fenglin Liu,Minhao Hu,Junde Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示AI在临床决策中易受多种人为因素（如权威、背景、观点等）说服，即使面对错误建议也可能偏离正确判断，强调需提升AI抗干扰能力以保障医疗安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI融入临床实践，其参与高风险、基于证据的医疗决策日益增多。为确保患者安全，亟需了解AI在面对他人试图影响时，是否能保持客观公正的判断力。

Method: 通过受控实验，模拟不同情境下的说服性输入（包括专业权威、国籍、机构、过往表现、多人意见、支持性临床观点及重复施压等），测试并量化这些因素对AI决策的影响程度。

Result: 1. 多种社会线索（如权威、背景、机构等）均能显著影响AI决策；2. 资深医生比医学生提出的相同建议多改变约10%的案例结果；3. 声称更好的过往表现能持续增强说服力；4. 即使临床观点是伪造且指向错误答案，只要看似合理，仍能误导AI做出错误决定。

Conclusion: AI极易被“说什么”、“谁来说”以及“如何表达”所左右，缺乏独立验证观点正确性的能力。因此，必须改进AI机制，使其在面临 persuasion 时能维持稳健判断，从而实现高 stakes 医疗场景下的安全可靠应用。

Abstract: As AI becomes increasingly integrated into clinical practice, it is playing a growing role in medical decision making. Medicine, however, is a high stakes and evidence based field, where decisions can directly affect patients' lives. It is therefore important to understand whether AI can maintain objective judgment when others try to persuade it. In this paper, we study how easily AI can be persuaded through controlled experiments. We find that professional authority, national background, institutional affiliation, claimed past performance, multiple physicians, supported clinician views, and repeated pressure can all affect AI decisions. Surprisingly, the same persuasive input changes about 10% more cases when it comes from a senior clinician than from a medical student. Simply claiming a better performance history consistently makes the physician more persuasive. More strikingly, a plausible clinician view can persuade AI away from a correct decision even when it is fabricated to support an incorrect answer. This indicates that AI can be strongly influenced by convincing support without reliably determining whether this view from the clinician is correct. Together, these findings suggest that AI can be easily persuaded by what people say, who says it, and how the opinion is presented. Therefore, it is essential for AI to maintain sound judgment under persuasion, enabling its safe and reliable use in high stakes medical decision making.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [MERIT: Mitigating Exposure Bias in Generative XMC for User-Interest Propensity Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28931)
*Abhinav Mahajan,Arindam Sarkar,Prakash Mandayam Comar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MERIT框架，通过自纠正目标解决大语言模型在大规模电商用户兴趣匹配中的曝光偏差问题，显著提升了召回率和转化率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型电商平台中，将用户匹配到兴趣类别面临标签空间持续演变、用户兴趣信号稀疏且长尾的挑战。虽然自回归语言模型具有语义先验优势，但在教师强制微调下，推理时的预测会成为后续条件的输入，导致早期错误引发连锁反应（曝光偏差），使模型过度生成相关标签而遗漏无关的真实兴趣。

Method: 提出MERIT框架，采用基于排列不变性的多目标损失函数。该损失函数通过对真实标签和挖掘出的难负例标签的随机混合进行训练，使生成器在保持教师强制训练效率的同时，暴露于错误的前缀序列中。这种监督方式集中在分类位置，产生与倾向性对齐的隐藏状态，用于支持轻量级评分器的双向检索（用户到兴趣和兴趣到用户）。

Result: 在包含25万+兴趣类别的内部电商数据集上，MERIT将全局召回率提高了至少11.9%，平均Hit@k提高了6.1%。在生产环境的A/B测试中，用户转化率实现了0.26%的提升。

Conclusion: MERIT通过自纠正目标有效缓解了自回归模型在大规模兴趣匹配中的曝光偏差问题，证明了其在提升个性化购物推荐效果方面的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Matching users to interest categories at scale is central to personalized shopping, but the task is challenging in large e-commerce platforms, where label spaces continually evolve and user-interest signals are sparse and long-tailed. Autoregressive language models are appealing because their world knowledge and semantic priors over descriptors generalize across extreme label spaces and accommodate multiple valid label assignments. Yet under teacher-forced fine-tuning, inference-time predictions become part of the conditioning context: early errors steer later outputs toward co-occurring labels, over-generating near-correlates and missing unrelated true interests. We present MERIT, a framework for user-interest propensity modeling that mitigates this exposure bias through a self-correction objective. A permutation-invariant multi-target loss over shuffled mixtures of gold and mined hard-negative labels exposes the generator to erroneous prefixes while preserving the efficiency of teacher-forced training. This training objective concentrates supervision at classification positions, yielding propensity-aligned hidden states powering a lightweight scorer for bidirectional retrieval (interests for users and users for interests). On a proprietary e-commerce dataset with 250k+ interest categories, MERIT improves global recall by at least 11.9% and average Hit@k by 6.1%. In production A/B tests, it achieves +0.26% gain in user conversion.

</details>


### [133] [Book Readership During Movie Releases: An Exploratory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29019)
*Sushobhan Parajuli,Vittoria Vineis,Samira Vaez Barenji,Michael D. Ekstrand*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究电影改编上映这一外生事件对图书推荐系统相关性的影响，利用Goodreads数据验证读者量激增现象，并评估现有模型在事件发生时的排名表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外生事件会暂时改变推荐系统中物品的相关性，但这种变化在用户响应前的历史交互数据中往往不可见，导致传统模型难以及时捕捉此类动态变化。

Method: 使用与电影上映日期匹配的Goodreads大型数据集，分析围绕电影发行月的读者阅读量激增现象，并据此评估现有推荐模型在电影上映日附近对改编书籍的排序能力。

Result: 发现电影上映月存在明显的读者阅读量激增现象；通过评估现有推荐模型，揭示了它们在处理此类由外生事件引发的短期相关性变化时的局限性。

Conclusion: 现有的推荐模型未能充分适应由外生事件（如电影改编）引起的物品相关性临时波动，表明需要改进模型以更好地捕捉和利用这类非历史数据可见的动态信号。

Abstract: Exogenous events can temporarily change the relevance of items in recommender systems, but these shifts are often not visible in historical interaction data until after users have already responded. In book recommendation, movie adaptations provide a clear example of such events: the release of a movie based on a book can temporarily increase attention to the source text and change its relevance for some readers. We examine this phenomenon using a large Goodreads dataset matched to movie release dates. We find a clear spike in readership around the release month, and then we evaluate existing recommendation models to understand how they rank movie-adapted books around the movie release date.

</details>


### [134] [TAAL: Mitigating Early Beam Pruning in Generative Recommendation via Temporal Autoregressive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29179)
*Lianjie Li,Zhiying Tu,Dianhui Chu,Hongliang Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TAAL方法，通过训练时的联合软目标对齐和推理时的PMI校准，解决生成式推荐中早期解码步骤的检索失败问题，显著提升NDCG@10和SID存活率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准下一个token预测未能覆盖交互序列中的多模态转换，导致真实语义标识符（SID）在早期束搜索分支中被不可逆地剪枝，造成高比例的检索失败（91.9%--96.6%发生在前两步）。

Method: 提出时序自回归对齐（TAAL）。训练时构建历史转换的联合$(c_1,c_2)$软目标，使用前向KL散度使早期前缀分布与之对齐；推理时利用点互信息（PMI）校准候选分数，降低全局频繁前缀的影响。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Instruments和Yelp三个基准上，TAAL将NDCG@10分别提升39.5%、6.7%和28.6%，全SID存活率提高3.9%--16.6%。束宽分析显示，随着束宽变窄，相对存活增益增加，在$B=5$时达到39.4%。

Conclusion: TAAL有效缓解了生成式推荐中早期解码阶段的检索失效问题，特别是在小束宽场景下表现更优，显著提升了推荐性能和模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation encodes items as hierarchical semantic identifiers (SIDs) and retrieves the next item through autoregressive decoding. Standard next-token prediction, however, does not explicitly cover the multimodal transitions present in interaction sequences, leaving the ground-truth SID vulnerable to irreversible pruning at early beam-search branches. Across three public benchmarks, we find that 91.9\%--96.6\% of retrieval failures occur within the first two decoding steps. We therefore propose Temporal Autoregressive Alignment (TAAL). During training, TAAL constructs a joint $(c_1,c_2)$ soft target from historical transitions and aligns the early-prefix distribution with a forward KL objective. During inference, it calibrates candidate scores with pointwise mutual information (PMI) to reduce the influence of globally frequent prefixes. On Amazon Beauty, Instruments, and Yelp, TAAL improves NDCG@10 over the standard baseline by 39.5\%, 6.7\%, and 28.6\%, respectively, while increasing full-SID survival by 3.9\%--16.6\%. Beam-width analysis further shows that the relative survival gain grows as the beam narrows, reaching 39.4\% at $B=5$.

</details>


### [135] [Database-Augmented RAG for Automated Repair of REST API Misuses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29290)
*Shoei Inoue,Norihiro Yoshida,Erina Makihara,Shiyu Yang,Katsuro Inoue*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究评估了不同RAG配置对REST API误用自动修复的影响，发现按版本和内容类型组织规范可将修复率从54.3%提升至88.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决开发者调试REST API误用时因错误响应信息不足导致的反复沟通问题，并探索如何有效存储API规范以增强RAG在自动化修复中的效果。

Method: 构建11种具有不同数据库结构的RAG配置，利用真实仓库收集的REST API误用案例进行评估，并与基线方法对比修复率。

Result: 基线方法修复率为54.3%，而采用四个数据库的RAG方法最高修复率达到88.6%；按版本和内容类型组织规范被证明是有效的设计选择。

Conclusion: 合理组织API规范（如按版本和内容类型）能显著提升基于RAG的REST API误用自动修复效果。

Abstract: Many Internet of Things (IoT) services provide Representational State Transfer (REST) APIs, which require client developers to implement applications that conform to the corresponding API specifications. When client programs contain API misuse, developers debug them based on error responses. However, such responses are often insufficient for identifying the root cause, requiring developers to repeatedly communicate with the server. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is a promising approach for providing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, in automated repair of REST API misuses, it remains unclear how specifications should be stored in a RAG database. This study evaluates how different configurations for organizing API specifications affect RAG-based repair of REST API misuse. We constructed 11 RAG configurations with different database structures and compared their repair rates with a baseline method. For evaluation, we used REST API misuse cases collected from real-world repositories. The results show that, in the studied datasets, the baseline method achieved a repair rate of 54.3%, whereas a RAG-based method using four databases achieved a maximum repair rate of 88.6%. These results indicate that organizing specifications according to version and content type can be an effective design choice for RAG-based REST API misuse repair.

</details>


### [136] [Personalized Recommender Systems for Gym Workouts: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29409)
*Roan Rosema,Helma Torkamaan,Masoud Mansoury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出基于强化学习的健身推荐框架，从单一动作推荐扩展至包含组数、次数和负荷的完整训练处方，并利用用户跳过行为进行个性化适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统仅关注动作选择，忽略了组数、重复次数和训练负荷等关键因素，且难以适应用户跳过动作等行为，导致在真实场景中的适用性受限。

Method: 构建四个强化学习环境（动作推荐与完整处方，有无跳过交互），通过模拟用户行为验证模型，利用跳过行为实现在线个性化。

Result: 合成用户实验表明，建模完整训练处方任务相比仅推荐动作能获得更高的奖励和用户参与度，证实了 realistic workout planning 的重要性。

Conclusion: 将推荐范围扩展至完整训练处方并结合用户行为反馈，能显著提升个性化健身房推荐系统的效果。

Abstract: Workout recommender systems aim to help gym users complete effective and engaging training sessions. However, recommending exercises alone is insufficient, as a practical system must also determine appropriate sets, repetitions, and training loads, while adapting to user behavior such as skipping exercises. Existing approaches typically consider only a subset of these factors, limiting their applicability in real-world settings. In this paper, we extend workout recommendation from exercise selection to full workout prescription. We propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based framework with four environments: exercise-only and full-prescription settings, each with and without skip-based interaction. The full-prescription environments recommend exercises, sets, repetitions, and load, while the skip-enabled environments use user skipping behavior for online personalization. Experiments with synthetic users show that modeling the full prescription task leads to higher rewards and greater user engagement than exercise-only recommendation, highlighting the importance of realistic workout planning in personalized gym recommender systems.

</details>


### [137] [Agents as Knowledge Integrator and Utilizer in Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29410)
*Jinfeng Xu,Zheyu Chen,Shuo Yang,Jinze Li,Puzhen Wu,Zewei Liu,Zheng Lin,Jianheng Tang,Jing Yang,Wei Wang,Xiping Hu,Edith Ngai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AgentMMRec框架，通过整合代理和利用代理协同工作，解决多模态推荐中的语义偏差问题，显著提升召回率和NDCG指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视觉和文本特征直接注入物品表示或构建同质图，导致信号与推荐目标错位，存在语义差距；需结合用户行为和多模态内容在构建推荐图或调整排名前进行联合解释。

Method: 提出基于智能体的多模态推荐框架AgentMMRec：1) 整合代理（Integrator Agent）从训练交互和内容中推断偏好与属性，存入可复用知识记忆；2) 利用代理（Utilizer Agent）利用该记忆细化模态特定物品-物品图、构建行为感知同质图，并在冻结的记忆下重排候选列表。该方法不同于直接LLM特征增强或纯LLM重排，而是先生成知识再转化为图结构和模型表示。

Result: 在三个Amazon多模态推荐数据集上，AgentMMRec在Recall和NDCG指标上持续优于最新基线方法；在数据稀疏和物品冷启动场景下保持有效；且其构建的知识可迁移至现有骨干网络。

Conclusion: AgentMMRec通过知识集成视角解决了多模态推荐的语义偏差问题，证明了先构建结构化知识再进行推荐的有效性，提升了推荐性能及泛化能力。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly rely on multimodal recommender systems to rank products, media, and other Web content. Existing methods usually inject visual and textual features into item representations or build homogeneous graphs from modality-level similarity, but the resulting signals can remain misaligned with the recommendation objective. We study this semantic gap from a knowledge-integration perspective: multimodal content should be interpreted together with user behavior before it is used to construct recommendation graphs or adjust rankings.
  We propose AgentMMRec, an agent-based multimodal recommendation framework with two coordinated roles. The Integrator Agent infers behavior- and multimodal-aware user preferences and item properties from training interactions and item content, then stores them in a reusable knowledge memory. The Utilizer Agent consumes this memory to refine modality-specific item-item graphs, construct behavior-aware homogeneous graphs, and rerank candidate lists under a frozen evaluation-time memory. This design differs from direct LLM feature augmentation and pure LLM reranking because the generated knowledge is first converted into graph structure and model representations before recommendation. Experiments on three Amazon multimodal recommendation datasets show that AgentMMRec consistently improves Recall and NDCG over recent multimodal baselines, remains effective under sparsity and item cold-start settings, and can transfer its constructed knowledge to existing backbones.

</details>


### [138] [Content Exploration Beyond the Feed: Creator Supply and the Shared Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29430)
*Yuanyuan Shen,Yiren Yan,Wenjie Li,Chunhui Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文分析了短视频平台中“探索性推荐”对创作者供给和观众消费的双重影响。研究发现，探索性预算能显著提升创作者活跃度，且在不损害短期观众体验的前提下可优化资源配置。然而，由于内容库的动态更替，传统A/B测试存在测量局限：短期实验无法捕捉长期整体效应（如创作者供给增加带来的内容多样性红利），仅能识别直接Feed价值。研究强调了设计更长周期或隔离内容库的实验以准确评估总价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的工业界推荐系统评估主要关注观众侧的消费指标，往往忽略了创作者侧的响应（即内容供给变化）。在短视频平台，探索机制是新视频获取初始流量的关键，但其对创作者生态和长期内容库的影响未被充分量化，导致对推荐系统总价值的评估存在偏差。

Method: 对某大型短视频平台的四项实验进行实证分析：1）为期8个月的创作者消融实验（Creator Ablation），考察探索性预算对发布频率和活跃度的影响；2）预算匹配的资源重新分配实验，对比不同配置下的创作者参与度和观众指标；3）为期一年的观众消融实验（Viewer Ablation），量化观看量和观看时长的变化；4）为期三周的联合分流实验（Co-diverted Experiment），用于区分直接Feed效应与长期内容库效应。此外，还通过理论模型分析了内容库周转率对实验置信区间的影响。

Result: 1. 创作者响应：探索性预算使每位创作者的视频发布量提升8.55%，活跃创作者比例提升7.10%。2. 资源效率：在保持观众侧短期指标无显著变化的前提下，调整预算分配提高了创作者参与度。3. 观众影响：长期观众消融显示观看量增加1.74%，但观看时长减少2.13%。4. 测量局限：由于内容库随时间更替，短期实验（如3周）无法确定长期内容库效应的符号（正负），只能识别直接的Feed价值；若要捕捉总价值，需解决内容库周转带来的统计不确定性。

Conclusion: 探索性推荐不仅直接影响观众消费，还通过激励创作者供给产生长期的“内容库效应”。传统的短期观众侧A/B测试因抵消了内容库效应而低估了推荐系统的总价值。为了准确评估推荐策略，必须认识到实验窗口期受限于内容库周转率，并采用适当的设计（如隔离内容库或延长观察期）来捕捉创作者供给带来的有机增长和长期收益。

Abstract: Industrial recommenders give new content initial views through budgeted exploration, then use early performance to decide further delivery. On many short-video platforms, exploration is the primary way new videos reach viewers. Viewer-side tests measure consumption; the published budget objectives we review omit creator response. We analyze four experiments on a major short-video platform. An eight-month creator ablation finds production exploration raises videos posted per creator by 8.55% and creators posting at least once by 7.10% relative to a minimal floor. A budget-matched reallocation raises creator participation with no detectable short-run viewer-side change. A year-long viewer ablation finds 1.74% more video views but 2.13% less view time. A delivered view creates immediate feed value, can trigger organic take-up, and can induce creator supply. Take-up and supply replenish a shared corpus, creating two measurement limits. Viewer-side A/B tests cancel the corpus effect when both arms consume the same corpus. Giving each arm its own corpus avoids cancellation, but turnover still controls the horizon. If the corpus turns over at rate w per posting cycle, a t-cycle experiment expresses at most wt of the eventual corpus effect. More users reduce noise but do not speed turnover. Before the corpus path visibly bends, data cannot distinguish a modest fast effect from an arbitrarily large slow one, so a valid confidence interval may lack a finite upper endpoint. As predicted, the three-week co-diverted experiment cannot determine the sign of the eventual corpus effect. Within the window, it identifies the direct feed effect, and an exploratory cohort analysis detects organic lift after exploration ends. The experiments establish a positive creator response, measure the gross corpus flow visible within three weeks, and show the design and duration needed to identify total value.

</details>


### [139] [The Edge Spectrum of Choice-Derived Item Graphs: Strong and Weak Edges Encode Different Relations in Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29578)
*Keigo Sakurai,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图协同过滤中基于选择模型的边权重假设失效问题，指出强弱边编码了截然不同的关系（边谱），导致平滑算子与排序梯度出现符号不匹配。这一诊断解释了为何传统方法失效，并证明只有感知边大小的操作符才能恢复正确排序，建议将邻居截断k视为语义开关而非超参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图协同过滤通常隐含假设边越强表示关系越相似，但在来自选择模型的实际重要图中，强弱边编码的是定性不同的关系，导致现有方法性能不佳或行为不可预测。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明在基于选择模型的图中，强边集中在点击项的同类竞争者上，而弱边则不然，形成“边谱”。2. 形式化诊断：指出平滑算子与排名梯度之间存在符号不匹配，且共现图因构造原因不存在此问题。3. 实证验证：在MIND和EB-NeRD数据集上验证三种经验现象。4. 提出协议：将诊断转化为可复用的实践协议。

Result: 1. 解释了为什么衍生自选择模型的算子不如共现图；2. 解释了均匀标量修复（如符号翻转、边际损失）为何失败；3. 证实只有感知边幅度的操作符能恢复预测的排序。4. 发现邻居截断k是语义开关，其边界由诊断确定而非调优。

Conclusion: 图协同过滤中的边权重蕴含丰富的语义信息，强弱边代表不同关系。简单调整损失或忽略边大小会导致失败，必须采用感知边幅度的方法，并将邻居数量视为具有语义意义的阈值。本文为部署基于选择模型的算子提供了理论依据和实践指南。

Abstract: Graph collaborative filtering relies on item--item graphs whose edges are used for positive smoothing, under the implicit assumption that stronger edges encode more of the same relation as weaker ones. We show that this assumption fails for a practically important class of graphs: those whose edge weights come from a choice model. On such graphs, strong and weak edges encode qualitatively different relations, which we call an edge spectrum. Specifically, strong edges concentrate on the in-slate competitors of clicked items, exactly the pairs that the within-slate ranking gradient pushes apart, while weak edges do not. We formalize this as a sign mismatch between the smoothing operator and the ranking gradient, and prove that co-click graphs cannot exhibit the same misalignment by construction. This diagnosis explains three empirical observations on MIND and EB-NeRD: (i) drop-in choice-derived operators do not beat co-click, despite indexing structurally distinct neighborhoods; (ii) uniform scalar fixes (sign flip, in-slate margin loss) fail predictably, because the misalignment lives in the graph, not in the loss; (iii) only edge-magnitude-aware operators, with the regime boundary located by the diagnosis rather than by tuning, recover the predicted ordering. The neighbor cutoff $k$ is therefore a semantic switch, not a sparsification hyperparameter. Our claim concerns which interventions fail or succeed and why, not absolute headline gains, which the diagnosis itself predicts to be small under the attenuated propagation channel we observe. We turn the diagnosis into a reusable protocol practitioners can run before deploying any choice-derived item-side operator. Code: https://github.com/kyomusso/Edge-Spectrum-in-CF.

</details>


### [140] [RePair: Turning Retrieval Failures into Counterfactual Hard Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29604)
*Siyi Liu,Xiaorong Zhu,Enjun Du,Xinyu Zuo,Lisheng Duan,Haijin Liang,Jin Ma,Junfu Pu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RePair方法，通过LLM引导对CLIP模型的错误预测（False Positives）进行最小化局部修正，生成高难度正负样本对进行对比学习训练。该方法仅需少量合成数据（107K），在Flickr30K和COCO30K上显著优于传统数据增强基线，证明了基于模型失败条件的修复比无差别增强更具数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision-language检索方法（如CLIP双编码器）虽然整体性能强劲，但在处理细微语义差异时表现受限。传统的难样本挖掘无法构建正确的对应样本，而合成数据增强若未针对模型的具体失败点进行条件约束，往往会在不相关的维度上增加难度，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 引入RePair框架，遵循有效性（Validity）、最小性（Minimality）和局部性（Locality）三大原则：1) 双向挖掘假阳性（False Positives）作为反事实脚手架；2) 利用大语言模型（LLM）引导对造成失败的局部残差进行最小化编辑；3) 将修正后的样本与原始样本构成硬负样本对，使用局部硬样本对比损失进行训练。

Result: 在Flickr30K和COCO30K数据集上，RePair的表现优于受控的数据增强基线。仅使用107K个合成样本（比可比方法少26%-75%），即实现了更优的性能，证实了基于失败条件的修复策略在数据效率上的优势。

Conclusion: 利用模型当前的错误预测作为反事实基础进行针对性修复，是一种高效的数据增强策略。这种方法能够生成紧贴决策边界的互补监督信号（Pull-Push supervision），以更少的数据量提升跨模态检索的细粒度区分能力。

Abstract: Vision-language retrieval with CLIP-style dual encoders achieves strong cross-modal performance, yet practical accuracy often hinges on localized semantic distinctions where top-ranked near misses differ from the true match by a single critical detail. Hard-sample mining can select confusable candidates but cannot construct corrected counterparts; synthetic augmentation can generate novel samples but, without conditioning on actual model failures, targets irrelevant dimensions of hardness. We observe that a top-ranked false positive is a counterfactual scaffold---sharing most of the query's semantics while differing in a localized failure-causing residual. Minimally correcting this residual yields a hard positive of the ground truth in the same modality; the corrected and unedited versions form a hard negative pair that straddles the decision boundary, producing complementary pull--push supervision. We introduce RePair, guided by three principles---Validity, Minimality, and Locality---which mines false positives bidirectionally, applies LLM-guided counterfactual editing, and trains with a local hard-pair contrastive objective. On Flickr30K and COCO30K, RePair outperforms controlled augmentation baselines with only 107K synthetic samples---26\%--75\% fewer than comparable methods---confirming failure-conditioned repair is more data-efficient than error-agnostic augmentation.

</details>


### [141] [ICEGR: An Intent-Coherent End-to-End Generative Retrieval Framework for E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29652)
*Jiayi Tuo,Hehan Li,Dongjun Fu,Xin Lu,Ling Zhuang,Fuwei Zhang,Meifang Li,Peizhi Xu,Hanmeng Liu,Shuanglong Li,Liwei Qian,Yanbiao Ma,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ICEGR框架，通过意图感知SID构建、合成查询增强SFT和相关性校准偏好优化，解决电商生成式检索中查询意图一致性问题，显著提升检索效果和商业指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索（GR）方法在训练过程中难以维持查询-意图一致性：1) 基于静态信息的语义ID（SID）无法充分编码产品-意图关联；2) 低曝光产品缺乏真实查询监督，导致检索性能差；3) 业务导向的偏好优化可能牺牲查询匹配度以迎合热门或高价值商品。

Method: 提出ICEGR框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 意图感知SID构建：将查询意图信号融入SID构建，使SID能捕捉超越静态信息搜索意图；(2) 合成查询增强统一SFT：统一多个SFT任务，利用合成查询增强稀疏在线日志监督，为低曝光产品提供互补意图监督；(3) 相关性校准偏好优化：结合查询-产品相关性与业务信号至边际自适应偏好目标，兼顾意图保持与业务学习。

Result: 离线实验显示，相比基线，Recall@20提升21.7%，NDCG@20提升26.6%。在百度电商搜索部署A/B测试中，CTR相对提升3.52%，订单量提升15.96%，GMV提升7.53%。

Conclusion: ICEGR通过在整个GR训练管线中整合查询意图，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，显著提升了检索精度和商业转化效果，证明了端到端生成式检索在电商场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) is promising for e-commerce search, yet existing methods struggle to maintain query-intent consistency throughout the training pipeline. First, semantic ID (SID) construction based on static product information limits the ability of SIDs to encode product-intent associations. Second, although supervised fine-tuning (SFT) learns product-SID mappings across the catalog, low-exposure products still lack real query-intent supervision because query-to-SID training relies solely on online logs, resulting in poor retrieval performance for these products. Third, business-oriented preference optimization may favor popular or high-value products over those that best match the query intent, weakening query-product relevance. To address these issues, we propose ICEGR, an Intent-Coherent End-to-End Generative Retrieval Framework for E-commerce Search that integrates query intent consistently throughout the GR training pipeline. ICEGR comprises three components: (1) Intent-Aware SID Construction incorporates query-intent signals into SID construction, enabling SIDs to capture search intent beyond static product information; (2) Synthetic Query-Enhanced Unified SFT unifies multiple SFT tasks under the query-to-SID objective and augments sparse supervision from online logs with synthetic queries, providing complementary query-intent supervision for low-exposure products; and (3) Relevance-Calibrated Preference Optimization integrates query-product relevance and business signals into a margin-adaptive preference objective, preserving query intent while enabling business preference learning. Offline results show that ICEGR improves Recall@20 by 21.7% and NDCG@20 by 26.6% over the baseline. Deployed as an end-to-end generative retrieval pathway in Baidu E-commerce Search, ICEGR achieves relative improvements of 3.52% in CTR, 15.96% in order volume, and 7.53% in GMV in an A/B test.

</details>


### [142] [Demand-Side Measurement for Generative Engine Optimization: Constructing and Validating a Million-Persona, Intent-Annotated Buyer Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30023)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin,Daniil Dzemesjuk*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文构建了PersonaGen-1M，这是一个包含103万合成买家画像的大型语料库，旨在解决现有数据缺乏搜索意图和信任来源标签的问题。该语料库支持将需求侧数据与供给侧推荐测量相结合，以研究品牌如何进入生成式引擎的答案短名单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
