Motivation: 现有的大型人设语料库主要用于训练数据多样性，缺乏分阶段的搜索意图标签（如信息型、商业型、交易型）和偏好来源字段，导致无法与供给侧的推荐测量数据进行关联。为了研究品牌在生成式引擎答案中的表现，需要能够反映买家真实需求和信任源的需求侧数据。

Method: 从四个公共数据集中提取约4000万原始人设描述，通过GPU加速的MinHash LSH进行语义去重，并丰富为固定模式。最终构建出PersonaGen-1M语料库，包含511个行业标签和4个市场背景，涵盖1900多万个结构化行为属性，其中516万个为搜索查询。每个画像包含一个主要意图标签和一个命名信任来源类型的字段。

Result: 成功构建了PersonaGen-1M语料库，包含1,031,732个合成买家画像。其意图分布为：78.3%信息型，17.4%商业型，4.3%交易型。与其他百万级语料库相比，PersonaGen-1M独特之处在于提供了按人设命名的来源列表、分阶段商业搜索意图标签以及附带的查询集。

Conclusion: 该语料库的主要预期用途是将意图字段与引用来源数据进行关联，以评估驱动推荐的查询。虽然受控的经验估计是未来工作，但该语料库已通过非商业研究请求共享完整版本，并公开了分层子集以供协议、模式和验证的检查与复用。

Abstract: Generative engines such as ChatGPT, Gemini, and Perplexity answer buyer questions directly and name a shortlist of brands inside the answer. Studying how brands enter or fail to enter that shortlist requires demand-side data: what buyers in a category ask, what information they need, and which sources they trust. Existing large persona corpora are built for training-data diversity and carry neither a staged search-intent label nor a preferred-sources field, so they cannot be joined to supply-side recommendation measurements. We built and validated PersonaGen-1M, a corpus of 1,031,732 synthetic buyer personas spanning 511 industry labels and 4 market contexts, carrying 19,416,821 structured behavioral attributes, 5,160,046 of them search queries. Each persona carries a single primary_intent label covering its query set (78.3% informational, 17.4% commercial, 4.3% transactional) and a preferred_sources field naming the source types that buyer would trust. The corpus was built from roughly 40 million raw persona descriptions drawn from four public datasets through GPU-accelerated MinHash LSH plus semantic deduplication, then enriched to a fixed schema. The intent field selects the commercial-evaluation personas whose queries drive recommendation, and the preferred_sources field pairs against citation-provenance data; that join is the primary intended use, and its controlled empirical estimate is future work. Among million-scale persona corpora surveyed in August 2026, one other carries a source-preference attribute, as a six-value media-channel enum; PersonaGen-1M pairs named per-persona source lists with a staged commercial search-intent label and an attached query set. The full corpus is shared on request for non-commercial research; a stratified subset is published openly so the protocol, the schema and the validation can be inspected and reused without asking us.

</details>


### [143] [The Language of the Question Selects the Market: Query Language and Exit IP as Separable Factors in Commercial Recommendations from a Generative Search Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30052)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究揭示了ChatGPT在商业问答中推荐产品的不稳定性，并指出查询语言比地理位置更决定本地供应商的出现。语言与位置的影响可分离：固定语言时，IP地址改变会改变推荐的供应商所属市场，但回答语言保持不变。少数语言处于中间层级，而类别是否受国家监管而非是否由本国供应，是解释语言效应的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究生成式搜索界面在回答商业问题时，其推荐的市场产品是由模型推理决定还是预先设定的；分析查询语言、地理位置（出口国）对本地品牌曝光率及系统稳定性的影响。

Method: 对登出的ChatGPT网页界面和OpenAI API进行了234次受控探测，数据收集于2026年8月29日和30日，涵盖四个出口国家和六种查询语言，每种提示词进行六次重复运行。

Result: 1. 顶级推荐不稳定：在四组提示词中，六次相同运行结果不同，且该现象在浏览器和API中一致，表明不稳定性源于系统本身。2. 查询语言决定本地供应商是否存在：当查询语言与国家匹配时，全球品牌仅占1/24；而在爱沙尼亚和土耳其用英语提问时，本地品牌出现率为0。3. 语言和位置影响可分离：固定查询语言仅改变出口IP时，推荐的供应商变为所在国品牌，但回答仍使用查询语言。4. 少数语言（如俄语）处于中间层级；控制实验表明，无语言效应是因为该类别不受国家监管，而非缺乏本国供应。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索的推荐结果具有系统性不稳定性；查询语言是决定本地品牌曝光的关键因素，而地理位置影响具体推荐的市场主体，两者作用机制分离；产品类别的国家监管属性解释了语言效应的存在与否，而非单纯的供应链分布。

Abstract: When a generative search interface answers a commercial question, which market's products it names is decided before the model reasons about the products. We report a controlled probe of 234 runs against the logged-out ChatGPT web interface and the OpenAI API, collected on 29 and 30 August 2026 across four exit countries and six query languages, with six identical runs per cell. Three results. First, the top recommendation is unstable: it changed across six identical runs on four of six prompts, and that rate was identical in the browser interface and in the API with web search both enabled and disabled, so instability is a property of the system and not of the surface. Second, query language, and not location, decides whether local suppliers appear at all. Where the query language matched the country, a global brand won 1 of 24 runs; asked in English on the same connections, local brands took 0 of 6 runs in Estonia and Turkiye. Third, language and location are separable and act on different things: holding the query language fixed and moving only the exit IP moves the market whose brands are named while the answer stays in the query language. We show this on two unrelated pairs, Turkish asked from Berlin and Russian asked from Tallinn, and in both the answer names the resident country's suppliers. A minority language occupies a middle tier: Russian asked from Estonia names an Estonian supplier in 4 of 6 runs and a global one in all six, where Estonian names a local supplier in every run and English names none. A negative control in a second category, coded with the same instrument, shows no language effect at all, and disconfirms our own expectation: that category does have domestic suppliers and none was named in any language, which points the explanation at whether a category is nationally regulated rather than at whether it is nationally supplied.

</details>


### [144] [E-SENS: Exclusion-Sensitive Penalization for Negative-Constraint Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30130)
*Yerang Kim,Jiyoon Myung,Joohyung Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出E-SENS，一种无需训练的重新排序方法，通过提取“陷阱查询”并减去其与原始查询的相似度得分，来解决检索增强语言模型在负约束（排除特定概念）场景下的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强语言模型在处理否定约束时容易失效。当用户明确排除某个概念时，密集检索器仍可能因该概念出现在查询文本中而赋予相关文档高相似度得分，导致检索结果包含被禁止的内容。

Method: 引入E-SENS方法，这是一种训练-free的重新排序策略。该方法从查询中提取一个紧凑的“陷阱查询”（trap query），代表被排除的概念，然后从原始查询的检索得分中减去该陷阱查询的相似度得分。

Result: 在ExcluIR数据集上，E-SENS在四种嵌入模型上展示了清晰的召回率与违规率的权衡关系，并在保持召回率的前提下显著减少了陷阱内容的检索。

Conclusion: E-SENS能有效缓解检索器对显式否定约束的不尊重问题，通过简单的相似度减法机制提升负约束场景下的检索准确性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented language models can fail to respect negative constraints when the retriever supplies evidence about concepts the user explicitly excluded. Beyond explicit negation, queries may ask for answers that include one concept while excluding another, or for entities that belong to a category but differ from a closely related instance. Because the excluded concept still appears in the query text, dense retrievers may assign high similarity to documents about that concept even when the user asks to avoid it. We introduce E-SENS, a training-free reranking method for negation-sensitive retrieval. E-SENS extracts a compact trap query for the excluded side and subtracts trap-query similarity from the original-query retrieval score. On ExcluIR, E-SENS shows a clear recall-violation trade-off across four embedding models and reduces trap retrieval at recall-preserving settings.

</details>


### [145] [Understanding before verifying: Claim normalization for automated citation verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30145)
*Yifan He,Mengjia Wu,Siming Deng,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CNCV框架，通过“主张规范化”解决现有两阶段引用验证中的范围、视角和命题纠缠问题，显著提升分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索-分类的两阶段框架在处理原始引用主张时存在复杂性，导致范围不匹配、视角不匹配和命题纠缠三个问题，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出Claim-Normalized Citation Verification (CNCV)三阶段框架：1. 主张规范化（应用三种重写策略简化原始主张）；2. 带定位的证据检索；3. 引用分类。在18种分类器上进行因子实验评估。

Result: 相比传统两阶段框架，CNCV使编码器模型的宏观F1平均提升12%，生成式LLM提升10%。主要归因于证据质量改善。自动规范化后检索的证据在下游分类性能上等同于人工标注证据。

Conclusion: 主张规范化有效解决了引用验证中的关键难题，提升了证据质量和分类精度，为学术声明可靠性评估提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Citation accuracy has been studied for decades because of its importance to research reliability. Content-level citation verification assesses the reliability of scholarly claims. Recent work adopts a two-stage retrieval-classification framework inherited from fact-checking. However, this design overlooks the complexity of the raw citing claim and introduces three issues into the verification system, namely scope mismatch, perspective mismatch, and proposition entanglement. These issues increase the difficulty of retrieval and classification, thereby limiting model performance. Motivated by this gap, we propose claim normalization, which applies three rewriting strategies to the raw citing claim before retrieval and classification, allowing each downstream model to perform a single, well-defined task. Building on this method, we develop Claim-Normalized Citation Verification (CNCV), a new three-stage framework consisting of claim normalization, evidence retrieval with grounding, and citation classification. We evaluate CNCV across 18 classifiers using a factorial experiment on human-annotated citation instances. Compared with the prior two-stage framework, CNCV improves macro F1 by an average of 12% for encoders and 10% for generative LLMs, driven by improved evidence quality, the dominant factor identified in our experiments. Evidence retrieved from automatically normalized claims yields downstream classification performance statistically equivalent to that obtained with manually annotated evidence.

</details>


### [146] [Doc-REFRAG: Rethinking Multimodal Document Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30163)
*Ruofan Hu,Shengyang Xu,Minjie Hong,Xiaoda Yang,Sashuai Zhou,Ke Lei,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有单图RAG模型在多图场景下准确率不足及计算开销大的问题，提出DocLongRAG数据集和Doc-REFRAG框架。后者通过基于RL的选择器压缩视觉token并选择性扩展相关问题相关部分，在六个基准测试中超越11个基线，实现SOTA精度且降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的知识存在于多模态文档中，需要检索增强生成（RAG）进行准确问答。现有模型主要针对单图或封闭文档设计，在处理真实多图场景时准确率有限；此外，处理大量检索图像会产生由无关视觉token带来的巨大计算开销。

Method: 1. 构建DocLongRAG大规模数据集：包含343K问答对，平均每对关联37.4张检索图像，以反映真实的RAG工作流程。
2. 提出Doc-REFRAG框架：一种基于问题引导的机制，将视觉token压缩为粗略块（coarse chunks），并通过轻量级的基于强化学习（RL）的选择器，有选择地扩展与问题相关的token。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，Doc-REFRAG的表现优于11个强基线模型，达到了最先进的（SOTA）准确率，同时显著降低了推理延迟。

Conclusion: 提出的Doc-REFRAG框架有效解决了多图RAG中的准确性和效率问题，资源已开源。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge resides in multimodal documents, necessitating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for accurate question answering. However, existing multimodal RAG models are primarily designed for single-image or closed-document settings and exhibit limited accuracy in realistic multi-image scenarios. Moreover, processing numerous retrieved images incurs substantial computational overhead from irrelevant visual tokens. To address these challenges, we introduce DocLongRAG, a large-scale dataset of 343K question--answer pairs, each associated with an average of 37.4 retrieved images to reflect authentic RAG workflows. Building on this dataset, we propose Doc-REFRAG, a question-guided framework that compresses visual tokens into coarse chunks and selectively expands question-relevant ones via a lightweight RL-based selector. Experiments on six benchmarks show that Doc-REFRAG outperforms eleven strong baselines, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly lower inference latency. Our resources are available at https://github.com/Collab-Gen/Doc-REFRAG.

</details>


### [147] [SetMIR: Multi-Interest Retrieval as Set Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30251)
*Xiaodong Liu,Congfei Zhang,Hsiang-wei Chao,Siman Wang,Xiao Bai,Tong Zhao,Jingxiao Ma,Wen Zhang,Zhe Liu,Shantanu Aggarwal,Di Huang,William Leach,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SetMIR，将多兴趣检索视为集合预测问题，通过匈牙利匹配解决兴趣坍塌，利用存在分数和NMS实现动态调度，显著减少查询量并提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一用户嵌入难以捕捉多样化兴趣；现有方法存在“兴趣坍塌”（不同嵌入学习相同兴趣）和“静态调度”（服务时使用固定检索预算，即使部分嵌入不必要）两个问题。

Method: 使用Transformer编码用户行为历史，通过K个可学习查询解码用户兴趣集合，每个查询生成检索嵌入和存在分数。训练时采用匈牙利匹配确保查询学习不同兴趣，推理时基于存在分数和查询级非极大值抑制（NMS）仅发起活跃且非冗余的ANN查询。

Result: 在Snap的动态产品广告（DPA）数据上，SetMIR在所有指标上优于四种学习型多兴趣检索器，且每次请求的ANN查询减少33%。部署后整体CVR提升3.1%，相比同配置的传统item-to-item检索源，CTR提升44%，CVR提升51%。

Conclusion: SetMIR有效解决了多兴趣检索中的兴趣坍塌和静态调度问题，实现了更精准的兴趣建模和更高效的资源利用，在实际工业场景中取得了显著的收益提升。

Abstract: Embedding-based retrieval is at the core of industrial recommender systems, but a single user embedding is often too limited to capture a user's diverse interests. Multi-interest retrieval addresses this by using multiple user embeddings, yet existing methods still suffer from two issues: interest collapse, where different embeddings learn the same interest, and static dispatch, where serving uses a fixed retrieval budget even when some embeddings are unnecessary. We propose SetMIR, which treats multi-interest retrieval as a set prediction problem. SetMIR encodes a user's behavior history with a transformer and uses K learnable queries to decode a set of user interests, each producing a retrieval embedding and a presence score. During training, Hungarian matching assigns targets to queries one-to-one, so matched queries learn distinct interests and the presence head learns which queries are active. At serving time, SetMIR uses presence scores and query-level Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS) to issue only active, non-redundant ANN queries. On Snap's Dynamic Product Ads (DPA) data, SetMIR outperforms four learned multi-interest retrievers on every metric while issuing 33% fewer ANN queries per request. Deployed as a new retrieval source in the DPA production stack, SetMIR lifts overall CVR by 3.1%, while lifting CTR by 44% and CVR by 51% over the item-to-item retrieval source with the same item embeddings, ANN index, and retrieval quota.

</details>


### [148] [CAMIE: Co-Engagement-Aware Multimodal Item Embeddings for Snap Dynamic Product Ads Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30255)
*Xiaodong Liu,Siman Wang,Congfei Zhang,Hsiang-wei Chao,Xiao Bai,Wen Zhang,Jingxiao Ma,Zhe Liu,Yunzhi Zhou,Yajun Wang,Jinchao Li,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Snap公司提出CAMIE框架，通过利用大语言/多模态模型（LLM/MLLM）的共享嵌入空间，并针对用户共同互动行为进行微调，解决了传统I2I检索中编码器碎片化和训练目标与业务转化不对齐的问题。该框架在离线评估中表现优异，并在线上部署后显著提升了点击率（CTR）和转化率（CVR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境的Snap动态产品广告（DPA）中，项目到项目（I2I）检索面临两大挑战：一是视觉、文本和多模态编码器分离导致检索栈碎片化；二是仅基于内容的训练方式未能使嵌入向量与驱动下游转化的“共同互动”（co-engagement）行为对齐。

Method: 提出CAMIE（Co-Engagement-aware Multimodal Item Embedding）框架。该方法基于LLM/MLLM骨干网络，利用其原生多模态接口将商品图像和元数据表示在共享嵌入空间中。随后，从用户旅程中挖掘共同互动的商品对，使用对称的批次内InfoNCE损失函数对骨干网络进行微调。

Result: 1. 离线效果：在Recall@10指标上优于最强的商业多模态嵌入模型，且同一检查点可支持文本检索，质量损失极小。
2. 线上效果：作为现有内容基I2I编码器的即插即用替代品，相比多模态对照组，CTR提升+0.390%，CVR提升+10.832%；相比文本对照组，CTR提升+18.958%，CVR提升+13.12%；整体DPA流量CTR提升+0.211%，CVR提升+1.911%。

Conclusion: CAMIE成功解决了I2I检索中的碎片化和目标错位问题，通过整合多模态信息并与用户实际互动行为对齐，显著提升了广告系统的业务指标，并已投入生产环境使用。

Abstract: Item-to-item (I2I) retrieval is a core primitive in large-scale recommendation and advertising systems. In production Snap Dynamic Product Ads (DPA), I2I retrieval faces two challenges: separate visual, textual, and multimodal encoders fragment the retrieval stack, and content-only training does not align embeddings with the co-engagement behavior that drives downstream conversions. We present CAMIE, a co-engagement-aware multimodal item embedding framework for Snap DPA retrieval. CAMIE builds on LLM/MLLM backbones, using their native multimodal interfaces to represent item images and metadata in a shared embedding space. It then fine-tunes the backbone on co-engaged item pairs mined from user journeys with a symmetric in-batch InfoNCE objective. Offline, CAMIE outperforms the strongest commercial multimodal embedding model on Recall@10 and serves text-only retrieval from the same checkpoint with minimal quality loss. Online, CAMIE serves as a drop-in replacement for two deployed content-based I2I encoders, delivering +0.390% CTR / +10.832% CVR over the multimodal control, +18.958% CTR / +13.12% CVR over the text control, and +0.211% CTR / +1.911% CVR on overall DPA traffic. CAMIE is deployed in production.

</details>


### [149] [PEARL: Front-Loading Relational Chains for Multi-Hop Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30291)
*Subeen Ho,Hyeongu Kang,SeongKu Kang,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PEARL框架，通过垂直分区和预计算多跳查询解决大语言模型在多表检索中忽略跨表语义的问题，无需训练且显著提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖整表表示，忽略了由连接关系（join relationships）引起的跨表语义，导致在处理碎片化和关系型真实世界数据时检索效果不佳。

Method: 提出PEARL，一种免训练的框架。该方法离线生成基于预识别连接路径的多跳查询，并将相关列重组为垂直分区的语料库单元，从而实现有效的多表检索，避免查询时的LLM推理。

Result: 实验表明PEARL持续优于现有方法，在3-hop查询的R@2指标上最高提升30.05%。

Conclusion: 垂直分区子表编码范式能有效解决多表检索中的语义缺失问题，提供了一种高效、无需训练的替代方案。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in tabular reasoning, retrieving relevant tables remains challenging due to the fragmented and relational structure of real-world data. Existing work typically relies on whole table representations that overlook cross-table semantics induced by join relationships. We propose PEARL, a training-free framework that shifts the paradigm toward vertical partitioning-based sub-table encoding. PEARL augments the retrieval corpus offline by generating multi-hop queries over pre-identified join paths and reorganizing relevant columns into vertically partitioned corpus units, enabling effective multi-table retrieval without query-time LLM inference. Experiments show that PEARL consistently outperforms existing methods, with up to +30.05% gains in R@2 on 3-hop queries. The source code is available at https://github.com/SOOB2NHO/PEARL.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Ranking Accuracy: Evaluating LLM-Cited Feature Rationales for Next Basket Repurchase Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30333)
*Yanan Cao,Anay Dombe,Murali Mohana Krishna Dandu,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Sinduja Subramaniam,Yogananth Mahalingam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现，现成的通用大语言模型（LLMs）在下一篮购买推荐任务中的排序准确率不及监督学习模型，因此不适合作为独立的推荐系统。然而，LLM生成的基于特征的可解释理由（rationales）能提供具有结果支撑的排名信号。建议将LLM定位为“经过验证的解释组件”，而非主要排序器，其理由质量应与排序准确性分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际生产环境中，推荐系统的服务质量不仅取决于排序准确率，还取决于能否向用户简洁地展示推荐理由（即“为什么现在推荐该商品”）。现有研究缺乏对LLM在此类可解释性证据生成方面能力的系统性评估。

Method: 1. 构建涵盖购买间隔、频率、最近购买时间、用户行为及物品流行度的复购特征集。
2. 在两个公开杂货数据集和一个专有零售数据集上评估LLM。
3. 对比LLM作为排序器与启发式及监督排序器的性能。
4. 通过跨模型特征掩码协议（cross-model feature-masking protocol），比较LLM引用的特征与特定模型的归因方法，测量掩码选定特征后的排序降级程度，以验证LLM引用特征的结果支撑信号。

Result: 1. LLM的排序分数不具有竞争力，显著低于监督排序器。
2. 在某些设置下，调整提示词和证据表示方式可以改善LLM引用特征的结果支撑掩码效果，即使排序性能未提升；但这种效果依赖于数据集，且并不始终匹配归因基线。

Conclusion: 现成的LLM不应单独用作复购推荐器。其实际应用场景应定位为“经过验证的解释组件”，用于提供人类可读的理由。理由是应独立于排序准确性进行评估，重点在于其提供的证据是否具有结果支撑的信号价值。

Abstract: Next-basket repurchase recommendation is commonly formulated as a ranking task: given a customer's purchase history, the system ranks previously purchased items that may be needed again. In production settings, however, ranking accuracy is only one component of recommendation quality. Customers may also benefit from concise evidence about why an item is recommended now. Large language models (LLMs) offer a potential way to surface such evidence through feature-based, human-readable rationales grounded in interpretable behavioral signals. We construct repurchase features spanning cadence, frequency, recency, user behavior, and item popularity, and evaluate LLMs on two public grocery datasets and one proprietary retail dataset. We investigate (1) whether off-the-shelf LLMs can use these features as next-basket scorers relative to heuristic and supervised rankers, and (2) whether LLM-cited features carry outcome-grounded ranking signal. For the latter, we compare LLM-cited features with model-specific attribution methods under a cross-model feature-masking protocol that measures ranking degradation after masking selected features. Our results show that LLM scores are not competitive with supervised rankers, suggesting that off-the-shelf LLMs should not be used as standalone repurchase recommenders. However, changes in prompt and evidence representation can improve outcome-grounded feature-masking results in some settings even when ranking performance does not improve; the effect is dataset-dependent and does not consistently match attribution baselines. These findings suggest a practical role for LLMs as validated explanation components rather than primary rankers, with rationale quality evaluated separately from ranking accuracy.

</details>


### [151] [HF-SID: High-Fidelity Semantic IDs for Generative Retrieval in Location-Based Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30479)
*Haowen Lin,Jing Li,Zhibin Hao,Fangye Wang,Lihui Su,Song Yang,Xiaojiang Zhou,Pengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HF-SID方法，通过恢复地理、数值和结构保真度解决LBS中语义ID（SID）信息丢失问题，使用3-token SID实现高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索中POI的语义ID（SID）无法保留细粒度差异，导致解码时信息不可恢复。具体痛点包括：1. LLM对连续坐标嵌入不连续，数值差异不能反映真实地理距离；2. 动态数值属性尺度差异大，相同差距在不同属性中意义不同；3. 短文本无法传达层级隶属关系，文本相似的POI可能属于不同层级。

Method: 提出HF-SID框架，在表示阶段恢复地理、数值和结构保真度。具体技术包括：将坐标转换为连续3D笛卡尔形式；使用Geo-CPT和Num-CPT及类型感知嵌入将每个数值编码为单位值；应用基于结构的对比学习目标（仅作用于最后一层残差），分离共享粗标签但细粒度不同的共址POI。

Result: HF-SID通过丰富表示而非延长标识符，使用3-token SID，在不增加解码成本的情况下实现了地理、数值和结构保真度的恢复。

Conclusion: HF-SID有效解决了LBS生成式检索中SID信息丢失问题，提升了检索精度，同时保持了高效的解码性能。

Abstract: Generative retrieval has attracted increasing attention in Location-Based Services (LBS), where each Point-of-Interest (POI) is represented as a Semantic ID (SID). As the SID is the only channel through which POI information reaches the generative model, whatever it fails to preserve is irrecoverable at decoding time, and LBS retrieval is especially sensitive to the fine-grained differences that existing SIDs blur. Specifically, (1) LLMs embed continuous coordinates discontinuously, so their numeric differences do not reflect true geographic distance; (2) dynamic numerical attributes differ vastly in scale, so an identical gap may be decisive for one attribute yet negligible for another; and (3) short text cannot convey hierarchical affiliation, as text-similar POIs may belong to different hierarchies. We therefore propose HF-SID, which restores geographic, numerical, and structural fidelity at the representation stage, before any information is committed to a discrete code. It transforms coordinates into a continuous 3D Cartesian form and encodes each numerical value as a single unit, consolidated inside the LLM by Geo-CPT and Num-CPT with type-aware embeddings; a Structure-based Contrastive Learning objective, applied only to the last-layer residual, then separates co-located POIs that share a coarse tag but differ at the fine level. Because these mechanisms enrich the representation rather than lengthen the identifier, HF-SID uses a 3-token SID at no extra decoding cost. On a large-scale industrial

</details>


### [152] [Local-to-Global Sentence-Level Graph Reranking for Scientific Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30525)
*Zheng Dou,Zhao Zhang,Hao Geng,Ningjing Wang,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LoG-Reranker，一种基于局部到全局的句子级图重排序框架，通过角色感知局部评分和句子关系建模，解决科学综合中信息覆盖不全及上下文关系缺失问题，显著提升生成回答的质量与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重排器多在段落级别操作，导致关键方法、实证和比较信息被淹没；且主要依赖独立的查询-候选评分，忽略了候选项间的互补、语境和对比关系，限制了信息覆盖率和综合回答的全面性。

Method: 提出LoG-Reranker框架：首先进行角色感知的局部评分以识别细粒度的相关句子；然后在候选集上构建句子图，对句子间关系进行建模以实现全局排名优化；最后将高分句子及其连接邻居组织为结构化输入供生成器使用。

Result: 在科学综合和重排序基准测试上的广泛实验表明，LoG-Reranker持续优于竞争性重排器，产生了更可靠的排名，并提高了生成综合回答的质量。

Conclusion: LoG-Reranker有效解决了传统重排器在粒度细化和上下文关系建模上的不足，为检索增强科学综合提供了更高质量的信息整合方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented scientific synthesis aims to answer complex research questions by integrating information from multiple papers into comprehensive and well-grounded responses. Since the generator can only synthesize the information selected and organized by the reranker, the quality of the generated synthesis depends critically on the reranked results. However, most rerankers operate at the passage level, which leaves key methodological, empirical, and comparative information buried in long and flat contexts, weakening the grounding of generated claims. Moreover, existing rerankers mainly rely on independent query-candidate scoring which overlooks complementary, contextual, and contrasting relations across scientific candidates, limiting information coverage and the comprehensiveness of the resulting synthesis. To address these limitations, we propose LoG-Reranker, a local-to-global sentence-level graph reranking framework for scientific synthesis. LoG-Reranker performs role-aware local scoring to identify fine-grained, query-relevant sentences and then models their relations on a sentence graph across the candidate set to globally refine sentence rankings. Top-ranked sentences and their connected neighbors are organized into a structured input context for generator to produce more grounded and comprehensive synthesis.Extensive experiments on scientific synthesis and reranking benchmarks show that LoG-Reranker consistently outperforms competitive rerankers, yielding more reliable rankings and improving the quality of generated synthesis.

</details>


### [153] [Preference Shapes Relevance: Cross-component Hierarchical Semantic Alignment for Personalized Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30553)
*Gaoming Zhang,Angqing Jiang,Jianchun Song,Kena Qi,Dayao Chen,Wei Lin,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CHAP框架，通过分层语义对齐和残差级联生成机制，解决生成式检索中查询意图与静态商品表示不匹配及推理延迟高的问题，实现个性化高效检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索（GR）中，仅基于商品内容的语义ID（SIDs）存在语义鸿沟，无法对齐动态查询意图；且现有范式缺乏对用户行为序列的建模，并受限于束搜索自回归解码的高推理延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出跨组件分层语义对齐个性化生成检索（CHAP）框架：1. 设计分层语义对齐模块，将查询潜在空间与商品量化路径对齐，同步多粒度语义；2. 结合离散SIDs进行结构引导和连续表示进行细粒度语义细化，构建个性化GR框架以建模用户行为；3. 引入残差级联生成机制，将昂贵的多步Transformer解码器限制为单次推理，提升吞吐量。

Result: 在三个公开数据集、一个专有工业数据集以及在线A/B测试中进行了广泛实验，证明CHAP优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和实用价值。代码已开源。

Conclusion: CHAP通过分层语义对齐和高效的单次推理机制，成功解决了生成式检索中的语义对齐和用户行为建模难题，显著提升了检索性能和推理效率，具有显著的实践价值。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm by mapping queries directly to Semantic IDs (SIDs) with powerful representation capabilities for candidate items. However, existing SIDs derived solely from item content create a semantic gap, failing to align dynamic query intents with static item representations. Furthermore, current generative paradigms rarely model user behavior sequences and are always bottlenecked by the high inference latency of beam-search autoregressive decoding. To address these challenges, we propose $\textbf{C}$ross-component $\textbf{H}$ierarchical semantic $\textbf{A}$lignment for $\textbf{P}$ersonalized generative retrieval ($\textbf{CHAP}$), a novel personalized GR framework from a hierarchical perspective. First, we design a Hierarchical Semantic Alignment module to align query's latent space with item's quantization path and synchronize multi-granular semantics. Second, we construct a personalized GR framework that models user behavior by synergizing discrete SIDs for structural guidance and continuous representations for fine-grained semantic refinement. Notably, we introduce a Residual Cascading Generation mechanism to restrict the costly multi-step Transformer Decoder to a single-pass inference, boosting inference throughput while mitigating information loss. Extensive experiments on three public datasets, one proprietary industrial dataset, and online A/B tests demonstrate CHAP's superiority, validating the effectiveness and practical value of our approach. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/zzzgm/CHAP.

</details>


### [154] [Generative Retrieval for E-commerce: Jointly Learning Embedding and Codebook with Same Product Cluster](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30606)
*Songtao Fang,Zihao Xu,Shaowei Wei,Jin Zhang,Zhuojun Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种联合训练嵌入模型与码本的新方法，通过引入同簇产品监督信号，解决现有两阶段生成式检索中的误差累积和交互建模缺失问题，显著提升电商检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流的两阶段生成式检索方法存在两大缺陷：一是误差累积，即第一阶段嵌入模型的偏差无法在第二阶段被修正；二是码本学习仅依赖产品嵌入，缺乏对查询-产品和产品-产品交互的建模，导致同一簇产品ID分配不一致，降低检索精度。

Method: 提出一种联合训练嵌入模型和码本的端到端方法，并将同产品簇信息作为额外的监督信号融入训练过程，以增强模型对语义一致性和交互关系的理解。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著提升了电商场景下的检索性能，同时改善了嵌入表示质量和码本学习效果。

Conclusion: 联合训练机制结合簇级监督信号能有效克服传统两阶段方法的局限性，为生成式检索在电商中的应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: With the development of large language models (LLMs), generative retrieval is becoming increasingly important in e-commerce scenarios. Current mainstream approaches typically use a two-stage training strategy: first train a product embedding model, and then learn a codebook that maps embeddings to product IDs. This cascaded approach suffers from two major issues: (1) error accumulation-if the embedding model in the first stage produces biased representations, the codebook in the second stage cannot correct these errors, degrading final retrieval performance; and (2) codebook learning relies solely on product embeddings and lacks modeling of query-to-product and product-to-product interactions. As a result, products belonging to the same cluster may be assigned inconsistent IDs by the codebook, further hurting retrieval accuracy. To address these problems, we propose a novel method that jointly trains the embedding model and the codebook, and incorporates same product cluster information as an additional supervision signal. Experimental results demonstrate that our method significantly improves e-commerce retrieval performance while simultaneously enhancing both embedding and codebook learning.

</details>


### [155] [Learning from What You Retrieve: Online RL Fine-Tuning for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30753)
*Shaowei Wei,Chong Huang,Songtao Fang,Jin Zhang,Zhuojun Wang,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PAO（Positive-Advantage-Only）选择性强化学习方法，解决双编码器检索器在冻结索引下因标准策略梯度更新导致的嵌入几何退化问题，显著提升大规模电商检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电商检索中，双编码器优化对比相似度，而重排序器捕捉细粒度相关性，目标不匹配限制端到端检索质量；且工业场景需冻结文档索引，导致标准强化学习更新破坏预训练语义流形。

Method: 提出PAO方法，分析发现无差别惩罚负样本会破坏高维空间中的预训练语义结构；因此仅对具有正优势（positive advantages）的检索项应用梯度更新，将查询嵌入拉向高奖励区域，同时保持全局拓扑稳定性。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集和公共基准测试上，PAO显著优于标准强化学习和蒸馏基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过选择性应用基于正优势的梯度更新，PAO有效解决了冻结索引下的嵌入几何退化问题，提升了检索系统的整体性能与语义稳定性。

Abstract: In large-scale e-commerce retrieval, dual-encoder retrievers are op- timized for contrastive similarity, whereas downstream rerankers capture finer-grained relevance preferences; this objective mis- match limits end-to-end retrieval quality. Reinforcement Learning offers a way to use reward-model feedback for retriever adaptation, but we observe that standard policy-gradient updates can degrade embedding geometry, especially when the document index must remain frozen due to industrial constraints. To address this, we propose PAO (Positive-Advantage-Only), a selective RL optimization method. Our analysis reveals that in- discriminate penalization of negative samples (pushing away) in a frozen high-dimensional space disrupts pre-trained semantic man- ifolds. PAO selectively applies gradient updates only to retrieved items with positive advantages, effectively pulling query embed- dings toward high-reward regions while preserving global topo- logical stability. Experiments on both a massive industrial dataset and public benchmarks demonstrate that PAO significantly outper- forms standard RL and distillation baselines.

</details>


### [156] [MULTI3IR: A Benchmark for Multi-perspective Multi-domain Multi-modal Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30949)
*Seokwon Song,Sohyeon Kim,Gunhee Kim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出Multi³IR基准和SPIN方法，解决现有检索系统在面对开放查询时视角单一的问题，通过引入噪声向量引导嵌入方向，显著提升多模态、跨领域检索的视角覆盖率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息检索（IR）基准主要关注封闭型查询，且即使针对开放查询的基准也多局限于单一主题领域和模态，缺乏对开放查询所蕴含的多重视角的评估能力。

Method: 1. 构建Multi³IR基准：包含104.9K个Stack Exchange查询，每个查询均标注了捕捉隐含观点的描述性视角；2. 提出SPIN方法：一种参数和标签高效的方法，通过学习噪声向量来引导嵌入向多样化但具有语义意义的方向移动。

Result: 实验表明，现有的多模态检索器存在单视角偏差；而SPIN方法在Multi³IR上显著提升了视角覆盖率，并能很好地泛化到未见过的开放IR基准测试中。

Conclusion: Multi³IR基准和SPIN方法有效解决了开放查询检索中的视角覆盖问题，为评估和提升多视角信息检索性能提供了新的标准和工具。

Abstract: Information retrieval (IR) increasingly targets open-ended queries that admit diverse perspectives. Existing IR benchmarks, however, focus primarily on closed-ended queries, while even open-ended benchmarks largely consist of queries whose supporting documents span a single subject domain and modality. We introduce Multi$^3$IR, a benchmark that evaluates how well retrievers cover the multifaceted perspectives of open-ended queries across diverse domains and modalities. It comprises 104.9K Stack Exchange queries, each annotated with perspective descriptions that capture the query's implicit viewpoints. We further propose SPIN, a parameter- and label-efficient method that learns noise vectors to steer embeddings toward diverse yet meaningful semantic directions. Experiments show that existing multimodal retrievers suffer from single-perspective bias, while SPIN substantially improves perspective coverage on Multi$^3$IR and generalizes well to unseen open-ended IR benchmarks. The dataset and experimental code are available at https://github.com/seokwon99/Multi3IR.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [PUFFER: Incremental Fuzzy Deduplication for Continuously Evolving Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28622)
*Xiao Yang,Erik Edward Aldape,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PUFFER是一种用于大规模语言模型训练语料的增量模糊去重系统，通过不可变分段和分层压缩技术，实现了内存高效、快速且支持数据集范围撤回的去重功能，在十亿级文档处理中显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型训练语料库的不断增长和冗余发布，需要在万亿token规模下进行高效的增量去重。现有方法在面对累积历史数据时，往往需要重复重建整个语料库索引，导致内存占用过高（如900 GiB RAM限制）和处理速度慢，缺乏对数据集生命周期的精细控制能力。

Method: 提出PUFFER（Provenance-aware Updatable Fuzzy Filtering for Evolving Repositories），基于MinHash-LSH构建模糊去重流水线。核心设计包括：1. 将每个LSH band存储为不可变、数据集标签化、内存映射的排序分段，实现不依赖语料库大小的历史成员资格检查；2. 采用T-fanout分层压缩定期合并分段以控制筛选扇出，平衡查询成本与维护写入；3. 支持数据集范围的撤回操作，未压缩或受保护的数据集移除为常数时间，压缩后仅重构受影响的分段。

Result: 1. 维护成本：对于N个键和K个等大小发布，PUFFER累计维护成本为O(N log N log_T K)，远低于快照重建的Theta(KN)。2. 资源效率：处理10亿文档仅需128字节/文档内存，而传统MinHash-LSH需6.5 KB/文档并超出900 GiB RAM限制。3. 速度优势：在十小时、10亿文档测试中，PUFFER比LSHBloom快11倍，比Milvus-LSH快35倍。4. 实际部署：已在超过300亿文档上部署，并开源。

Conclusion: PUFFER通过创新的不可变分段存储和分层压缩机制，解决了大规模语料库增量去重的内存瓶颈和性能问题，提供了高效、可扩展且支持灵活生命周期管理的解决方案，具有显著的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Large language model training corpora grow through successive, often redundant releases, so each release must be deduplicated against both itself and the accumulated history. At trillion-token scale, this requires incremental ingestion, bounded resident memory, deterministic retry, and dataset-scoped lifecycle control without repeated corpus-wide rebuilding. We introduce PUFFER (Provenance-aware Updatable Fuzzy Filtering for Evolving Repositories), a MinHash-LSH fuzzy-deduplication pipeline built around two design choices. First, PUFFER stores each LSH band as immutable, dataset-tagged, memory-mapped sorted segments, enabling exact historical band-key membership checks without RAM proportional to corpus size. Second, T-fanout tiered compaction periodically merges segments to control screening fanout, trading lower query cost against additional index-maintenance writes while preserving membership decisions. Across N ingested keys and K equal-sized releases, PUFFER's cumulative maintenance cost is O(N log N log_T K), compared with Theta(KN) for repeated snapshot rebuilding. Dataset-tagged segments also support dataset-scoped withdrawal: removal is constant-time for uncompacted or protected datasets, while post-compaction withdrawal reconstructs only the affected merged segment, even if the original dataset is unavailable. In our implementation, PUFFER completed cumulative index-stage ingestion for one billion documents in about 1.75 hours in a single process, using 128 bytes per document for a 16-band index. A classical resident MinHash-LSH table required about 6.5 KB per document and exceeded a 900 GiB RAM cap. In a ten-hour comparison capped at one billion documents, PUFFER was 11x faster than LSHBloom and 35x faster than Milvus-LSH. PUFFER is deployed on more than 30 billion documents, and we release it as open-source software at https://github.com/Zyphra/puffer.

</details>


### [158] [Engaging the scientific community in high-quality biocuration: a report on the International Society for Biocuration workshop, 'Maximizing community curation for the benefit of all'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28835)
*Daniela Raciti,Susan L. M. Coort,Christian Grove,Jade Hotchkiss,Matt Jeffryes,Nancy T. Li,Zhiyong Lu,Bastien Molcrette,Sushma Naithani,Maria Victoria Nugnes,Jolene Ramsey,Rene Ranzinger,Leonore Reiser,Karen E. Ross,Garrett Stevens,Courtney Thaxton,Sabrina Toro,Valerie Wood,Karen Yook,Kimberly Van Auken*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文总结了在生物知识库专业策展支持减少的背景下，通过研讨会探讨“社区策展”的现状、挑战及标准化最佳实践，旨在推动其成为维护高质量生物资源的标准做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可策展的生物医学知识量不断增加，但对生物知识库的专业支持却在下降。为了解决内容更新和维护的问题，知识库需要探索除传统专家策展外的其他途径，特别是推广“社区策展”模式，以应对当前缺乏统一标准和广泛采用的困境。

Method: 在第18届国际生物策展会议上举办了一场名为“最大化社区策展以造福所有人”的研讨会。会议邀请了来自18个不同资源（包括模式生物和专科知识库、期刊及文献资源）的代表，展示其工作成果，交流策略、成功经验、失败案例及持续面临的挑战。

Result: 对社区策展的当前技术水平进行了全面评估，深入讨论了如何将社区策展转化为维持及时、高质量生物资源的标准实践，并提出了促进更广泛采用共同最佳实践和有效工具的建议交付物。

Conclusion: 社区策展是解决生物知识库维护危机的重要潜在方案。通过分享经验和制定通用最佳实践，可以克服现有方法的局限性，使社区策展成为确保科学家获得研究所需关键信息的标准流程。

Abstract: Biological knowledgebases traditionally rely on expert, professional curation of the research literature to maintain up-to-date collections of data organized in machine-readable form. However, despite the increasing amount of curatable biomedical knowledge, support for knowledgebases is declining, leaving these resources no alternative but to explore additional ways of updating and maintaining content. One way in which knowledgebases have addressed this problem is by engaging researchers to help curate their published papers, a process generally known as 'community curation'. As helpful as community curation can be, though, it is not universally adopted and, for groups that do have it, there is a wide range of approaches. To learn about existing community curation pipelines and explore possibilities for working towards a common approach, we organized a workshop, Maximizing Community Curation for the Benefit of All, at the 18th International Biocuration Conference, hosted by the Stowers Institute for Medical Research. Our aim was to examine the different strategies that groups use, share successes, failures, and ongoing challenges, and produce suggested deliverables for broader adoption of common best practices and tools for effective community curation. Representatives from 18 different resources, ranging from model organism and specialty knowledgebases to journals and literature resources, presented their work. The result was a comprehensive assessment of the state-of-the-art for community curation and an in-depth discussion on how community curation can become standard practice for maintaining timely, highquality biological resources that will continue to provide scientists with the essential information they need for their research.

</details>


### [159] [RENSA: Rich Environment Metadata to Navigate Shared and Distributed Endpoints for Automated Federated SPARQL Query Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28963)
*Victor Eiti Yamamoto,Takeda Hideaki,Yamamoto Yasunori*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出RENSA框架，通过扩展SPARQL Builder Metadata实现无需运行时通信的高效联邦SPARQL查询源选择与约束推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦环境中缺乏对去中心化数据集的详细结构知识，标准元数据（如VoID）无法捕捉复杂的互连和权威分布，导致引擎依赖高开销的运行时ASK查询进行源选择。

Method: 构建基于扩展SBM的RENSA框架，整合类与权威信息，将主体/客体使用映射到特定谓词，生成压缩的数据配置文件以支持精确源选择和语义约束推断。

Result: 在LargeRDFBench基准测试中，RENSA实现了与最先进方法相当的源选择结果，消除了ASK查询开销；生成的配置文件通常仅占原始三元组的1%以下，并能在异构端点间识别数据源。

Conclusion: RENSA通过高效的元数据配置文件优化了联邦SPARQL查询性能，提供了人类可读的结构洞察，促进了半自动查询生成。

Abstract: The number of knowledge graph databases has increased significantly with the proliferation of knowledge graph technologies. Knowledge graphs enable the dynamic integration of distributed data through federated SPARQL queries. However, constructing efficient queries in a federated environment is challenging due to the lack of detailed structural knowledge across decentralized datasets. While standards like VoID provide basic metadata, they often fail to capture the complex interlinks and authority distributions necessary for optimization. Consequently, current engines frequently rely on runtime ASK queries for source selection, increasing communication overhead. We propose RENSA, a federated SPARQL query generation framework that leverages an extension of SPARQL Builder Metadata (SBM). By integrating class and authority information, mapping subject and object usage to specific predicates, RENSA enables precise source selection and semantic constraint inference for query variables without runtime communication. The generated profiles represent less than 1\% of the original dataset triples in most cases, ensuring storage efficiency. Evaluation on the LargeRDFBench benchmark (13 datasets with >1B triples, 32 queries) shows that RENSA achieves source selection results comparable to state-of-the-art methods while eliminating ASK query overhead. Furthermore, we demonstrate that RENSA infers class and authority constraints for query variables, enabling the identification of data sources even across heterogeneous endpoints. These profiles additionally offer human-readable structural insights for semi-automated query generation.

</details>


### [160] [Diachronic Hypergraphs for Orchestrated Multi-Agent Multimodal Memory Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29678)
*Yichao Feng,Ran Zhang,Haoran Luo,Zhenghong Lin,Carl Yang,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出MAGE，一种基于超图的多模态数据库作为多智能体系统（MAS）的记忆引擎，通过保留高阶协作事件来解决现有记忆结构扁平化导致的信息丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量图和图记忆将复杂结构扁平化为嵌入或二元痕迹，模糊了涉及智能体、工具、文档、错误和证据的事件，限制了知识共享、追踪、复用、修订和编排能力。

Method: 构建异构时间超图来存储智能体、消息、工具、错误、程序、文档、实体、决策和证据；支持驱动决策的更新、角色感知检索、验证、生命周期管理及预算受限的上下文打包。

Result: 实验表明，MAGE在各种记忆基线上表现优于对比方法，能有效扩展智能体和编排者的知识边界而不需修改模型。

Conclusion: MAGE通过以超图形式保留高阶协作事件作为可复用记忆，显著提升了多智能体系统的记忆效能和知识传递能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems solve tasks through collaboration, tool use, multimodal reasoning, and orchestration, but each agent operates within a knowledge boundary defined by its observations, context, and resources. Memory must preserve and transfer evidence, role specific context, decisions, procedures, and experience across interactions, not only outcomes. Vector and graph memories flatten these structures into embeddings or dyadic traces, obscuring events involving agents, tools, documents, errors, and evidence. This limits knowledge sharing, tracing, reuse, revision, and orchestration. We present MAGE, a hypergraph based multimodal database designed as a memory engine for MAS. MAGE stores agents, messages, tools, errors, procedures, documents, entities, decisions, and evidence in a heterogeneous temporal hypergraph, preserving high order collaborative events as reusable memory. It supports decision driven updates, role aware retrieval, validation, lifecycle management, and budget bounded context packing. By delivering knowledge to agents and orchestrators, MAGE expands their knowledge boundaries without modifying the models. Experiments show MAGE outperforms on various memory baselines.

</details>


### [161] [Demystifying and Improving Lazy Promotion in Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29993)
*Qinghan Chen,Muhammad Haekal Muhyidin Al-Araby,Ziyue Qiu,Zhuofan Chen,Rashmi Vinayak,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种懒晋升（Lazy Promotion）缓存淘汰策略，发现 Delay-LRU 和 FIFO-reinsertion 在提升晋升效率方面表现最佳。针对传统 LRU 存在的过度晋升问题，提出了两种新增强算法 D-FR 和 AGE，可将晋升次数减少 20-60% 且保持较低的缺失率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代数据系统中缓存淘汰算法因对象晋升计算开销大、锁竞争高而导致的扩展性受限问题。

Method: 利用真实生产环境追踪数据，基准测试五种懒晋升策略（Probabilistic-LRU, Batch-LRU, Delay-LRU, FIFO-reinsertion, Random-LRU），评估指标包括缺失率、扩展性、晋升次数及晋升效率；同时分析这些技术在 ARC 和 2Q 等高级算法中的应用，并提出两种新增强方案：延迟 FIFO 重插入（D-FR）和年龄引导淘汰（AGE）。

Result: Delay-LRU 和 FIFO-reinsertion 显著提升了晋升效率；Batch-LRU 和 Probabilistic-LRU 难以在不大幅增加缺失率的情况下减少晋升。研究发现多数缓存晋升是不必要的。提出的 D-FR 和 AGE 能将晋升次数降低 20-60%，并实现相同或更低的缺失率。

Conclusion: 懒晋升技术能有效缓解缓存系统的性能瓶颈，特别是 Delay-LRU 和 FIFO-reinsertion 策略具有较高价值。通过引入 D-FR 和 AGE 等新机制，可以在保证缓存命中率的同时大幅优化晋升开销，为缓存系统设计提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Cache eviction algorithms play a critical role in the performance of modern data systems, yet their scalability is often limited by the high computational overhead associated with object promotions. Lazy Promotion techniques have emerged as relaxations of traditional Least-Recently-Used (LRU) methods, designed to alleviate lock contention and increase throughput. This work uses production traces from real-world systems to benchmark five Lazy Promotion strategies: Probabilistic-LRU, Batch-LRU, Delay-LRU, FIFO-reinsertion, and Random-LRU. We evaluate these techniques across miss ratio, scalability, promotion count, and a novel metric called promotion efficiency, which measures the number of hits per promotion.
  Our results reveal that Delay-LRU and FIFO-reinsertion significantly improve promotion efficiency, whereas Batch-LRU and Probabilistic-LRU struggle to reduce promotions without significantly increasing miss ratio. We further explore the impact of lazy promotion in advanced algorithms such as ARC and 2Q and make a similar observation. Moreover, we uncover substantial optimization potential, showing that most cache promotions are unnecessary when equipped with oracle knowledge. To further reduce promotions in LRU, we propose two novel enhancements-Delayed FIFO-reinsertion (D-FR) and Age-Guided Eviction (AGE)-that reduce promotions by 20-60% while achieving a similar or lower miss ratio.

</details>


### [162] [ELASTIC: Trajectory-Based Synchronization of Event and Tracking Data in Soccer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30227)
*Hyunsung Kim,Hoyoung Choi,Kunhee Lee,Sangwoo Seo,Tom Boomstra,Jinsung Yoon,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出ELASTIC框架，仅利用球员和球轨迹推断足球事件的时间戳，解决现有方法依赖标注位置且难以检测接球的问题。通过插入虚拟终止事件并使用改进的Needleman-Wunsch算法对齐序列，在Sportec Open DFL数据集上显著优于现有方法，提升了下游分析任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代足球分析中，事件数据与追踪数据往往缺乏良好对齐。人工标注的事件时间戳常偏离真实动作时刻，扭曲时空上下文；现有同步方法依赖噪声较大的标注位置，且无法有效检测“接球”这一关键动作，导致球员控球权转换信息缺失。

Method: 提出ELASTIC（Event-Location-AgnoSTIC synchronizer）框架：1) 仅基于球员和球的轨迹推断事件的起止时间戳，不依赖标注的位置信息；2) 为恢复接球检测，在连续事件间插入虚拟终止事件，联合检测每个事件的结束与开始；3) 提取物理上合理的稀疏候选帧作为球触球点；4) 使用扩展的Needleman-Wunsch算法将插入终止事件的事件序列与候选帧序列进行对齐。

Result: 构建了包含真值时间戳标注的公开基准数据集（基于Sportec Open DFL Dataset）。实验表明，ELASTIC在该基准上显著优于现有同步方法。下游任务评估显示，更精确的时间同步带来了可量化的足球分析性能提升。

Conclusion: ELASTIC通过摆脱对标注位置的依赖并利用轨迹数据推断事件时间，有效解决了事件与追踪数据对齐难题，特别是改善了接球时刻的检测。该方法提高了数据质量，进而增强了后续足球分析模型的效果，相关代码和基准已开源。

Abstract: Combining event and tracking data is fundamental to modern soccer analytics, yet the two sources are rarely well aligned: event timestamps recorded by human annotators often miss the true moment of the action, distorting the spatiotemporal context that downstream models rely on. Existing synchronization methods depend on noisy human-annotated event locations and fail to detect ball receptions, obscuring when each player gains ball possession. To address these limitations, we propose ELASTIC (Event-Location-AgnoSTIC synchronizer), a framework that infers the start and end timestamps of events solely from player and ball trajectories, without relying on annotated event locations. To recover ball receptions, ELASTIC enriches the event sequence by inserting virtual termination events between consecutive events, so that the end of each event is detected jointly with its start. It then extracts a sparse set of candidate frames where ball touches are physically plausible, and aligns the termination-inserted event sequence with the candidate-frame sequence using an extended Needleman-Wunsch algorithm. For reproducible evaluation, we construct a publicly available benchmark by annotating ground-truth timestamps on the Sportec Open DFL Dataset, on which ELASTIC substantially outperforms existing methods. Through downstream task evaluation, we further show that improved synchronization translates into measurable gains in soccer analytics. The source code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/hyunsungkim-ds/elastic.git.

</details>


### [163] [Detecting DBMS Bugs by Constructing Equivalent Representations of Intermediate Query Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30385)
*Xiaoxu Niu,Gong Chen,Jinfu Chen,Xiaoyuan Xie*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出ERIQ方法，通过比较视图(views)、公共表表达式(CTEs)和临时表(Temps)对同一中间查询结果的不同等价表示的执行结果一致性，来检测数据库管理系统(DBMS)的逻辑缺陷。在MySQL、MariaDB等四种主流开源数据库中发现了64个缺陷，其中54个为未知逻辑错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DBMS逻辑缺陷检测方法未探索等效中间查询结果表示（如VIEWs、CTEs、TEMPTs）之间的一致性检查。尽管这些机制应产生一致的结果，但实际中常出现不一致，暗示存在潜在的DBMS逻辑漏洞。

Method: 提出ERIQ测试方法：1) 构造SQL变体，分别使用VIEW、CTE或TEMPT表示相同的中间查询结果；2) 执行这些变体；3) 比较返回结果以检测不一致性。在MySQL、MariaDB、Percona和OceanBase四个开源DBMS上进行评估。

Result: ERIQ共检测到64个缺陷，其中63个经开发者确认，2个已修复。在已确认的缺陷中，54个是此前未知的独特逻辑缺陷，1个为文档问题。

Conclusion: 基于等效中间查询结果表示一致性检查的方法能有效发现DBMS中的深层逻辑缺陷，填补了现有检测技术的空白，并揭示了多个未被发现的严重问题。

Abstract: Database Management Systems (DBMSs) support multiple SQL mechanisms for representing intermediate query results, including VIEWs, Common Table Expressions (CTEs), and Temporary Tables (TEMPTs). When these mechanisms are used to represent the same intermediate query result, the corresponding queries are expected to produce consistent results. However, we observe that such queries can return inconsistent results, indicating potential DBMS logic bugs. Existing approaches for detecting DBMS logic bugs have never explored result consistency across such equivalent representations. In this paper, we propose ERIQ, a novel testing approach for detecting DBMS logic bugs from the perspective of checking result consistency across Equivalent Representations of Intermediate Query Results. ERIQ constructs SQL variants using a VIEW, a CTE, or a TEMPT to represent the same intermediate query result, executes these variants, and compares their returned results. We evaluated ERIQ on four widely used open-source DBMSs: MySQL, MariaDB, Percona, and OceanBase. In total, ERIQ detected 64 bugs, 63 of which were confirmed by developers, and two have been fixed. Among the confirmed bugs, 54 were unique and previously unknown logic bugs, and one was a documentation issue.

</details>


### [164] [Strengthening LargeRDFBench for Interoperable Federated SPARQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30465)
*Bryan-Elliott Tam,Muhammad Saleem,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文修复了LargeRDFBench基准测试中的数据质量问题，通过清洗管道生成符合RDF标准的序列化数据，并将预期结果重编码为标准JSON格式，从而扩展了基准测试的适用范围并提高了可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LargeRDFBench基准测试中，部分数据转储违反RDF规范，导致仅能使用宽松解析器的引擎才能运行；同时，预期结果以非标准格式分发且存在不一致，影响了评估的可复现性和公平性。

Method: 1. 识别、分类并修复数据质量问题，建立可复现的数据清洗管道，生成符合标准的序列化文件。2. 将预期结果重编码为W3C SPARQL 1.1查询结果JSON格式，并修正差异。3. 使用独立实现端到端复现预期结果，发现并解决参考数据中的损坏和不一致问题。4. 对FedX算法中的ASK和COUNT基于源选择进行初步比较。

Result: 所有数据集现在均可在严格的RDF解析器下正确解析；预期结果变为机器可验证的标准格式；通过独立复现发现了原始发布参考数据中的损坏及若干需进一步研究的不一致之处；增强了社区资源的价值，使更多符合标准的引擎能够被公平比较。

Conclusion: 通过将大型基准测试工件与RDF标准对齐，扩大了其适用引擎范围并提升了可复现性。同时提出了关于如何在自动源选择下实现联邦查询结果可复现性的开放性问题。

Abstract: LargeRDFBench is one of the most comprehensive benchmarks for evaluating federated SPARQL query engines, combining real, interlinked datasets with a rich query suite that has made it a reference point for the community. Evaluations of federated engines are published by comparing engine results against the benchmark's expected results, so those expected results must themselves be reproducible. Moreover, several of its data dumps violate the RDF specifications, so only engines that parse RDF leniently can host them, and its expected results are distributed in an ad hoc format. We identify, categorize and repair these data-quality issues with a reproducible cleaning pipeline, producing standards-conformant serializations of every affected dataset. Furthermore, we re-encode the benchmark's expected results in the W3C SPARQL 1.1 Query Results JSON Format and correct their discrepancies. Every dataset now parses under strict RDF parsers, and the expected results are machine-verifiable through a standard format, extending the benchmark's reach to the full range of conformant engines while staying faithful to the original data. Reproducing the expected results end-to-end with an independent implementation uncovers corruption in the published reference, and discrepancies between our results and the original ones, some not trivial to resolve, others open questions. We further perform a preliminary comparison, not previously explored, of ASK- and COUNT-based source selection in the FedX algorithm. This work strengthens an already valuable community resource by aligning its artifacts with the RDF standards, broadening the set of engines that can be fairly and reproducibly compared. We also raise the question of how the results of federated queries under automatic source selection can be made reproducible.

</details>


### [165] [UBASE: An AI Search Engine for Trillion-Scale Vector Data Management at ByteDance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30607)
*Yao Tian,Yuncheng Lu,Liyao Xiong,Yuming Xu,Hao Zhang,Weichen Zhao,Xi Zhao,Bo Kuang,Dongyu Wang,Jiehui Li,Yakun Li,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: UBASE是字节跳动基于搜索基础设施演进的统一AI搜索系统，通过引入SymRaBitQ量化技术和混合存储引擎，解决了大规模向量检索中的内存瓶颈和构建成本问题，实现了吞吐量提升3倍、索引内存降低80%及运营成本降低86%的性能突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工作负载需求的增长，UBASE从文本搜索引擎演变为支持向量检索、 lexical匹配和谓词过滤的统一AI搜索系统。然而，在索引近一万亿高维向量的超大规模部署中，面临两大核心瓶颈：一是持续摄入下的内存密集型图索引构建困难；二是将向量索引完全保留在内存中带来的高昂成本。

Method: 1. 提出基于SymRaBitQ的感知量化向量内核：这是一种新的对称量化方案，具有严格的理论保证，允许直接在量化空间中准确高效地构建索引，无需保留全精度向量的副本。
2. 提供混合存储引擎：支持内存驻留、混合模式和SSD驻留部署，并通过细粒度的记录级缓存机制，在操作控制下以内存换取延迟，优化性能与成本的平衡。

Result: 在大规模基准测试中，相较于先前系统，UBASE将吞吐量提高了高达3倍，索引内存减少了80%，运营成本降低了86%。同时，该系统能够在生产环境中支持万亿级向量规模、写密集或延迟敏感的工作负载。

Conclusion: UBASE通过创新的量化技术和灵活的存储架构，有效解决了AI时代大规模向量检索中的内存和成本瓶颈，证明了其在处理超大规模、高并发AI搜索场景下的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Since 2016, UBASE has been the foundation of ByteDance's search infrastructure, scaling to more than 7,000 clusters and 300 PB of indexed data. Driven by the demands of AI workloads, UBASE has evolved from a text search engine into a unified AI search system supporting vector retrieval, lexical matching, and predicate filtering. Its largest deployment indexes nearly one trillion high-dimensional vectors. This scale exposes two central bottlenecks in AI-era retrieval: memory-intensive graph-index construction under sustained ingestion, and the prohibitive cost of keeping vector indexes entirely in memory. UBASE addresses these bottlenecks with two techniques. First, it introduces a quantization-aware vector kernel based on SymRaBitQ, a new symmetric quantization scheme with tight theoretical guarantees that allows index construction to run directly in the quantized space accurately and efficiently without retaining a copy of full-precision vectors. Second, it provides a hybrid storage engine that supports memory-resident, hybrid, and SSD-resident deployments, with fine-grained record-level caching to trade memory for latency under operational control. On large-scale benchmarks, UBASE improves throughput by up to 3x, reduces indexing memory by 80%, and lowers operating cost by 86% compared with prior systems, while supporting trillion-vector scale, write-heavy or latency-sensitive workloads in production.

</details>


### [166] [GRBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark Evaluation for Graph-relational Data Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31027)
*Zepeng Liu,Xinxin Huang,Xuanming Liu,Sheng Wang,Zhiyong Peng*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出GRBench基准测试，用于评估图关系数据管理系统。通过从SciSciNet-v2提取真实模式与数据，构建统一的查询系列和语义等价查询，解决现有基准在图关系负载覆盖不足及性能分析维度单一的问题，旨在指导未来系统设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据密集型应用需要同时管理结构化记录和图数据，催生了图关系数据管理需求。然而，现有的评估方法滞后：传统基准孤立评估关系或图模型，多模型基准对图关系工作负载覆盖有限，且现有图关系工作负载仅支持功能验证和端到端延迟测量，无法深入揭示存储、算子和优化设计对性能的具体影响。

Method: 1. 从真实世界数据集SciSciNet-v2构建链接的图关系模式，并通过一致性保留的子集提取生成可扩展实例；2. 组织专用的查询系列，以受控方式评估查询处理及系统组件；3. 提供语义等价的原生查询形式，并通过统一的多维方法论评估代表性系统架构。

Result: 开发了GRBench基准测试工具，实现了对图关系数据管理的全面评估。通过对代表性系统架构的评估，分析了设计权衡（trade-offs），并识别出开放挑战。

Conclusion: GRBench填补了图关系数据管理评估领域的空白，通过多维度的系统化评估，为未来数据库系统的设计和优化提供了指导依据。

Abstract: Modern data-intensive applications increasingly require database systems to manage structured records and graph data. This demand gives rise to graph-relational data management, spanning storage, query processing, and optimization across relational and graph data. In response, relational database extensions, multi-model databases, and dedicated graph-relational systems have emerged with diverse architectures. However, evaluation methodologies have not kept pace. Existing relational and graph benchmarks assess the two models largely in isolation, while multi-model benchmarks provide limited coverage of graph-relational workloads. Available graph-relational workloads mainly support functional validation and end-to-end latency measurement, revealing little about how storage, operator, and optimization designs affect performance. To evaluate system capabilities in graph-relational data management, we present GRBench. First, GRBench constructs a linked graph-relational schema from the real-world SciSciNet-v2 dataset and derives scalable instances through consistency-preserving subset extraction. Second, it organizes purpose-built query series for controlled evaluation of query processing and system components. Third, GRBench provides semantically equivalent native query formulations and evaluates representative system architectures through a unified, multidimensional methodology. Based on this evaluation, we analyze design trade-offs and identify open challenges to guide future system design and optimization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [DS-Lighting: Making Agent Harnesses Explicit for Data-Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28590)
*Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DS-Lighting，一个统一的数据科学自动化Harness工具包，通过显式分解为数据、工作流、执行和评估四层，提升LLM代理在数据科学任务中的可复现性、可比性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据科学LLM代理的Harness（任务表示、状态管理、输出约束及评估反馈）通常是隐式的，导致结果难以在不同异构任务间进行复现、比较和归因。

Method: 设计DS-Lighting框架，将Harness显式分解为四个可复用层：数据、工作流、执行和评估；将多样化代理表示为支持预定义管道和自适应搜索的可执行算子程序；整合多个开源基准至MLE-Bench风格的任务格式，提供共享任务接口、沙箱运行时和指标协议。

Result: 实验表明，显式的Harness设计显著提高了数据科学工作流的复现性、可比性和可靠性，并减少了端到端流程中可避免的系统级故障。

Conclusion: DS-Lighting通过标准化的Harness设计解决了现有代理系统的碎片化问题，为数据科学自动化提供了更稳健、可比较的基础设施。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise for automating data-science workflows, yet their end-to-end performance depends critically on the agent harness that represents tasks, manages execution state, constrains output artifacts, and provides evaluation feedback. Existing data-science agents often leave this harness implicit, making results difficult to reproduce, compare, and attribute across heterogeneous tasks. We introduce DS-Lighting, a unified harness toolkit that makes harness design explicit for data-science automation. DS-Lighting decomposes the harness into four reusable layers: data, workflow, execution, and evaluation, and represents diverse agents as executable operator programs that support both predefined pipelines and adaptive search. We further integrate multiple open-source data-science benchmarks into an MLE-Bench-style task format, enabling controlled comparison under a shared task interface, sandboxed runtime, and metric protocol. Experiments across agents, harnesses, models, and ablations show that explicit harness design improves reproducibility, comparability, and reliability, while reducing avoidable system-level failures in end-to-end data-science workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/dslighting

</details>


### [168] [Expert-validated STEM QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28591)
*Kihwan Han,Saurabh Patil,Chinmayee Shukla,Abhinav Sharma,Marko Pavlovic,Anshuman Lall,Mahesh Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“专家验证的STEM问答”数据集，旨在解决现有AI评估数据集中的性能饱和、分布偏差及格式不匹配等问题。该数据集由241位领域专家创建，包含398个经过严格审核的物理、化学、生物和数学问题。实验表明，前沿AI模型在该基准上表现较低（<25%），且基于私有版本数据的后训练能显著提升开源模型性能，证明了其作为高质量训练和评估资源的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在科学领域的突破，现有的STEM评估数据集面临诸多局限：1) 模型性能趋于饱和，缺乏区分度；2) 分类学分布不均；3) 多项选择题格式不符合科学家实际使用AI的场景；4) 由于竞赛式收集和时间限制导致答案和推理过程存在不准确之处。因此，亟需构建一个由人类专家创建的高质量、无偏见且符合现实场景的新数据集。

Method: 研究团队联合241位领域专家构建了包含398条记录的“Expert-validated STEM QA”数据集。具体方法包括：1) 设计具有平衡分布的分类体系；2) 通过质量驱动的激励机制筛选题目贡献者；3) 进行多轮审查与修订，确保由领域专家达成共识验证；4) 采用可验证的问答格式而非传统多项选择。此外，还利用包含2,000条记录的私有版本数据集对开源模型进行了后训练实验。

Result: 1) 前沿AI模型在该新数据集上的基准测试表现较低（正确率<25%），显示出良好的评估区分度；2) 在HLE-verified数据集的STEM子集上，经过私有数据集后训练的开源模型相比基线模型性能提升了15%（p=0.045）；3) 部分数据集已开源供社区使用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个高质量、专家验证的STEM数据集，有效填补了现有评估资源的空白。实验结果证实了该数据集不仅能提供有意义的模型评估基准，还具有辅助模型训练的巨大潜力，为AI在科学领域的应用提供了新的数据基础设施。

Abstract: Recent advancements in AI are helping scientists achieve breakthroughs in fields such as mathematics, medicine, and materials sciences. New evaluation datasets for AI models contribute to such advancement in AI. In the STEM domain, frontier models have consumed most of the available online data, creating the need for human-created datasets that codify the knowledge of leading experts in the domain. There are several STEM datasets available for the research community in this field. However, there are some gaps in these datasets, leaving room for improvement. Examples of gaps include (1) saturation in model performance on these datasets, leaving no head-room for meaningful evaluations, (2) skewed taxonomy distributions, (3) multiple choice question format that is misaligned with how scientists use AI in the real world, and (4) inaccurate answers and rationales partially led by a contest-based data collection and a time-bound review process. In this study, we present 'Expert-validated STEM QA', a high-quality, expert-validated STEM dataset (N=398) in Physics, Chemistry, Biology, and Mathematics, created by 241 domain experts. We (1) carefully designed a taxonomy with balanced distribution, (2) vetted question contributors with quality-driven incentive, (3) conducted multiple rounds of reviews with revisions validated by domain experts based on consensus, and (4) created the dataset in verifiable question and answer format. Our study demonstrated low performance ($<25\%$) of frontier AI models on the dataset as a benchmark. Post-training on a separate, private version of the dataset (N=2,000) increased performance of the open source model by $15\%$ relative to the baseline model (p=0.045) on the STEM subset of HLE-verified dataset, indicating potential utility of the dataset for model training. We have open-sourced a portion of our dataset for the AI research community.

</details>


### [169] [A collective capability boundary in frontier large language models on guideline-conformant and case-specific oncology decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28592)
*Zhang Sheng,Jinming Li,Wangyang Chen,Zhiwei Bao,Yu YoSean Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究构建了肿瘤学决策边界基准（ODBB），评估前沿大语言模型在临床决策中的表现。结果显示，42.1%的测试项无一模型答对，失败主要集中在指南路径选择前的“元判断”盲点及承诺缺失。单纯增加数据或追求决断力无法解决此问题，临床部署的关键瓶颈在于单一模型的局限性，需采用能识别能力边界并路由至医生的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）在医学知识考试中得分很高，但真实世界的肿瘤学涉及不确定性下的指南路径选择、升级判断和承诺。现有基准主要衡量事实回忆，无法揭示前沿LLMs是否存在结合模型也无法修复的决策路径盲点。

Method: 构建ODBB基准，包含来自NCCN指南和结直肠癌病例的2,005个肿瘤学决策点。评估了2025年6月至2026年4月间发布的9个前沿LLMs（4个闭源，5个开源）。使用完全确定性的评分器（零LLM推理）将输出分类为14种失败类型，并由两名肿瘤学家独立验证。

Result: 作为整体超级模型评估时，42.1%的项目无一模型回答正确（其中NCCN项目35.7%，结直肠癌病例66.4%）。失败集中在指南路径选择前的推理之前，显示临床元判断存在一致盲点。追求决断力的模型（GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview）不安全承诺率是谨慎模型的3-5倍，且得分未更高。3-9%的项目中，模型知道正确步骤但未做出承诺，属于决策失败而非知识失败。

Conclusion: 模型质量不再是临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈；主要约束在于假设任何单一模型都可以作为临床决策的唯一基础。进步需要能够检测模型何时达到其能力边界并将决策路由给临床医生的架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve high scores on medical knowledge examinations, yet real-world oncology is not a knowledge test--it is a sequence of guideline-pathway choices, escalation judgments, and commitments under uncertainty. Existing benchmarks largely measure factual recall, leaving open whether frontier LLMs share decision-path blind spots that combining models cannot fix. We built the Oncology Decision Boundary Benchmark (ODBB)--2,005 oncology decision points across NCCN guidelines and colorectal cancer cases--and evaluated nine frontier LLMs (four closed-source, five open-weight families) released between June 2025 and April 2026. A fully deterministic scorer (zero LLM inference) classified outputs into 14 failure types, independently validated by two oncologists (Cohen's weighted $κ$ = 0.939 and 0.790) on a 225-item stratified sample. Treating the nine as a pooled super-model, 42.1% (Wilson 95% CI 40.0--44.3%) of all items--35.7% of the 1,586 NCCN items and 66.4% of the 419 colorectal-cancer cases--were answered correctly by none, with failures concentrated in choosing between guideline pathways before reasoning within any: a consistent blind spot in clinical meta-judgment that likely requires architectural intervention rather than more training data. Two models tuned for decisiveness (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) made unsafe commitments three to five times more often than the seven cautious models without scoring higher. In 3--9% of items, models stated the correct next clinical step yet did not commit to it--failures of decision, not knowledge. Model quality is no longer the primary bottleneck for clinical LLM deployment; the binding constraint is the assumption that any single model can be the sole basis for a clinical decision. Progress requires architectures that detect when a model reaches its competence boundary and route the decision to a clinician.

</details>


### [170] [Statutory AI: Aligning Large Language Models With Legal Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28593)
*Cindy Delage,Stéphane Canu,Marc Décombas,Jonathan Foureur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Statutory AI框架，利用法律文本作为宪法基础，通过两阶段思维链提示实现AI输出的自主审查与修正，显著降低有害内容并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法存在局限：Constitutional AI依赖人工监督，而GfH等规范过于宽泛模糊，缺乏可操作性的治理指导。

Method: 构建Statutory AI混合方法，从法律语料库中提取具体主题的人类 authored 原则作为宪法框架。采用两阶段Chain-of-Thought提示：第一阶段对用户提示进行分类；第二阶段结合相关法律条文分析并修正输出。

Result: 在1,000个红队测试提示和5个刑罚主题（歧视、泄露机密、暴力、欺诈、虐待弱势群体）实验中，Statutory AI使有害内容减少52-59个百分点，比标准Constitutional AI高出约10个百分点，同时计算时间减少超过50%。

Conclusion: Statutory AI能有效克服现有方法的局限性，提供更精确、高效且自动化的AI合规与价值引导方案。

Abstract: With the increasing development of AI regulatory frameworks, ensuring that artificial intelligence systems, particularly generative models, operate in accordance with legal and ethical standards has become a critical priority. Existing proposals for AI alignment and value-guided behavior, however, face some limitations. Approaches such as Constitutional AI depend on human supervision, while broad normative frameworks like the Good-for-Humanity (GfH) principle may be overly general and ambiguous to provide actionable governance guidance. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid approach called Statutory AI that employs pre-existing human-authored principles drawn from specific themes within a legal corpus. Specifically, Statutory AI uses legal texts as a constitutional framework, enabling AI systems to autonomously critique and revise their outputs according to established norms. It operates in two stages, both using Chain-of-Thought prompting. The first stage classifies the user prompt into one of the identified themes, while the second stage analyzes it in conjunction with relevant articles selected from the legal corpus of that theme. To illustrate the potential of our approach, we conducted an experiment involving 1,000 red-teaming prompts and five penal themes: discrimination, disclosure of confidential information, violence, fraud, and abuse of vulnerable persons. Statutory AI reduced harmful content by 52 to 59 percentage points across tested models, approximately 10 percentage points higher than standard Constitutional AI, while cutting computation time by over 50%.

</details>


### [171] [From Question-First to Analyst-First: Domain-Expert Skills and Verified Knowledge Compilation for Proactive Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28594)
*Harmohit Singh,Rahul Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种“分析师优先”的生产级分析系统架构，通过可插拔的领域专家技能模块和离线知识编译循环，解决非专家用户在陌生数据模式下面临的查询难题，实现从被动问答到主动推荐报告与问题的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式分析系统假设用户已具备清晰的问题，导致非专家用户在面对不熟悉的企业数据模式时面临空白查询框的困境；商业主动工具仅能检测统计异常，而学术推荐器依赖历史查询日志，无法适应新数据集。

Method: 1. **可插拔领域专家“技能”抽象**：基于文件夹结构的无数据库主题包（包含清单、提示词片段、关键词路由引用等），通过确定性模式匹配自动选择并嵌入智能体管道各阶段，形成可扩展的领域专家市场。2. **离线知识编译循环**：利用DuckDB探测Parquet文件（零生产负载），通过批判者门控的收敛机制和自我修复重试生成持久的模式知识，并通过值重叠验证连接，确保指标SQL重新执行以进行验证。

Result: 构建了能够自动生成专家报告和建议问题的系统，报告中的数字可衍生出相关问题，点击即可启动经过验证的深度探索，形成“报告展示数据->数据启发问题->点击深入探索”的主动闭环；提供了形式化模型及单租户实例证据。

Conclusion: 该贡献主要在于系统架构及其防御性（鲁棒性/可靠性），而非用户研究或基准测试表现；通过架构创新有效填补了非专家用户与新数据模式之间的交互鸿沟。

Abstract: Conversational analytics systems assume the user already has a well-formed question, leaving a non-expert facing a blank query box on an unfamiliar enterprise schema. Commercial 'proactive' tools narrow this gap only by detecting statistical anomalies over analyst-curated metric layers, and academic next-question recommenders depend on query logs that a fresh dataset lacks. We describe a production analytics system that inverts the interaction model from question-first to analyst-first through two coupled architectural ideas. First, a pluggable domain-expert 'skill' abstraction: a folder-based, database-free subject-matter pack (a manifest, per-stage prompt facets, keyword-routed references, report templates, and optional compute) auto-selected per (client, dataset) by deterministic schema matching and spliced as a cross-cutting concern into every stage of an agentic pipeline, the schema explorer, and the report engines, degrading to a strict no-op when absent. Because a skill is a self-contained folder resolved deterministically, the catalogue is open-ended: an extensible marketplace of domain experts. Second, an offline knowledge-compilation loop: an agent probes the dataset's parquet via DuckDB (zero load on production), runs critic-gated per-table convergence with self-healing retries, and data-validates joins by value overlap, producing durable schema knowledge that drives standing expert reports whose every published metric is re-verified by re-executing its evidence SQL, plus suggested questions that mirror the report agenda. These close a proactive loop: reports surface numbers, the numbers seed questions, and a click launches a verified deep dive, all before the query box is used. We give a formal model and report illustrative single-tenant evidence. We make no user-study or benchmark claims; the contribution is the architecture and its defensibility.

</details>


### [172] [The Signal in the Noise: An Auditable Reliability Layer for Biomedical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28595)
*Moustafa Yehia Hassan,Sharon Wong,Woh Kai Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种保守、可审计的拼写纠错模块，用于修复大规模生物医学语料库中由PDF解析产生的噪声，在不确定时不修改文本以遵循“不伤害”原则，显著恢复下游分类器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型生物医学语料库常因自动PDF解析产生OCR伪影、分词错误等噪声，导致词汇证据受损并降低下游分类器性能，而现有NLP管道通常假设输入文本是干净的。

Method: 构建一个基于确定性架构的安全预处理模块：结合有界编辑距离候选生成、语料库n-gram评分及生物医学安全门控；采用内在校验（2,104个案例）和外在评估（CORD-19三分类任务，10,000个示例）进行验证。

Result: 内在评估显示合成错误修复召回率为94.61%，对负面控制零有害编辑；外在评估中恢复约80.45%的噪声导致的宏观F1下降，将宏观F1从0.7654提升至0.7717，同时保持接近干净数据的表现（0.7721）。

Conclusion: 该确定性系统通过保守纠错有效提升了噪声数据的下游任务性能，未来研究可探索整合神经信号与UMLS词表的灰盒架构，同时保持可审计性。

Abstract: Biomedical NLP pipelines routinely presuppose clean input text, yet large-scale corpora assembled through automated PDF parsing harbour pervasive OCR-like artifacts, token splits and merges, hyphenation remnants, and character-level corruption, that systematically erode lexical evidence and degrade downstream classifiers. We introduce a conservative, fully auditable spell-correction reliability layer conceived as a safety-oriented preprocessing module rather than a maximal-accuracy corrector: under conditions of uncertainty, the system abstains from editing, in accordance with a medical do-no-harm philosophy. The deterministic architecture couples bounded edit-distance candidate generation with corpus-derived n-gram scoring and a suite of biomedical safety gates that protect domain-critical terminology. We evaluate the layer both intrinsically, on a manually curated benchmark of 2,104 token-level cases, and extrinsically, on a tri-class CORD-19 topic classifier (Prevention, Treatment, Epidemiology) spanning 10,000 examples under a principled four-run protocol (Clean, Noisy, Restored, Safety). Intrinsically, the layer attains 94.61% error-fix recall on synthetic errors with zero harmful edits on negative controls. Downstream, it recovers approximately 80.45% of the noise-induced macro-F1 degradation, elevating macro-F1 from 0.7654 (Noisy) to 0.7717 (Restored) while preserving near-clean performance (Safety: 0.7721). A supplementary case study on 103 real-world OCR-extracted abstracts classified with BioBERT confirms that transformer encoders appeared relatively robust to mild noise, motivating a future grey-box architecture that integrates bounded neural signals and UMLS lexicons without compromising auditability. The system is fully deterministic, artifact-driven, and designed with deployment and auditability in mind.

</details>


### [173] [Paper Pilot: A Human-in-the-Loop Expert System for Evidence-Traceable Scientific Manuscript Generation in Applied Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28596)
*Nidhi Jha,Siddharth Chaudhary,Ajinkya Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Paper Pilot，一种基于CARE方法论的人机协作系统，通过审批门控和证据锁定机制解决LLM生成科学稿件时的治理与可追溯性问题。实验表明，该框架能有效消除伪造引用并识别证据缺口，将LLM辅助写作从完全自主转变为受控的人类决策支持过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM系统在科学工作流中虽能实现自主发现和稿件生成，但缺乏必要的治理机制，导致思想、方法、结果和主张在AI辅助流程中传播时，缺少强制性的人类审批和针对具体产出的可追溯性（traceability）。

Method: 构建Paper Pilot系统，适应Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE) 方法论。核心机制包括：8个从构思到主张的审批门控、明确的不通过标准、主张分类（文献驱动vs实物驱动）、审计日志、建议性LLM审查以及证据锁定的版本控制。公开系统提示词以支持多平台部署。

Result: 在引文接地层的基准测试中，无门控的LLM草稿生成器在压力下伪造了高达25%的引用且未标记证据缺口；而在Paper Pilot的证据锁定规则下，同一模型产生了零伪造引用，并将植入的证据缺口显式标记为占位符。初步结果显示该方法在结果接地、修订和对抗鲁棒性方面表现一致。

Conclusion: Paper Pilot成功将LLM辅助写作定位为受控的人机决策支持过程，而非全自动的作者身份流水线，有效解决了科学稿件生成中的可信度和治理问题。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly embedded in scientific workflows for literature analysis, drafting, and review. Existing systems advance autonomous discovery and manuscript generation, but do not resolve the governance problem that arises when ideas, methods, results, and claims propagate through AI-assisted workflows without mandatory human approval or artifact-level traceability. This paper proposes Paper Pilot, a human-in-the-loop expert system for evidence-traceable scientific manuscript generation in applied sciences. It adapts the Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE) methodology to manuscript development through manuscript-owner approval gates, explicit no-pass criteria, claim classification, audit logging, advisory LLM review, and evidence-locked revision control. The framework defines eight approval gates across the idea-to-claim pipeline and distinguishes literature-grounded from artifact-grounded claims, requiring reported numbers and interpretations to remain traceable to approved evidence; its system prompt is openly released for deployment in ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, or institutional LLM environments. As a first empirical validation, we evaluate the citation-grounding layer with a controlled, mechanically scored benchmark (two commercial LLMs, real arXiv papers, no LLM judge): under coverage pressure ungated drafters fabricated up to 25% of their citations and never flagged an evidence gap, whereas the same models under Paper Pilot's evidence-locked rules produced zero fabricated citations and surfaced the planted gaps as explicit placeholders. Preliminary results for result grounding, revision, and adversarial robustness point the same way; full evaluation is left to future work. Paper Pilot positions LLM-assisted writing as a controlled human-AI decision-support process rather than a fully autonomous authorship pipeline.

</details>


### [174] [The Race between Agentic AI Capabilities and Data Quality Control in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28597)
*Sourav Panda,Hillmer Chona,Rupak Kumar Das,Shreyash Kale,Shikha Soneji,Jonathan Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于大语言模型的多模态智能体（Agentic AI）在在线调查中通过注意力检查的能力，揭示了现有防御机制的脆弱性，并提出了DOM元数据混淆作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具备工具增强能力的多模态AI智能体的快速兴起，传统的在线调查注意力检查机制面临严峻挑战，其作为响应质量守护者的稳健性受到质疑，亟需探究AI智能体能否绕过这些 safeguards。

Method: 构建了一个受控的调查沙盒环境，评估单智能体架构（支持多模态输入和基于工具的网页交互）完成网络调查及通过标准注意力检查的表现；从攻击视角分析结构漏洞（如暴露的DOM元数据和可预测的选项编码），并从防御视角实施DOM元数据混淆以移除文本问题中的语义线索；同时评估多个开源语言和视觉模型的能力与编排效果。

Result: 研究发现，智能体能够通过结构化解析仅利用暴露的DOM元数据和可预测的选项编码来轻松解决注意力检查，证明现有机制存在结构性脆弱；实施的DOM元数据混淆策略能有效移除语义线索，提升防御效果。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前在线调查安全机制在面对高级AI智能体时的局限性，并为实证研究者与AI研究人员提供了平衡数据质量保障与AI能力发展的建议视角。

Abstract: Online surveys are a foundational data collection instrument in a variety of fields, with attention checks serving as critical guardians of response quality. However, the rapid emergence of agentic AI (goal directed systems powered by a large language model (LLM) brain and/or a multimodal processing unit with tool-augmented capabilities) raises new questions about the robustness of these safeguards. We investigate how well agentic AI architectures can complete web-based surveys and pass standard attention checks. We evaluate a single-agent architecture capable of multimodal input processing and tool-based web interaction on a controlled survey sandbox. We analyze the problem from two perspectives. From an attack perspective, we demonstrate how structural vulnerabilities such as exposed DOM metadata and predictable option encoding allow agents to resolve attention checks through structured parsing only. From a defense perspective, we implement a mitigation strategy of DOM metadata obfuscation to remove semantic cues in text-based questions. We evaluate multiple open-source language and multimodal models to study capability and orchestration effectiveness. Based on our evaluations, we offer perspectives on how to simultaneously meet the needs of empiricists and agentic AI researchers.

</details>


### [175] [CDPR: Counterfactual Advantage-based Credit Assignment for Cost-Aware Sequential Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28599)
*Qi Peng,Yi Cai,Changmeng Zheng,Xin Wu,Jiayuan Xie,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CDPR（反事实诊断过程奖励），通过强化学习将临床诊断建模为成本感知的序列决策过程，在无需专家标签和批评家的情况下，平衡诊断准确性与检查成本/数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗语言模型通常将诊断视为一次性分类任务，忽略了检查的价值与成本之间的权衡，且难以解决长轨迹中的信用分配问题。

Method: 1. 将诊断建模为成本感知的序列决策过程，使用强化学习训练策略。
2. 提出CDPR机制：利用动作分布的不确定性识别犹豫状态；通过短滚动评估所选动作相对于替代方案的优势，效用函数综合考量正确性、检查数量、成本和不可行请求。
3. 使用滚动缓存复用批内轨迹以降低计算成本，并将CDPR集成到GRPO算法中。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV（域内）以及ClinicalBench和私有医院数据集（域外）三个基准测试上，CDPR显著提高了诊断准确率，同时明显减少了检查的数量和总成本。

Conclusion: CDPR有效解决了诊断过程中的信用分配难题，实现了在不依赖专家标注的情况下，优化医疗诊断的准确性和经济效率。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is a step-by-step, cost-aware process: a physician orders examinations one at a time, observes the results, and updates the diagnosis before reaching a final conclusion. Most medical language models instead treat diagnosis as a one-pass classification task and ignore the trade-off between a test's value and its cost. We model diagnosis as a cost-aware sequential decision process and train the policy with reinforcement learning. The main difficulty is credit assignment: the only reliable signal comes once at the end of a long trajectory, so it scores a wasteful workup the same as an efficient one. We propose CDPR (Counterfactual Diagnostic Process Reward), which needs no expert labels and no learned critic.
